# SpecDx Phase P1b (act→read diagnosis with observability) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Implement the SpecDx v2 delta: the act→read inference with observability, paraphrase sensitivity and T-guided scenarios; check it offline on the P1 cache (GO/NO-GO); then the neutral harness, the capability screen, gate v2 with observability-aware scoring, parallel workers and portability; finally run the screen and gate v2 on real models.

**Architecture:**
- **Inference first (Tasks 2–6).** Stage 1 (`infer/act.py`) turns every run into act indicators per interpreter step and fits a Rasch model. Its observability Oₛ gates which items carry evidence. Stage 2 (`infer/latent.py`) is the v1 set-valued model, fed with acted behaviours only, with θ anchored by a Beta(7, 3) prior. Diagnosis (`infer/scores.py`) types only candidates that observable items read, adds τ_H, and reports ambiguity from S1 = P(|Aₛ| ≥ 2) or S2, a permutation test of class against Tell variant (`infer/sensitivity.py`).
- **Offline replay (Task 7)** runs this inference on the P1 cache with no GPU. It is a pre-registered GO/NO-GO for everything after it.
- **Population and evaluation (Tasks 8–13).** A harness at the executor's chat boundary parses tool calls written as text and nudges drafting steps that wrote nothing. T-guided scenarios come from thresholds in T. Mutation operators declare truth targets, so the gate can score only mutants the population can reach. A capability screen chooses the population.
- **Infrastructure (Tasks 14–17).** Parallel workers with one database each, multiple Ollama hosts, `doctor`, `cache-info`, profiles, a run guide and the pre-registration.
- **Task 18** runs it all on real models.

**Tech stack:** Python 3.13, PostgreSQL 16 (Docker, port 55434), psycopg 3, httpx, numpy, pytest, ruff, Ollama.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-specdx-v2-design.md` (binding). It is a delta on `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-specdx-design.md`, whose §11 clarifications stay binding. Before starting, read v2 in full, then v1 §2.2, §3, §4.3–§4.8 and §11. The P1 execution ledger (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-06-specdx-p1-core-ledger.md`) records the rulings behind the current code.

**Pre-check (2026-10-11):** every code block of Tasks 2–17 was materialised task by task in a scratch copy of the repository at `8e76f04` and run.
- After each task, the whole suite passed against the SpecDx PostgreSQL on 55434, and `ruff check .` was clean. Each task gives the test count it reached.
- The replay script of Task 7 ran read-only against the P1 cache, which it left byte-identical.
  - It maps all 4,200 population runs, in 3.5 s.
  - Under the criterion first drafted ("θ(qwen3:14b) ≥ θ(llama3.1:8b)"), its verdict was NO-GO on all 21 specifications: θ(llama3.1:8b) = 0.961 against θ(qwen3:14b) = 0.743. κ ranks qwen3 > qwen2.5 > llama.
  - Only the 24 items of the three non-drafting steps (check_availability, P2, P6) are observable on the P1 population. llama "acts" there by completing the step without writing. 61 of the 91 P1 base findings become Unobservable.
  - The verdict does not depend on two planning resolutions: with P1's plug-in ε, θ is 0.918 against 0.515; if every interpreter step must write to act, llama never acts and keeps the prior mode 0.75 against 0.507.
  - **Author's ruling (2026-10-12).**
    - On the P1 population only one family (qwen) writes. Proposition 2′ needs at least two acting families, so a θ ordering there carries no information about v2.
    - Task 7 is therefore a **mechanism check**: κ, no θ collapse, and Unobservable share.
    - The θ-ordering condition moves to the screened population, after the Task 18 smoke run and before the gate.
    - The change and these pre-check numbers are disclosed in `experiments/p1b-preregistration.md`.
- On this machine `nvidia-smi` fails with `Driver/library version mismatch` (kernel module 595.84, user library 595.99), so Ollama may not reach the GPU until a reboot. `specdx doctor` reports it (Task 15), and Task 18 stops on it.
- The pre-check's database tests created `tonomyx_w0`–`tonomyx_w2` on 55434. Task 14 creates the same databases on demand.

## Global Constraints

- `vendor/` is never edited. `vendor/tonomyx/` is byte-identical to TonomyX commit `34c1d2ce70583b2de54573d28ba4f49e0af4cc7f`.
- The plan executes in the git worktree on branch `worktree-specdx-p1b`, with its own `.venv` (Task 1).
- **The P1 run cache.**
  - Every command that needs the P1 run cache or the mutant packs is prefixed with `SPECDX_WORK=/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work`. Never `export` it, so tests never see it.
  - That directory is the main checkout's git-ignored run cache: about 6,300 runs and 4 GPU-days. It is never modified except by appending runs through the Runner, and never deleted.
  - Before Task 11, no command that builds mutants (`specdx mutants`, `specdx gate`, `specdx run --variant <mutant>`) may run with that `SPECDX_WORK`. From Task 11 on, `all_mutants` never rewrites an existing pack.
- **Database.**
  - SpecDx's database is `postgresql://tonomyx:tonomyx@localhost:55434/tonomyx`. Worker databases `tonomyx_w0`, `tonomyx_w1`, … live in the same server (Task 14).
  - Ports 55432 and 55433 belong to other projects; never touch them.
  - Every DB test is marked `@pytest.mark.db` and uses port 55434 only.
  - The `specdx-postgres` container already runs. Do not run `docker compose up` from the worktree: its fixed container name would conflict.
- **One process per database.** The advisory lock refuses a second process. Never run pytest while a screen, smoke run or gate is running.
- **Infrastructure faults.** An Ollama outage, a wedged Ollama, a database fault or a held lock raises `InfrastructureError` (or the psycopg/httpx error). It is never cached and it stops the gate (v1 §11 item 10).
- **Run cache keys.** `RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION = 3` from Task 8 on, because the harness changes what interpreters see. Runs cached under version 2 are never reused for new runs.
- **No test calls a real Ollama.** Tests use `httpx.MockTransport` or scripted executors. Real models run only in Task 18.
- **Gate scoring.** Gate comparisons use exact-arithmetic F1 = 2tp / (2tp + fp + fn) (`matching.exact_f1`). The differential and the ordering of base locations of v1 §11 item 13 are unchanged.
- **Code.**
  - Python 3.13, typed, `ruff check .` clean with line length 100 (pyproject.toml).
  - Comments are sparse and explain why.
  - Identifiers, comments, tests and documents are in English. Vietnamese appears only in prompt strings, Tell text and `docs/RUNNING.md`'s quick-start.
- **Pre-registered values (spec v2 §3.10), copied exactly:**
  - harness nudges per step: 2;
  - screen: 9 runs, act rate ≥ 0.6, valid-call rate ≥ 0.8;
  - population: ≤ 2 per family, ≤ 4 in all, ≥ 2 families;
  - Rasch L2 penalty λ = 1.0;
  - observability ω = 0.3, m_min = 3;
  - θ prior Beta(7, 3);
  - competent model for S2: θₘ ≥ 0.5;
  - hidden threshold τ_H = 0.5;
  - S2: 2000 permutations, at least 6 behaviours, BH q = 0.1;
  - T-boundary scenarios per specification: ≤ 12;
  - scorability k = 3;
  - budgets (population / probe / localise): 200 / 100 / 80.
- **Unchanged v1 defaults:**
  - temperature 0.7, Tell variants 0–2, seeds 1–3;
  - K_max = 6, P(|A| = 1) = 0.9, τ = 0.05;
  - the development split: `dev_per_type` 5, `rng_seed` 20261006.
- **Gate v2 rule (v2 §4.2):**
  - contradiction and hidden: SpecDx typed F1 > max(B2, B3) untyped F1;
  - ambiguity and weak: SpecDx typed F1 ≥ 0.4;
  - a type with fewer than 3 scorable mutants is a loss;
  - the gate passes on at least 3 wins of 4.
- **Never tune on the test split.** The test split is not run in P1b.
- **Commands.** Run tests with `.venv/bin/python -m pytest` (pyproject's `addopts = "-q"` prints `N passed`; do not add another `-q`, which hides the count). Lint with `.venv/bin/ruff check .`.

## Review Focus

Five conditions the spec implies but the main tests do not exercise. Each has a test in the task that owns the code:

1. **Resuming over the P1 run cache.** Its lines have no `harness` or `spec` field. They must load, and no v2 run may reuse a P1 run. *(Task 8: `test_harness_counts_and_the_spec_round_trip_and_old_runs_load_without_them`, and the `RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION` case of `test_any_interpreter_visible_setting_changes_the_context_digest`.)*
2. **A mutant pack in `work/mutants/` that differs from what the code builds** (edited by hand, or built by other code). It must be refused with an error, never overwritten or deleted. An identical pack must be left untouched. *(Task 11: `test_a_pack_that_differs_from_what_the_code_builds_is_refused`, `test_a_second_build_leaves_every_existing_pack_untouched`.)*
3. **Two Ollama hosts holding different builds of one model tag.** Every run must stop with an error before anything is cached. *(Task 15: `test_several_hosts_must_list_a_model_with_the_same_digest`.)*
4. **An infrastructure failure inside a parallel batch.** It must be raised, and nothing collected after it may be cached. *(Task 14: `test_an_infrastructure_failure_stops_the_batch_and_is_never_cached`.)*
5. **A population that acts nowhere** (every model fails or writes nothing). The loop must still finish and report every item unobservable. It types nothing, and θ stays at the prior mode. *(Task 10: `test_a_population_that_never_acts_types_nothing_and_still_reports`.)*

## Planning-time resolutions (spec v2 left these open)

Each is implemented in the named task and recorded in `experiments/p1b-preregistration.md` (Task 17).

1. **"Done" (§3.1)** means a completed step: `done` or `needs_gate` (`clauses.DONE_STATUSES`, v1 §11 item 7). Otherwise a correct `draft_purchase_order`, which always opens APR-0155, would never act. *(Task 2)*
2. **"The step's work order drafts tables" (§2.1, §3.1)** reads as the step's token draft scope: `draft_tables` in `de_scope.csv`. Personas (P2, P6) and DE-OP-01 draft nothing, so they act by completing. *(Tasks 2, 8)*
3. **"Locked fields" of a class (§3.3)** are the v1 §2.2 ≡_S projection (`ColumnModel.project`): locked values and the presence of blank columns, which W1 truths need. Only the step status is removed. *(Task 3)*
4. **ε, the error distribution.**
   - Spec v2 keeps the generation formula and does not say how ε is estimated. P1 plugged in the smoothed distribution of all outputs (P1 ledger, Task 11 ruling).
   - Under that plug-in θ is unidentified: on an item where everyone agrees, ε explains the agreement as well as competence does, the likelihood is maximal near θ → 0, and EM drifts there. This is the P1 inversion's mechanism.
   - With it, the §6 inversion-regression test cannot pass: acting models' θ falls below the prior mean with no non-acting model present.
   - So stage 2 re-estimates ε in each EM iteration from the outputs not explained as faithful, as v1 §3.2 defines it ("the error distribution"). *(Task 4)*
5. **Family effect with a closed-form Beta MAP.** θ follows the spec's formula exactly. The family effect is still estimated and reported (a family's mean logit θ minus the population's), but it does not feed θ. A listed model with no acted behaviour gets θ = 0.75, the prior mode. *(Task 4)*
6. **A run's hitting-set weight.** It is the mean, over the items where the run acted, of P([b] ∈ Aₛ) × θₘ; a run that acted nowhere weighs 0. When no acted view supports Hidden(c), the score is 0, below τ_H. A weak deviant is compared with the MAP set's most probable class; v1 already requires the whole MAP set to be clean. The ambiguity score is max(S1, 1 − p), with 1 − p = 0 when S2 did not test the item. *(Task 6)*
7. **GO criterion (author's ruling, 2026-10-12), a mechanism check.**
   - (a) On the base and on every one of the 20 development mutants, stage 1 gives llama3.1:8b, the model that never writes, a κ strictly below every other model's.
   - (b) On the same 21 specifications, every model with at least one acted behaviour has θ ≥ 0.5, and at least one model acted.
   - (c) At least 50% of the 91 P1 base findings become Unobservable.
   - The θ ordering is checked on the screened population in Task 18 instead. *(Tasks 7, 18)*
8. **Text tool calls (§2.1).**
   - A call names the tool with `name` or `function` and gives the arguments with `arguments` or `parameters`, in any pairing. In the P1 cache, llama3.1:8b wrote `{"name": …, "parameters": …}` in 7,226 claims.
   - Every top-level JSON object in the reply is a candidate, so fences, `<tool_call>` tags and surrounding prose all work. Several calls run in order.
   - The valid-call rate counts native calls, valid or not, and parsed calls, which are valid by construction. *(Task 8)*
9. **T literals (§3.7).** English keywords also match their inflections (exceeds, exceeded). Decimals, identifiers (`APR-0102`) and dates are not numbers. v ± 1 stays ≥ 0, and a discount stays ≤ 100. T-boundary scenarios skip canon points. *(Task 9)*
10. **Truth targets (§4.1).**
    - An H2 target is any event with the demanded event's reference. The demanded event is one T never motivates, so no admissible run writes it, and the literal rule would make every H2 mutant unscorable.
    - The A sites' governed steps are declared in `mutate/operators.py`.
    - A behaviour is counted once, whatever the scenario. *(Task 11)*
11. **Parallel dispatch (§5.1).**
    - All attempts of one DirectedProbe candidate are charged, then dispatched as one batch. The witness is the first in submission order, so a found witness no longer saves the remaining attempts. This holds for one worker too, which keeps the report byte-identical.
    - `--workers 1` uses the main database. With 2 or more workers, runs use `<name>_w0`, …, and symbolic repair keeps the main database. *(Task 14)*
12. **Mutant packs.** They are built once and never overwritten, so the `SPECDX_WORK` rule above holds. A `Behaviour` also records its specification's name, for `cache-info`. *(Tasks 11, 8)*

### Task 2: The act indicator and the Rasch fit (stage 1)

Spec v2 §3.1–§3.2. A behaviour b acts at an item s (a scenario step of kind `de` or `persona`) when the step completed and, if its actor drafts tables, its slice ends with a row the starting state did not hold. A Rasch model P(a = 1) = σ(κₘ − δₛ) is fitted by penalised maximum likelihood, with mean δ fixed at 0. An item is observable when Oₛ ≥ 0.3 and at least 3 behaviours acted there.

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/infer/act.py`
- Test: `tests/infer/test_act.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `specdx.spec.clauses.DONE_STATUSES` (`{"done", "needs_gate"}`), `specdx.spec.columns.Cells` and `Projection`, `specdx.orchestrate.work_orders.WorkStep`, `tonomyx.canon.pack.CanonPack` (`pack.tables["de_scope"]` rows with `ma_de`, `draft_tables`).
- Produces (used by Tasks 3, 6, 7, 10, 13, 17):
  - `INTERPRETED: frozenset[str]` (= `{"de", "persona"}`), `RASCH_L2 = 1.0`, `OMEGA = 0.3`, `M_MIN = 3`;
  - `SliceRows = frozenset[tuple[str, str, Cells]]`;
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) Act(item: str, run_key: str, model: str, acted: bool)`;
  - `drafting_steps(pack: CanonPack, steps: Sequence[WorkStep]) -> frozenset[str]`;
  - `slice_rows(projection: Projection) -> SliceRows`;
  - `is_act(status: str, drafts: bool, now: SliceRows, before: SliceRows) -> bool`;
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) Rasch(kappa: dict[str, float], delta: dict[str, float], observability: dict[str, float], acted: dict[str, int])`, with `.observable(item, *, omega=OMEGA, m_min=M_MIN) -> bool` and `.observable_items(*, omega=OMEGA, m_min=M_MIN) -> frozenset[str]`;
  - `fit_rasch(acts: Iterable[Act], *, lam: float = RASCH_L2, tol: float = 1e-6, max_iter: int = 100) -> Rasch`;
  - `act_rate(acts: Iterable[Act]) -> Mapping[str, float]` (per model).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/infer/test_act.py`:
```python
import math
import random

from specdx.infer.act import Act, act_rate, drafting_steps, fit_rasch, is_act, slice_rows
from specdx.orchestrate.work_orders import load_work_orders

STEPS = load_work_orders()


def test_the_steps_that_draft_are_the_de_steps_with_draft_tables(pack) -> None:
    assert drafting_steps(pack, STEPS) == {
        "DE-SM-02:draft_quote", "DE-FN-04:draft_purchase_order",
        "DE-OP-02:record_goods_receipt", "DE-FN-01:draft_sales_invoice",
        "DE-FN-02:reconcile", "DE-FN-03:draft_payment",
    }


def test_acting_needs_a_completed_step_and_a_new_row_when_the_step_drafts() -> None:
    before = slice_rows((("so", ()),))
    now = slice_rows((("so", (("SO-0031", (("trang_thai", "Nháp"),)),)),))
    assert is_act("done", True, now, before)
    assert is_act("needs_gate", True, now, before)
    assert not is_act("done", True, before, before)  # completed, but wrote nothing
    assert not is_act("error", True, now, before)
    assert is_act("done", False, before, before)  # a step that drafts nothing only completes
    assert not is_act("", False, before, before)


def _synthetic(kappa, delta, trials, seed):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    acts = []
    for m, k in kappa.items():
        for s, d in delta.items():
            p = 1 / (1 + math.exp(-(k - d)))
            acts += [Act(s, f"{m}|{s}|{t}", m, rng.random() < p) for t in range(trials)]
    return acts


def test_rasch_recovers_planted_capability_and_difficulty() -> None:
    kappa = {"weak": -1.0, "mid": 0.0, "strong": 1.5}
    raw = [-1.5 + 3.0 * j / 29 for j in range(30)]
    delta = {f"s{j:02d}": d - sum(raw) / len(raw) for j, d in enumerate(raw)}
    fit = fit_rasch(_synthetic(kappa, delta, 40, 0))
    assert abs(sum(fit.delta.values())) < 1e-9
    assert all(abs(fit.kappa[m] - k) < 0.3 for m, k in kappa.items())
    pairs = [(fit.delta[s], d) for s, d in delta.items()]
    mx = sum(a for a, _ in pairs) / len(pairs)
    my = sum(b for _, b in pairs) / len(pairs)
    cov = sum((a - mx) * (b - my) for a, b in pairs)
    sx = math.sqrt(sum((a - mx) ** 2 for a, _ in pairs))
    sy = math.sqrt(sum((b - my) ** 2 for _, b in pairs))
    assert cov / (sx * sy) > 0.95


def test_an_item_is_observable_only_with_enough_probability_and_enough_acts() -> None:
    acts = []
    for m in ("a", "b", "c"):
        for t in range(6):
            acts.append(Act("easy", f"{m}|easy|{t}", m, True))
            acts.append(Act("rare", f"{m}|rare|{t}", m, m == "a" and t < 2))
            acts.append(Act("never", f"{m}|never|{t}", m, False))
    fit = fit_rasch(acts)
    assert fit.observable("easy")
    assert fit.acted["rare"] == 2 and not fit.observable("rare")  # fewer than m_min = 3
    assert fit.observability["never"] < 0.3 and not fit.observable("never")
    assert fit.observable_items() == {"easy"}
    assert fit.kappa["a"] > fit.kappa["b"]


def test_a_model_that_never_acts_gets_the_lowest_capability() -> None:
    acts = [Act(f"s{j}", f"{m}|{j}|{t}", m, m != "silent" and (t + j) % 4 != 0)
            for m in ("x", "y", "silent") for j in range(8) for t in range(4)]
    fit = fit_rasch(acts)
    assert fit.kappa["silent"] < min(fit.kappa["x"], fit.kappa["y"])
    assert act_rate(acts)["silent"] == 0.0 and act_rate(acts)["x"] == 0.75


def test_no_acts_give_an_empty_fit() -> None:
    fit = fit_rasch([])
    assert fit.kappa == {} and fit.observable_items() == frozenset()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`specdx/infer/act.py`:
```python
"""Stage 1 of the act→read model (spec v2 §3.1–§3.2): whether an interpreter acted.

An item is a (scenario, step) pair of an interpreter step (kind de or persona). An
interpreter acted there when the step completed and, if the step's actor may draft
tables, the end state holds a row of the step's slice that the starting state did not
hold. A Rasch model P(act) = σ(κ_m − δ_s) separates a model's capability to act (κ) from
an item's difficulty (δ). Observability O_s is the population's mean probability of
acting at s; only observable items carry evidence about the specification.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from tonomyx.canon.pack import CanonPack

from specdx.orchestrate.work_orders import WorkStep
from specdx.spec.clauses import DONE_STATUSES
from specdx.spec.columns import Cells, Projection

INTERPRETED: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"de", "persona"})
RASCH_L2 = 1.0  # spec v2 §3.10
OMEGA = 0.3
M_MIN = 3

SliceRows = frozenset[tuple[str, str, Cells]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Act:
    """Whether one behaviour (a run) acted at one item."""

    item: str
    run_key: str
    model: str
    acted: bool


def drafting_steps(pack: CanonPack, steps: Sequence[WorkStep]) -> frozenset[str]:
    """The interpreter steps whose actor may draft tables (draft_tables in de_scope).
    Personas decide gates with the human tools and draft nothing."""
    drafts = {r["ma_de"]: [t for t in r["draft_tables"].split("|") if t]
              for r in pack.tables.get("de_scope", [])}
    return frozenset(s.id for s in steps if s.kind in INTERPRETED and drafts.get(s.actor))


def slice_rows(projection: Projection) -> SliceRows:
    """The rows of a step's slice, each as (table, key, compared cells)."""
    return frozenset((table, key, cells) for table, rows in projection for key, cells in rows)


def is_act(status: str, drafts: bool, now: SliceRows, before: SliceRows) -> bool:
    """Spec v2 §3.1. A completed step is done or needs_gate (v1 §11 item 7)."""
    if status not in DONE_STATUSES:
        return False
    return not drafts or bool(now - before)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rasch:
    """kappa per model, delta per item (mean 0), O_s per item, acted behaviours per item."""

    kappa: dict[str, float]
    delta: dict[str, float]
    observability: dict[str, float]
    acted: dict[str, int]

    def observable(self, item: str, *, omega: float = OMEGA, m_min: int = M_MIN) -> bool:
        return self.observability.get(item, 0.0) >= omega and self.acted.get(item, 0) >= m_min

    def observable_items(self, *, omega: float = OMEGA, m_min: int = M_MIN) -> frozenset[str]:
        return frozenset(i for i in self.delta if self.observable(i, omega=omega, m_min=m_min))


def _objective(x: np.ndarray, n: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray, m: int, lam: float) -> float:
    eta = x[:m, None] - x[None, m:]
    return float((y * eta - n * np.logaddexp(0.0, eta)).sum() - lam / 2 * (x @ x))


def fit_rasch(
    acts: Iterable[Act], *, lam: float = RASCH_L2, tol: float = 1e-6, max_iter: int = 100
) -> Rasch:
    """Penalised maximum likelihood (L2 penalty lam on κ and δ) with mean δ fixed at 0:
    constrained Newton steps, halved while they would lower the objective."""
    data = list(acts)
    if not data:
        return Rasch({}, {}, {}, {})
    models = sorted({a.model for a in data})
    items = sorted({a.item for a in data})
    mi = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(models)}
    si = {name: j for j, name in enumerate(items)}
    m, s = len(models), len(items)
    n = np.zeros((m, s))
    y = np.zeros((m, s))
    for a in data:
        n[mi[a.model], si[a.item]] += 1
        y[mi[a.model], si[a.item]] += 1 if a.acted else 0
    x = np.zeros(m + s)
    # The constraint row: the δ entries sum to zero, and every step keeps that sum.
    kkt = np.zeros((m + s + 1, m + s + 1))
    kkt[m:m + s, -1] = 1.0
    kkt[-1, m:m + s] = 1.0
    for _ in range(max_iter):
        p = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-(x[:m, None] - x[None, m:])))
        r = y - n * p
        w = n * p * (1 - p)
        grad = np.concatenate([r.sum(axis=1), -r.sum(axis=0)]) - lam * x
        hess = np.zeros((m + s, m + s))
        hess[:m, :m] = -np.diag(w.sum(axis=1))
        hess[m:, m:] = -np.diag(w.sum(axis=0))
        hess[:m, m:] = w
        hess[m:, :m] = w.T
        hess -= lam * np.eye(m + s)
        kkt[:m + s, :m + s] = hess
        step = np.linalg.solve(kkt, np.concatenate([-grad, [0.0]]))[:m + s]
        before = _objective(x, n, y, m, lam)
        t = 1.0
        while t > 1e-6 and _objective(x + t * step, n, y, m, lam) < before - 1e-12:
            t /= 2
        x = x + t * step
        if float(np.max(np.abs(t * step))) < tol:
            break
    kappa = {name: float(x[i]) for name, i in mi.items()}
    delta = {name: float(x[m + j]) for name, j in si.items()}
    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-(x[:m, None] - x[None, m:])))
    observability = {name: float(prob[:, j].mean()) for name, j in si.items()}
    acted = {name: int(y[:, j].sum()) for name, j in si.items()}
    return Rasch(kappa, delta, observability, acted)


def act_rate(acts: Iterable[Act]) -> Mapping[str, float]:
    """The fraction of a model's (behaviour, item) pairs where it acted."""
    total: dict[str, int] = {}
    hits: dict[str, int] = {}
    for a in acts:
        total[a.model] = total.get(a.model, 0) + 1
        hits[a.model] = hits.get(a.model, 0) + (1 if a.acted else 0)
    return {m: hits[m] / total[m] for m in sorted(total)}
```

### Task 3: Items without step status, acted views and act-filtered violation sets

Spec v2 §3.1, §3.3 and §3.4.
- Items are the interpreter steps only; `env` steps are not interpreter behaviour.
- A class is the ≡_S projection of the step's slice. The step status is no longer part of the key: a failed step is a capability failure (stage 1), not a reading of T.
- Each view records whether its behaviour acted there, and the Tell variant read.
- V(b) keeps only the violated clauses relevant to an item where b acted.

- Modify: `specdx/workbench.py` (`Workbench.loop_context`, the lens line, and imports)

**Interfaces:**

Its `.steps` are the interpreter steps only; `.relevant: dict[str, frozenset[str]]` is keyed by interpreter step id. - [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`specdx/infer/items.py` (whole file):
```python
"""Per-step views of a behaviour: the class an interpreter's output falls in at each step.

Items are the interpreter steps of a scenario (spec v2 §3.1). A class is the projection
of the end state onto the step's slice under ≡_S (v1 §2.2), without the step's status
(v2 §3.3): a failed step is a capability failure (stage 1), not a reading of T.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass

from specdx.infer.act import INTERPRETED, Act, SliceRows, is_act, slice_rows
from specdx.infer.latent import Observation
from specdx.orchestrate.work_orders import WorkStep
from specdx.spec.clauses import Clause, ClauseSet, rows_read
from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnModel, Projection, Tables
from specdx.types import Behaviour


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ItemView:
    """acted: whether the behaviour acted at this item (stage 1); only acted views enter
    stage 2. variant: the Tell variant the interpreter read (for paraphrase sensitivity)."""

    item: str
    run_key: str
    model: str
    family: str
    klass: str
    violated: frozenset[str]
    projection: Projection
    acted: bool = True
    variant: int = 0


def _keyed(step: WorkStep, table: str) -> set[str] | None:
    """None when the step owns the whole table; else the keys it owns (maybe none)."""
    keys: set[str] = set()
    for entry in step.slice:
        name, _, key = entry.partition("=")
        if name == table:
            if not key:
                return None
            keys.add(key)
    return keys


def _owns(step: WorkStep, table: str, key: str | None) -> bool:
    """Whether the step's slice owns these rows: the whole table, or the keyed row."""
    keys = _keyed(step, table)
    return keys is None or (key is not None and key in keys)


def _relevant(step: WorkStep, clause: Clause) -> bool:
    if clause.kind == "step":
        return bool(clause.payload["step"] == step.id)
    if clause.kind == "vocabulary":
        return False
    if clause.kind == "golden_event":
        keys = _keyed(step, "evt")
        return keys is None or clause.payload["event"][1] in keys
    if clause.kind == "golden":
        keys = _keyed(step, clause.payload["table"])
        ident = ":".join(str(v) for v in clause.payload["key"].values())
        return keys is None or ident in keys
    if clause.kind == "invariant":
        inv = clause.payload["invariant"]
        keys = _keyed(step, inv["table"])
        if keys is None:
            return True
        return bool(keys & {str(v) for v in (inv.get("where") or {}).values()})
    if clause.kind == "blank":
        keys = _keyed(step, clause.payload["table"])
        return keys is None
    if clause.kind in ("expectation", "reconcile", "guard_pay"):
        # Cross-cutting: only the steps owning the rows read, never mere table overlap.
        return any(_owns(step, table, key) for table, key in rows_read(clause))
    raise ValueError(f"unknown clause kind {clause.kind!r}")


class SpecLens:
    """drafting: the steps whose actor may draft tables (act.drafting_steps). initial: the
    tables every run starts from (the background rows); a drafting step acts only when its
    slice ends with a row these did not hold."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        clauses: ClauseSet,
        steps: Sequence[WorkStep],
        columns: ColumnModel,
        *,
        drafting: frozenset[str] = frozenset(),
        initial: Tables | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.clauses, self.columns, self.drafting = clauses, columns, drafting
        self.steps = tuple(s for s in steps if s.kind in INTERPRETED)
        self.relevant = {
            s.id: frozenset(c.id for c in clauses if _relevant(s, c)) for s in self.steps
        }
        self._before: dict[str, SliceRows] = {
            s.id: slice_rows(columns.project(initial or {}, s.slice)) for s in self.steps
        }

    def _acted(self, behaviour: Behaviour, step: WorkStep, projection: Projection) -> bool:
        if behaviour.status != "ok":
            return False
        return is_act(behaviour.state.steps.get(step.id, ""), step.id in self.drafting,
                      slice_rows(projection), self._before[step.id])

    def acts(self, behaviour: Behaviour) -> list[Act]:
        """Stage-1 data: one act indicator per interpreter step. A run that failed as a
        whole acted nowhere."""
        return [
            Act(f"{behaviour.scenario.id}|{step.id}", behaviour.run_key,
                behaviour.interpreter.model,
                self._acted(behaviour, step,
                            self.columns.project(behaviour.state.tables, step.slice)))
            for step in self.steps
        ]

    def violations(self, behaviour: Behaviour) -> frozenset[str]:
        if behaviour.status != "ok":
            return frozenset()
        return self.clauses.violations(behaviour.state, behaviour.scenario)

    def views(self, behaviour: Behaviour, violated: frozenset[str]) -> list[ItemView]:
        if behaviour.status != "ok":
            return []
        out: list[ItemView] = []
        for step in self.steps:
            projection = self.columns.project(behaviour.state.tables, step.slice)
            klass = hashlib.sha1(repr(projection).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
            out.append(ItemView(
                item=f"{behaviour.scenario.id}|{step.id}", run_key=behaviour.run_key,
                model=behaviour.interpreter.model, family=behaviour.interpreter.family,
                klass=klass, violated=violated & self.relevant[step.id], projection=projection,
                acted=self._acted(behaviour, step, projection),
                variant=behaviour.interpreter.variant,
            ))
        return out

    def diff(self, a: Projection, b: Projection) -> frozenset[str]:
        return self.columns.diff(a, b)


def observations(views: Iterable[ItemView]) -> list[Observation]:
    """Stage-2 data (spec v2 §3.3): acted views only; the others contribute nothing."""
    return [Observation(v.item, v.run_key, v.model, v.family, v.klass) for v in views if v.acted]


def acted_violations(views: Iterable[ItemView]) -> frozenset[str]:
    """V(b) of spec v2 §3.4: the violated clauses relevant to an item where b acted. A
    clause relevant only where b did not act is missing for b, not violated."""
    return frozenset().union(*(v.violated for v in views if v.acted))


def items_reading(
    clause: str, scenario_id: str, relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]]
) -> list[str]:
    """The items of one scenario whose step the clause is relevant to."""
    return [f"{scenario_id}|{step}" for step, ids in relevant.items() if clause in ids]
```

### Task 4: Stage 2 on acted behaviours with a Beta(7, 3) anchor

Spec v2 §3.3: θₘ has a Beta(7, 3) prior, and the M-step is its MAP, θₘ = (Sₘ + 6) / (nₘ + 8).
- Sₘ is the expected number of the model's admissible behaviours (the sum of its responsibilities); nₘ is the number of its acted behaviours.
- Only acted behaviours are observations; callers filter them with `items.observations` (Task 3).
- **Planning resolutions 4 and 5:** ε is re-estimated in every EM iteration as the error distribution. The family effect is reported, not fed back. A listed model with no acted behaviour gets θ = 0.75.

The end-to-end test grows from one run per model and scenario to two seeds (budget 12). With the anchored θ, one reference run against one deviant run per item no longer makes P(|A| ≥ 2) reach 0.5. From Task 6 on, an item also needs at least 3 acted behaviours to be observable.

**Files:**
- Modify: `specdx/infer/latent.py` (whole file below)
- Modify: `tests/test_end_to_end.py` (`CONFIG` and two population counts)
- Test: `tests/infer/test_latent.py` (imports, appended tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 3): `ItemView`, `observations` (tests only).
- Produces (used by Tasks 6, 7, 10, 17):
  - `ALPHA, BETA = 7.0, 3.0`;
  - `fit(observations, *, models: Mapping[str, str] | None = None, k_max=6, p_single=0.9, alpha=ALPHA, beta=BETA, iters=50, tol=1e-6, theta0=0.7) -> Posterior`. `models` maps model → family for the whole population;
  - `ItemPosterior` gains `error: dict[str, float]` (default empty), the item's ε;
  - `Posterior.family_effect` is a family's mean logit θ minus the population's.
  - The other names (`RARE`, `Observation`, `ItemPosterior.map_set`, `Posterior`, `_subset_prior`) are unchanged.

```python
import random

import pytest

from specdx.infer.items import ItemView, observations

```
and append:
```python
def test_theta_is_the_beta_map_of_the_expected_admissible_behaviours() -> None:
    obs = _dataset()
    post = fit(obs)
    for model in FAMILY:
        mine = [o for o in obs if o.model == model]
        admissible = sum(post.items[o.item].responsibility[o.run_key] for o in mine)
        assert post.theta[model] == pytest.approx((admissible + 6) / (len(mine) + 8), abs=1e-4)


def test_a_listed_model_that_never_acted_keeps_the_prior_mode() -> None:
    post = fit(_dataset(), models={"silent": "fz"})
    assert post.theta["silent"] == pytest.approx(0.75)
    assert fit([], models={"silent": "fz"}).theta == {"silent": pytest.approx(0.75)}


def _p1_shaped(seed: int = 3) -> list[ItemView]:
    """After the P1 measurements (72 items, 9 runs per model and item): two qwen models act
    in about a quarter of their runs and then mostly agree; their failed steps scatter over
    several failure modes. llama acts in none of its runs and always fails the same way."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    views = []
    for j in range(72):
        for model, family in (("qwen3", "qwen"), ("qwen2.5", "qwen"), ("llama", "llama")):
            for r in range(9):
                key = f"{model}|{j}|{r}"
                acting = model != "llama" and rng.random() < 0.25
                if acting:
                    klass = f"A{j}" if rng.random() < 0.9 else f"noise-{rng.randrange(4)}"
                elif model == "llama":
                    klass = "stuck"
                else:
                    klass = f"fail-{rng.randrange(6)}"
                views.append(ItemView(f"i{j}", key, model, family, klass, frozenset(), (),
                                      acted=acting))
    return views


def test_a_model_that_never_acts_cannot_pull_the_acting_models_below_the_prior_mean() -> None:
    views = _p1_shaped()
    population = {"qwen3": "qwen", "qwen2.5": "qwen", "llama": "llama"}
    unfiltered = fit([Observation(v.item, v.run_key, v.model, v.family, v.klass) for v in views])
    assert unfiltered.theta["qwen3"] < 0.7 < unfiltered.theta["llama"]  # the P1 inversion
    post = fit(observations(views), models=population)
    assert post.theta["qwen3"] >= 0.7 and post.theta["qwen2.5"] >= 0.7
    assert post.theta["qwen3"] >= post.theta["llama"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`specdx/infer/latent.py` (whole file):
```python
"""Stage 2 of the act→read model: a set-valued latent-class model (spec v1 §3.2, v2 §3.3).

Each item (a scenario step) has an unknown SET A of admissible output classes. A model
with competence θ emits an admissible class with probability θ (uniformly within A),
and otherwise a class drawn from the item's error distribution ε. Competence is shared
across items, which is what separates a specification defect (every competent model
deviates at the same place) from an agent error (scattered).

ε is re-estimated in every EM iteration from the outputs not explained as faithful. P1
used the smoothed distribution of all outputs instead; then an item where everyone agrees
is explained as well by ε as by competence, the likelihood favours θ → 0, and EM drifts
there (the P1 inversion).

Only acted behaviours are observations here (items.observations). θ has a Beta(α, β)
prior and the M-step is its MAP, θ_m = (S_m + α − 1) / (n_m + α + β − 2), with S_m the
expected number of the model's admissible behaviours and n_m its acted ones: a model that
never acts keeps the prior mode and cannot drag the others' θ.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from itertools import combinations

RARE = "__rare__"
_EDGE = 1e-4
ALPHA, BETA = 7.0, 3.0  # spec v2 §3.10: θ ~ Beta(7, 3)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Observation:
    item: str
    run_key: str
    model: str
    family: str
    klass: str


@dataclass
class ItemPosterior:
    """error: the item's error distribution ε over its classes and RARE."""

    classes: tuple[str, ...]
    set_posterior: dict[frozenset[str], float]
    p_member: dict[str, float]
    p_ambiguous: float
    responsibility: dict[str, float]
    error: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def map_set(self) -> frozenset[str]:
        return max(
            self.set_posterior,
            key=lambda s: (self.set_posterior[s], -len(s), tuple(sorted(s))),
        )


@dataclass
class Posterior:
    """family_effect: each family's mean logit θ minus the population's (reported; the
    Beta prior, not the family, anchors θ)."""

    theta: dict[str, float]
    family_effect: dict[str, float]
    items: dict[str, ItemPosterior]


def _logit(p: float) -> float:
    return math.log(p / (1 - p))


def _log_sum_exp(values: Sequence[float]) -> float:
    top = max(values)
    return top + math.log(sum(math.exp(v - top) for v in values))


def _subset_prior(m: int, p_single: float) -> dict[int, float]:
    """Prior mass of one subset of each size: singletons share p_single."""
    if m == 1:
        return {1: 1.0}
    weights = {s: 0.5 ** (s - 1) for s in range(2, m + 1)}
    total = sum(weights.values())
    prior = {1: p_single / m}
    for size, w in weights.items():
        prior[size] = (1 - p_single) * (w / total) / math.comb(m, size)
    return prior


def _top(obs: Sequence[Observation], k_max: int) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    counts = Counter(o.klass for o in obs)
    return tuple(k for k, _ in sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))[:k_max])


def _error_update(
    obs: Sequence[Observation], top: tuple[str, ...], responsibility: Mapping[str, float]
) -> dict[str, float]:
    """The M-step for ε: what the outputs not explained as faithful look like, smoothed by
    one per class. Starting from every output (all responsibilities 0) gives the plain
    smoothed empirical distribution."""
    mass: dict[str, float] = {k: 1.0 for k in (*top, RARE)}
    for o in obs:
        k = o.klass if o.klass in top else RARE
        mass[k] += 1.0 - responsibility.get(o.run_key, 0.0)
    total = sum(mass.values())
    return {k: v / total for k, v in mass.items()}


def _item_posterior(
    obs: Sequence[Observation],
    theta: dict[str, float],
    k_max: int,
    p_single: float,
    eps: Mapping[str, float] | None = None,
) -> ItemPosterior:
    top = _top(obs, k_max)
    m = len(top)
    if eps is None:
        eps = _error_update(obs, top, {})
    labelled = [(o, o.klass if o.klass in top else RARE) for o in obs]
    prior = _subset_prior(m, p_single)
    subsets = [frozenset(c) for size in range(1, m + 1) for c in combinations(top, size)]
    log_post: dict[frozenset[str], float] = {}
    for subset in subsets:
        lp = math.log(prior[len(subset)])
        for o, k in labelled:
            th = theta[o.model]
            faith = th / len(subset) if k in subset else 0.0
            lp += math.log(faith + (1 - th) * eps[k])
        log_post[subset] = lp
    z = _log_sum_exp(list(log_post.values()))
    post = {s: math.exp(v - z) for s, v in log_post.items()}
    p_member = {k: sum(p for s, p in post.items() if k in s) for k in top}
    p_ambiguous = sum(p for s, p in post.items() if len(s) >= 2)
    responsibility: dict[str, float] = {}
    for o, k in labelled:
        th = theta[o.model]
        r = 0.0
        for s, p in post.items():
            faith = th / len(s) if k in s else 0.0
            r += p * faith / (faith + (1 - th) * eps[k])
        responsibility[o.run_key] = r
    return ItemPosterior(top, post, p_member, p_ambiguous, responsibility, dict(eps))


def _family_effect(theta: dict[str, float], family: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, float]:
    logits = {m: _logit(min(max(th, _EDGE), 1 - _EDGE)) for m, th in theta.items()}
    mu = sum(logits.values()) / len(logits)
    out: dict[str, float] = {}
    for fam in sorted(set(family.values())):
        members = [logits[m] for m in sorted(theta) if family[m] == fam]
        out[fam] = sum(members) / len(members) - mu
    return out


def fit(
    observations: Sequence[Observation],
    *,
    models: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    k_max: int = 6,
    p_single: float = 0.9,
    alpha: float = ALPHA,
    beta: float = BETA,
    iters: int = 50,
    tol: float = 1e-6,
    theta0: float = 0.7,
) -> Posterior:
    """models: model → family of the whole population. A listed model without any acted
    behaviour still gets θ, the prior's mode (n_m = 0)."""
    family = dict(models or {}) | {o.model: o.family for o in observations}
    if not family:
        return Posterior({}, {}, {})
    by_item: dict[str, list[Observation]] = defaultdict(list)
    for o in observations:
        by_item[o.item].append(o)
    theta = {m: theta0 for m in sorted(family)}
    eps: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for _ in range(iters):
        items = {i: _item_posterior(obs, theta, k_max, p_single, eps.get(i))
                 for i, obs in by_item.items()}
        new_eps = {i: _error_update(obs, items[i].classes, items[i].responsibility)
                   for i, obs in by_item.items()}
        moved = max((abs(new_eps[i][k] - items[i].error[k]) for i in new_eps for k in new_eps[i]),
                    default=0.0)
        eps = new_eps
        admissible: dict[str, float] = defaultdict(float)
        acted: Counter[str] = Counter()
        for o in observations:
            admissible[o.model] += items[o.item].responsibility[o.run_key]
            acted[o.model] += 1
        new = {
            m: min(max((admissible[m] + alpha - 1) / (acted[m] + alpha + beta - 2), _EDGE),
                   1 - _EDGE)
            for m in theta
        }
        delta = max(abs(new[m] - theta[m]) for m in theta)
        theta = new
        if max(delta, moved) < tol:
            break
    items = {i: _item_posterior(obs, theta, k_max, p_single, eps.get(i))
             for i, obs in by_item.items()}
    return Posterior(theta, _family_effect(theta, family), items)
```

### Task 5: S2, paraphrase sensitivity

Spec v2 §3.6. For an item, the readings are the acted behaviours of competent models (θₘ ≥ 0.5), each a (model, variant, class).
- **Statistic:** the plug-in I(K; V | M), the mutual information between class and Tell variant within each model, weighted by the model's share.
- **Null distribution:** permuting V within each model R = 2000 times with a seeded generator. Each item has its own stream.
- **p-value:** pₛ = (1 + #{I_perm ≥ I_obs}) / (R + 1).
- **Eligibility:** an item is tested only with at least 6 readings, at least 2 classes and at least 2 variants.
- **Multiple testing:** Benjamini–Hochberg at q = 0.1 across the tested items of one specification.

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/infer/sensitivity.py`
- Test: `tests/infer/test_sensitivity.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 3): `ItemView` (`acted`, `variant`, `klass`, `model`, `item`).
- Produces (used by Tasks 6, 10, 17):
  - `COMPETENT = 0.5`, `PERMUTATIONS = 2000`, `MIN_BEHAVIOURS = 6`, `BH_Q = 0.1`;
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) Reading(model: str, variant: int, klass: str)`;
  - `eligible(readings: Sequence[Reading], min_behaviours: int = MIN_BEHAVIOURS) -> bool`;
  - `permutation_test(readings, *, permutations=PERMUTATIONS, seed: int | str = 0, item: str = "") -> tuple[float, float]`, returning (statistic, p);
  - `benjamini_hochberg(pvalues: Mapping[str, float], q: float = BH_Q) -> frozenset[str]`;
  - `paraphrase_sensitivity(views, theta, items, *, competent=COMPETENT, permutations=PERMUTATIONS, min_behaviours=MIN_BEHAVIOURS, seed: int | str = 0) -> dict[str, tuple[float, float]]`, with an entry only for each tested item.

```python
import random

from specdx.infer.items import ItemView
from specdx.infer.sensitivity import (
    Reading,
    benjamini_hochberg,
    eligible,
    paraphrase_sensitivity,
    permutation_test,
)

MODELS = ("m1", "m2")


def _readings(klass_of) -> list[Reading]:
    return [Reading(m, v, klass_of(m, v, s)) for m in MODELS for v in (0, 1, 2) for s in (1, 2, 3)]


def test_a_planted_variant_effect_is_detected() -> None:
    stat, p = permutation_test(_readings(lambda m, v, s: "X" if v == 0 else "Y"), seed=1)
    assert stat > 0.6 and p < 0.01


def test_the_type_one_error_is_controlled_under_the_null() -> None:
    rng = random.Random(7)
    pvalues = {}
    for i in range(200):
        readings = _readings(lambda m, v, s: rng.choice("XY"))
        if eligible(readings):
            pvalues[f"i{i}"] = permutation_test(readings, seed=1, item=f"i{i}")[1]
    rejected = sum(p <= 0.1 for p in pvalues.values()) / len(pvalues)
    assert len(pvalues) >= 190 and rejected <= 0.1 + 0.05
    assert len(benjamini_hochberg(pvalues, 0.1)) / len(pvalues) <= 0.1 + 0.05


def test_the_test_is_deterministic_for_a_seed() -> None:
    readings = _readings(lambda m, v, s: "X" if (v + s) % 2 else "Y")
    assert permutation_test(readings, seed=3, item="a") == permutation_test(readings, seed=3,
                                                                            item="a")


def test_benjamini_hochberg_steps_up() -> None:
    p = {"a": 0.001, "b": 0.01, "c": 0.03, "d": 0.2, "e": 0.5}
    assert benjamini_hochberg(p, 0.1) == {"a", "b", "c"}
    assert benjamini_hochberg({"a": 0.05, "b": 0.05}, 0.1) == {"a", "b"}
    assert benjamini_hochberg({}, 0.1) == frozenset()


def test_only_acted_views_of_competent_models_on_eligible_items_are_tested() -> None:
    def view(item, model, variant, klass, acted=True):
        return ItemView(item, f"{item}|{model}|{variant}|{klass}", model, "f", klass,
                        frozenset(), (), acted=acted, variant=variant)

    views = [view("split", m, v, "X" if v == 0 else "Y") for m in ("m1", "m2", "weak")
             for v in (0, 1, 2)]
    views += [view("split", "m1", 1, "Y", acted=False), view("few", "m1", 0, "X"),
              view("few", "m1", 1, "Y")]
    tested = paraphrase_sensitivity(views, {"m1": 0.9, "m2": 0.6, "weak": 0.2},
                                    ["split", "few"], seed=1)
    assert set(tested) == {"split"}  # "few" has 2 behaviours, fewer than 6
    assert not eligible([Reading("m1", v, "X") for v in (0, 1, 2)] * 2)  # one class
    assert not eligible([Reading("m1", 0, k) for k in "XYXYXY"])  # one variant
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`specdx/infer/sensitivity.py`:
```python
"""S2, paraphrase sensitivity (spec v2 §3.6): does a competent model's class depend on
which paraphrase of the Tell text it read?

The statistic is the plug-in conditional mutual information I(K; V | M) between class K
and Tell variant V, stratified by model M. Its null distribution comes from permuting V
within each model's stratum, which keeps every model's variant counts and class counts.
Items of one specification are corrected together with Benjamini–Hochberg.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from collections import defaultdict
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from specdx.infer.items import ItemView

COMPETENT = 0.5  # spec v2 §3.10
PERMUTATIONS = 2000
MIN_BEHAVIOURS = 6
BH_Q = 0.1
# Permuted statistics within this distance of the observed one count as reaching it.
_TIE = 1e-12


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Reading:
    """One acted behaviour of a competent model at one item."""

    model: str
    variant: int
    klass: str


def eligible(readings: Sequence[Reading], min_behaviours: int = MIN_BEHAVIOURS) -> bool:
    return (len(readings) >= min_behaviours and len({r.klass for r in readings}) >= 2
            and len({r.variant for r in readings}) >= 2)


def _codes(values: Sequence) -> np.ndarray:
    index = {v: i for i, v in enumerate(sorted(set(values)))}
    return np.array([index[v] for v in values], dtype=np.int64)


def _mutual_information(variants: np.ndarray, klass: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """I(K; V) for each row of variants (rows: permutations), in nats."""
    rows, n = variants.shape
    nv, nk = int(variants.max()) + 1, int(klass.max()) + 1
    cells = variants * nk + klass[None, :]
    counts = np.zeros((rows, nv * nk))
    np.add.at(counts, (np.repeat(np.arange(rows), n), cells.ravel()), 1.0)
    joint = counts.reshape(rows, nv, nk)
    pv = joint.sum(axis=2, keepdims=True)
    pk = joint.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        terms = np.where(joint > 0, joint / n * np.log(joint * n / (pv * pk)), 0.0)
    return terms.sum(axis=(1, 2))


def _seed(seed: int | str, item: str) -> int:
    return int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(f"{seed}|{item}".encode()).digest()[:8], "big")


def permutation_test(
    readings: Sequence[Reading], *, permutations: int = PERMUTATIONS, seed: int | str = 0,
    item: str = "",
) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """(I(K; V | M), p) with p = (1 + #{I_perm ≥ I_obs}) / (R + 1)."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(_seed(seed, item))
    strata: dict[str, list[Reading]] = defaultdict(list)
    for r in readings:
        strata[r.model].append(r)
    total = len(readings)
    observed = 0.0
    permuted = np.zeros(permutations)
    for model in sorted(strata):
        mine = strata[model]
        variants = _codes([r.variant for r in mine])
        klass = _codes([r.klass for r in mine])
        weight = len(mine) / total
        observed += weight * float(_mutual_information(variants[None, :], klass)[0])
        shuffled = rng.permuted(np.tile(variants, (permutations, 1)), axis=1)
        permuted += weight * _mutual_information(shuffled, klass)
    reached = int((permuted >= observed - _TIE).sum())
    return observed, (1 + reached) / (permutations + 1)


def benjamini_hochberg(pvalues: Mapping[str, float], q: float = BH_Q) -> frozenset[str]:
    """The items whose test is significant at false discovery rate q."""
    ranked = sorted(pvalues.items(), key=lambda kv: (kv[1], kv[0]))
    cut = 0
    for rank, (_, p) in enumerate(ranked, start=1):
        if p <= rank * q / len(ranked):
            cut = rank
    return frozenset(item for item, _ in ranked[:cut])


def paraphrase_sensitivity(
    views: Iterable[ItemView],
    theta: Mapping[str, float],
    items: Iterable[str],
    *,
    competent: float = COMPETENT,
    permutations: int = PERMUTATIONS,
    min_behaviours: int = MIN_BEHAVIOURS,
    seed: int | str = 0,
) -> dict[str, tuple[float, float]]:
    """(statistic, p) for every eligible item among `items`, from the acted views of models
    with θ ≥ competent."""
    wanted = set(items)
    readings: dict[str, list[Reading]] = defaultdict(list)
    for v in views:
        if v.acted and v.item in wanted and theta.get(v.model, 0.0) >= competent:
            readings[v.item].append(Reading(v.model, v.variant, v.klass))
    return {
        item: permutation_test(readings[item], permutations=permutations, seed=seed, item=item)
        for item in sorted(readings) if eligible(readings[item], min_behaviours)
    }
```

Under the null, the raw rejection rate at 0.1 measured 0.035–0.055 over three seeds, and BH rejected nothing.

### Task 6: Diagnosis with observability, τ_H and the S1/S2 ambiguity signals

Spec v2 §3.4–§3.6, and the loop wiring that the new `diagnose` needs.
- **Observability gate.** A candidate is typed only if every clause in it is read by an observable item of its scenario. Otherwise it goes to the Unobservable list, which is reported, never refuted, never typed and never scored.
- **Hidden.** A singleton needs Hidden(c) ≥ τ_H = 0.5.
- **Weak.** It is computed on observable items only.
- **Ambiguity.** It is reported when S1 = P(|Aₛ| ≥ 2) ≥ 0.5, or when S2 is significant after BH. Its score is max(S1, 1 − p).
- **The loop.** It now fits stage 1 on every behaviour's acts and stage 2 on acted views only, and feeds `diagnose` the act-filtered V(b). Planning resolution 6 applies.

**Files:**
- Modify: `specdx/infer/scores.py` (whole file below)
- Modify: `specdx/active/loop.py` (imports, `LensLike`, the body of `run_specdx`)
- Test: `tests/infer/test_scores.py` (whole file below), `tests/active/test_loop.py` (`FakeLens`), `tests/eval/test_pilot.py` (`_Lens`, `_config`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 2: `Act`, `fit_rasch`, `Rasch.observable_items()`;
  - Task 3: `ItemView.acted`, `acted_violations`, `observations`, `items_reading`, `SpecLens.relevant`, `SpecLens.acts`;
  - Task 4: `fit(..., models=)`;
  - Task 5: `paraphrase_sensitivity`, `benjamini_hochberg` and the constants.
- Produces (used by Tasks 7, 10, 12):
  - `TAU_H = 0.5` and `UNOBSERVABLE = "unobservable"` (a `Defect.type` that is never in `DEFECT_TYPES`);
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) ItemSignal(s1: float, s2: float | None, p: float | None, significant: bool)`;
  - `@dataclass Diagnosis(defects: list[Defect], unobservable: list[Defect] = [], signals: dict[str, ItemSignal] = {})`;
  - `run_weights(views, posterior) -> dict[str, float]`: the mean of P([b] ∈ Aₛ) × θ over acted items, 0 for a run that acted nowhere;
  - `diagnose(behaviours, violated, views, posterior, refuter, diff, *, observable: frozenset[str], relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]], tau=0.05, ambig_threshold=0.5, weak_threshold=0.5, weak_min_runs=2, hidden_threshold=TAU_H, competent=COMPETENT, permutations=PERMUTATIONS, s2_min_behaviours=MIN_BEHAVIOURS, bh_q=BH_Q, rng_seed=0, known=frozenset()) -> Diagnosis`.
  - `merge_defects`, `identity`, `ambiguity_key`, `UNATTEMPTED` and `_weak` are unchanged.
- `LensLike` (in `active/loop.py`) gains `relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]]` and `acts(behaviour) -> list[Act]`. Every fake lens in the tests implements them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/infer/test_scores.py` (whole file, replacing the v1 tests):
```python
import pytest

from specdx.infer.items import ItemView, observations
from specdx.infer.latent import ItemPosterior, fit
from specdx.infer.scores import (
    UNATTEMPTED,
    UNOBSERVABLE,
    _weak,
    diagnose,
    merge_defects,
    run_weights,
)
from specdx.types import Behaviour, Defect, InterpreterId, RefuteResult, Scenario, State

GOOD = ("g1", "g2", "g3", "g4")
BAD = ("b1", "b2")
S = Scenario("s1", 15, 12, 0)
CLAUSES = frozenset({"a", "b", "c", "x", "n0", "n1", "n2", "n3", "noise", "low", "high"})
RELEVANT = {"step": CLAUSES}


def _proj(name: str):
    return (("x", ((name, (("v", name),)),)),)


def _diff(a, b):
    return frozenset({"x.v"}) if a != b else frozenset()


class _Refuter:
    def __init__(self, refutes: bool) -> None:
        self.refutes = refutes

    def refute(self, targets, scenario, behaviours):
        return RefuteResult(self.refutes, "w" if self.refutes else None, 1)


def _world(outputs):
    """outputs: model -> list of (klass, violated set) per run, on one item."""
    behaviours, violated, views = [], {}, []
    for model, runs in outputs.items():
        for i, (klass, bad) in enumerate(runs):
            key = f"{model}|{i}"
            behaviours.append(Behaviour(key, InterpreterId(model, model[0], 0, i), S, State({})))
            violated[key] = frozenset(bad)
            views.append(ItemView("s1|step", key, model, model[0], klass, frozenset(bad),
                                  _proj(klass)))
    # Calibration items: competent models agree, each weak model errs in its own way.
    clean = [ItemView(f"c{j}|step", f"{m}|c{j}", m, m[0], "OK" if m in GOOD else f"E{j}-{m}",
                      frozenset(), _proj("OK")) for j in range(6) for m in outputs]
    return behaviours, violated, views + clean, fit(observations(views + clean))


def _diagnose(b, v, views, post, refuter, *, observable=None, permutations=200, **kwargs):
    seen = frozenset(x.item for x in views) if observable is None else observable
    return diagnose(b, v, views, post, refuter, _diff, observable=seen, relevant=RELEVANT,
                    permutations=permutations, **kwargs)


def _shared(bad_clause_too: bool = False):
    """Competent models all violate c; weak models scatter."""
    return {m: [("K", {"c"})] * 3 for m in GOOD} | {
        m: [(f"E{i}", {"c", f"n{i}"} if bad_clause_too else {f"n{i}"}) for i in range(3)]
        for m in BAD}


def test_run_weights_are_membership_times_competence_over_acted_items() -> None:
    _, _, views, post = _world(_shared())
    idle = [ItemView("s1|step", "idle|0", "g1", "g", "K", frozenset(), _proj("K"), acted=False)]
    weights = run_weights(views + idle, post)
    for key, model in (("g1|0", "g1"), ("b1|0", "b1")):
        mine = [v for v in views if v.run_key == key]
        expected = sum(post.items[v.item].p_member.get(v.klass, 0.0) for v in mine) / len(mine)
        assert weights[key] == pytest.approx(expected * post.theta[model])
    assert weights["idle|0"] == 0.0
    assert post.theta["g1"] > post.theta["b1"]


def test_a_shared_violation_that_repair_refutes_is_hidden() -> None:
    b, v, views, post = _world(_shared())
    found = [d for d in _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Refuter(True)).defects
             if d.type == "hidden"]
    assert found and found[0].location == ("c",)


def test_the_same_pattern_unrefuted_is_a_contradiction() -> None:
    b, v, views, post = _world(_shared(bad_clause_too=True))
    found = [d for d in _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Refuter(False)).defects
             if d.type == "contradiction"]
    assert found and found[0].location == ("c",)


def test_competent_models_splitting_cleanly_is_ambiguity() -> None:
    outputs = {m: [("X", set()), ("Y", set()), ("X", set()), ("Y", set())] for m in GOOD}
    outputs |= {m: [(f"E{i}", {f"n{i}"}) for i in range(4)] for m in BAD}
    b, v, views, post = _world(outputs)
    diagnosis = _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Refuter(True))
    found = [d for d in diagnosis.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"]
    assert found and found[0].location == ("x.v",) and found[0].note == "s1|step"
    assert diagnosis.signals["s1|step"].s1 >= 0.5


def test_a_clean_deviant_class_beside_a_clean_admissible_one_is_weak() -> None:
    outputs = {m: [("A", set())] * 3 for m in GOOD} | {m: [("W", set())] * 3 for m in BAD}
    b, v, views, post = _world(outputs)
    found = [d for d in _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Refuter(True)).defects if d.type == "weak"]
    assert found and found[0].location == ("x.v",)


def test_no_weak_when_the_admissible_class_itself_violates() -> None:
    outputs = {m: [("A", {"c"})] * 3 for m in GOOD} | {m: [("W", set())] * 3 for m in BAD}
    b, v, views, post = _world(outputs)
    assert not [d for d in _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Refuter(True)).defects
                if d.type == "weak"]


def test_hidden_score_follows_the_posterior() -> None:
    b, v, views, post = _world(_shared())
    found = [d for d in _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Refuter(True)).defects
             if d.type == "hidden"]
    assert found
    expected_score = sum(p for s, p in post.items["s1|step"].set_posterior.items() if "K" in s)
    assert abs(found[0].score - expected_score) < 1e-9


def test_a_singleton_no_admissible_class_violates_alone_is_not_hidden() -> None:
    # Every competent run violates c together with x: Hidden(c) is 0, below τ_H.
    outputs = {m: [("K", {"c", "x"})] * 3 for m in GOOD} | {
        m: [(f"E{i}", {"c", f"n{i}"}) for i in range(3)] for m in BAD}
    b, v, views, post = _world(outputs)
    assert not [d for d in _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Refuter(True)).defects
                if d.type == "hidden"]


class _MustNotRefute:
    def refute(self, targets, scenario, behaviours):
        raise AssertionError("an unobservable candidate must not be refuted")


def test_a_candidate_no_observable_item_reads_is_unobservable_and_never_refuted() -> None:
    b, v, views, post = _world(_shared())
    diagnosis = _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _MustNotRefute(), observable=frozenset())
    assert diagnosis.defects == [] and diagnosis.signals == {}
    assert [(d.type, d.location) for d in diagnosis.unobservable] == [(UNOBSERVABLE, ("c",))]


def _variant_world():
    """One competent model reads variant 2 another way; everyone else agrees."""
    behaviours, violated, views = [], {}, []
    for m in GOOD:
        for variant in (0, 1, 2):
            for seed in (1, 2, 3):
                key = f"{m}|{variant}|{seed}"
                klass = "Y" if m == "g1" and variant == 2 else "X"
                behaviours.append(Behaviour(key, InterpreterId(m, m[0], variant, seed), S,
                                            State({})))
                violated[key] = frozenset()
                views.append(ItemView("s1|step", key, m, m[0], klass, frozenset(), _proj(klass),
                                      variant=variant))
    clean = [ItemView(f"c{j}|step", f"{m}|c{j}", m, m[0], "OK", frozenset(), _proj("OK"))
             for j in range(6) for m in GOOD]
    return behaviours, violated, views + clean, fit(observations(views + clean))


def test_paraphrase_sensitivity_reports_an_ambiguity_the_set_signal_misses() -> None:
    b, v, views, post = _variant_world()
    diagnosis = _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Refuter(True), permutations=2000)
    signal = diagnosis.signals["s1|step"]
    assert signal.s1 < 0.5 and signal.significant and signal.p is not None
    found = [d for d in diagnosis.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"]
    assert found and found[0].location == ("x.v",)
    assert found[0].score == pytest.approx(1 - signal.p)


class _Unattempted:
    """The probe budget ran out: no refutation was attempted."""

    def refute(self, targets, scenario, behaviours):
        return RefuteResult(False, None, 0, attempted=False)


def test_an_unattempted_singleton_is_hidden_not_a_contradiction() -> None:
    b, v, views, post = _world(_shared(bad_clause_too=True))
    defects = _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Unattempted()).defects
    assert not [d for d in defects if d.type == "contradiction"]
    found = [d for d in defects if d.type == "hidden"]
    assert found and found[0].location == ("c",) and "budget" in found[0].note


def test_an_unattempted_set_is_no_contradiction() -> None:
    outputs = {m: [("A", {"a"})] * 3 for m in GOOD[:2]} | {m: [("B", {"b"})] * 3
                                                          for m in GOOD[2:]}
    outputs |= {m: [(f"E{i}", {"a", "b"}) for i in range(3)] for m in BAD}
    b, v, views, post = _world(outputs)
    attempted = _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Refuter(False)).defects
    assert [d.location for d in attempted if d.type == "contradiction"] == [("a", "b")]
    skipped = _diagnose(b, v, views, post, _Unattempted()).defects
    assert not [d for d in skipped if d.location == ("a", "b")]


def test_candidates_are_refuted_in_descending_score_order(monkeypatch) -> None:
    supports = {"low": 0.96, "high": 0.99}
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "specdx.infer.scores.soft_hitting_sets",
        lambda sets, tau: [(sets[0][0], supports[next(iter(sets[0][0]))])],
    )
    s2 = Scenario("s2", 15, 13, 0)
    behaviours, violated, views = [], {}, []
    for scenario, clause in ((S, "low"), (s2, "high")):
        for m in GOOD:
            key = f"{scenario.id}|{m}"
            behaviours.append(Behaviour(key, InterpreterId(m, m[0], 0, 1), scenario, State({})))
            violated[key] = frozenset({clause})
            views.append(ItemView(f"{scenario.id}|step", key, m, m[0], "K",
                                  frozenset({clause}), _proj("K")))
    post = fit(observations(views))
    order = []

    class _Recording:
        def refute(self, targets, scenario, behaviours):
            order.append(next(iter(targets)))
            return RefuteResult(True, "w", 1)

    _diagnose(behaviours, violated, views, post, _Recording())
    assert order == ["high", "low"]
    # On a mutant, a location the base report already holds goes last, whatever its score
    order.clear()
    _diagnose(behaviours, violated, views, post, _Recording(), known=frozenset({("high",)}))
    assert order == ["low", "high"]


def test_weak_tie_break_is_deterministic() -> None:
    views = [
        ItemView("item", f"model|{i}", "model", "m", klass, frozenset(),
                 _proj(klass))
        for klass in ("A", "B", "C") for i in range(3)
    ]
    ip = ItemPosterior(
        classes=("A", "B", "C"),
        set_posterior={frozenset({"A"}): 1.0},
        p_member={"A": 1.0, "B": 0.0, "C": 0.0},
        p_ambiguous=0.0,
        responsibility={},
    )
    result = _weak(ip, views, 2)
    assert result == ("B", 1.0)


def test_weak_union_score() -> None:
    views = [
        ItemView("item", f"model|{i}", "model", "m", klass, frozenset(),
                 _proj(klass))
        for klass in ("A", "B", "C") for i in range(3)
    ]
    ip = ItemPosterior(
        classes=("A", "B", "C"),
        set_posterior={
            frozenset({"A"}): 0.5,
            frozenset({"A", "B"}): 0.3,
            frozenset({"A", "C"}): 0.2,
        },
        p_member={"A": 1.0, "B": 0.0, "C": 0.0},
        p_ambiguous=0.0,
        responsibility={},
    )
    result = _weak(ip, views, 2)
    assert result is not None
    klass, score = result
    assert klass == "C"
    assert score == 1.0


def test_a_merged_hidden_finding_keeps_an_attempted_refutation_note() -> None:
    unattempted = Defect("hidden", ("c",), ("s1",), ("r1",), 0.9, UNATTEMPTED)
    refuted = Defect("hidden", ("c",), ("s2",), ("r2",), 0.5, "refuted by w")
    for pair in ((unattempted, refuted), (refuted, unattempted)):
        (merged,) = merge_defects(pair)
        assert merged.note == "refuted by w" and merged.score == 0.9
        assert merged.scenarios == ("s1", "s2")
```
