- [ ] **Step 3: Implement diagnose**

`specdx/infer/scores.py` (whole file):
```python
"""From behaviours and the act→read posterior to typed, located defects (spec v2 §3.4–§3.6).

Only observable items carry evidence (stage 1). A candidate reading any clause that no
observable item reads is Unobservable: it is reported, never typed and never scored.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from collections.abc import Callable, Hashable, Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from itertools import combinations

from specdx.infer.hitting import soft_hitting_sets
from specdx.infer.items import ItemView, items_reading
from specdx.infer.latent import ItemPosterior, Posterior
from specdx.infer.sensitivity import (
    BH_Q,
    COMPETENT,
    MIN_BEHAVIOURS,
    PERMUTATIONS,
    benjamini_hochberg,
    paraphrase_sensitivity,
)
from specdx.spec.columns import Projection
from specdx.types import Behaviour, Defect, Refuter

Diff = Callable[[Projection, Projection], frozenset[str]]
TAU_H = 0.5  # spec v2 §3.10: a hidden finding needs Hidden(c) ≥ τ_H
# The kind of an Unobservable entry: not a defect type, never in DEFECT_TYPES.
UNOBSERVABLE = "unobservable"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ItemSignal:
    """An observable item's ambiguity signals: S1 = P(|A_s| ≥ 2); S2's statistic and
    p-value (None when the item was not eligible); whether S2 is significant after BH."""

    s1: float
    s2: float | None
    p: float | None
    significant: bool


@dataclass
class Diagnosis:
    defects: list[Defect]
    unobservable: list[Defect] = field(default_factory=list)
    signals: dict[str, ItemSignal] = field(default_factory=dict)


def run_weights(views: Iterable[ItemView], posterior: Posterior) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Spec v2 §3.3: an acted behaviour weighs P([b] ∈ A_s) × θ_m. A run's weight is the
    mean over the items where it acted; a run that acted nowhere weighs 0."""
    acc: dict[str, list[float]] = defaultdict(list)
    for v in views:
        acc.setdefault(v.run_key, [])
        if not v.acted:
            continue
        item = posterior.items.get(v.item)
        member = item.p_member.get(v.klass, 0.0) if item is not None else 0.0
        acc[v.run_key].append(member * posterior.theta.get(v.model, 0.0))
    return {k: (sum(w) / len(w) if w else 0.0) for k, w in acc.items()}


def ambiguity_key(d: Defect) -> tuple[str, str, tuple[str, ...]]:
    """An ambiguity's identity before localisation: its step and the fields its admissible
    classes differ on (its note is "scenario|step"). Localisation replaces the fields with
    sentence ids that vary between runs, so differentials compare on this key (§11)."""
    return (d.type, d.note.split("|", 1)[-1], d.location)


def identity(d: Defect) -> tuple:
    """What makes two findings one: type and location, and the step for an ambiguity
    that is not localised yet."""
    return ambiguity_key(d) if d.type == "ambiguity" else d.key


# The note of a singleton typed hidden only because the probe budget ran out before any
# refutation attempt: its type is a default, not evidence (§11).
UNATTEMPTED = "refutation not attempted: probe budget exhausted"


def merge_defects(
    defects: Iterable[Defect], key: Callable[[Defect], Hashable] = lambda d: d.key
) -> list[Defect]:
    merged: dict[Hashable, Defect] = {}
    for d in defects:
        k = key(d)
        old = merged.get(k)
        if old is None:
            merged[k] = d
            continue
        best = d if d.score > old.score else old
        note = best.note
        if UNATTEMPTED in (old.note, d.note) and old.note != d.note:
            # A refutation attempted in any scenario is evidence; keep its note (§11).
            note = d.note if old.note == UNATTEMPTED else old.note
        merged[k] = Defect(
            d.type, d.location,
            tuple(sorted(set(old.scenarios) | set(d.scenarios))),
            tuple(sorted(set(old.witnesses) | set(d.witnesses))),
            best.score, note,
        )
    return sorted(merged.values(), key=lambda d: (-d.score, d.type, d.location, d.note))


def _scenario_of(item: str) -> str:
    return item.split("|", 1)[0]


def _hidden_score(
    clause: str, items: Iterable[str], posterior: Posterior,
    by_item: Mapping[str, list[ItemView]],
) -> float | None:
    """Spec §3.4: the posterior mass of admissible sets holding a class whose only
    step-relevant violation is this clause, maximised over the scenario's items."""
    scores = []
    for item in items:
        ip = posterior.items.get(item)
        views = by_item.get(item, [])
        if ip is None or not any(clause in v.violated for v in views):
            continue
        hits = {v.klass for v in views if v.violated == frozenset({clause})}
        scores.append(sum(p for s, p in ip.set_posterior.items() if s & hits))
    return max(scores) if scores else None


def _observable(
    clause: str, scenario_id: str, observable: frozenset[str],
    relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]],
) -> bool:
    return any(item in observable for item in items_reading(clause, scenario_id, relevant))


def _weak(ip: ItemPosterior, views: Sequence[ItemView], min_runs: int) -> tuple[str, float] | None:
    violated = {v.klass: v.violated for v in views}
    map_set = ip.map_set
    if any(violated.get(k) for k in map_set):
        return None  # the admissible output itself fails a check: that is not a weak check
    counts = Counter(v.klass for v in views)
    eligible = sorted(
        k for k in {v.klass for v in views if not v.violated}
        if counts[k] >= min_runs and k not in map_set
    )
    if not eligible:
        return None

    def outside(k: str) -> float:
        if k not in ip.classes:
            return 1.0
        return sum(p for s, p in ip.set_posterior.items() if k not in s)

    if any(k not in ip.classes for k in eligible):
        score = 1.0
    else:
        score = sum(p for s, p in ip.set_posterior.items() if any(k not in s for k in eligible))
    chosen = sorted(eligible, key=lambda k: (-outside(k), -counts[k], k))[0]
    return chosen, score


def _reference(ip: ItemPosterior) -> str:
    """The clean admissible class a weak deviant is compared with: the MAP set's most
    probable member (_weak has checked that the whole MAP set is clean)."""
    return max(sorted(ip.map_set), key=lambda k: ip.p_member.get(k, 0.0))


def diagnose(
    behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour],
    violated: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]],
    views: Sequence[ItemView],
    posterior: Posterior,
    refuter: Refuter,
    diff: Diff,
    *,
    observable: frozenset[str],
    relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]],
    tau: float = 0.05,
    ambig_threshold: float = 0.5,
    weak_threshold: float = 0.5,
    weak_min_runs: int = 2,
    hidden_threshold: float = TAU_H,
    competent: float = COMPETENT,
    permutations: int = PERMUTATIONS,
    s2_min_behaviours: int = MIN_BEHAVIOURS,
    bh_q: float = BH_Q,
    rng_seed: int = 0,
    known: frozenset[tuple[str, ...]] = frozenset(),
) -> Diagnosis:
    """violated: each run's V(b), already restricted to the items where it acted (§3.4).
    observable: the observable items (stage 1). relevant: step id → the clauses relevant
    to it. known: locations the base specification's report already holds. On a mutant
    they are refuted last, so base noise does not spend the probe budget meant for the
    mutant's own candidates."""
    defects: list[Defect] = []
    unobservable: list[Defect] = []
    weights = run_weights(views, posterior)
    by_item: dict[str, list[ItemView]] = defaultdict(list)
    for v in views:
        if v.acted:
            by_item[v.item].append(v)
    groups: dict[str, list[Behaviour]] = defaultdict(list)
    for b in behaviours:
        if b.status == "ok":
            groups[b.scenario.id].append(b)
    # Every scenario's candidates, refuted in descending score order, locations the base
    # report does not hold first: the probe budget goes to the best supported new
    # candidates first (§11).
    candidates: list[tuple[float, str, frozenset[str]]] = []
    for sid, group in groups.items():
        sets = [(violated[b.run_key], weights.get(b.run_key, 0.0)) for b in group]
        candidates += [(support, sid, hitting)
                       for hitting, support in soft_hitting_sets(sets, tau=tau)]
    candidates.sort(key=lambda c: (tuple(sorted(c[2])) in known, -c[0], c[1], len(c[2]),
                                   sorted(c[2])))
    for support, sid, hitting in candidates:
        group = groups[sid]
        witnesses = tuple(b.run_key for b in group if violated[b.run_key] & hitting)
        location = tuple(sorted(hitting))
        if not all(_observable(c, sid, observable, relevant) for c in hitting):
            # No refutation either: nothing the population does can tell its type (§3.9).
            unobservable.append(Defect(UNOBSERVABLE, location, (sid,), witnesses, support,
                                       "no observable item reads every clause"))
            continue
        result = refuter.refute(hitting, group[0].scenario, group)
        if not result.refuted and result.attempted:
            defects.append(Defect("contradiction", location, (sid,), witnesses, support,
                                  f"unrefuted after {result.attempts} attempts"))
        elif len(hitting) == 1:
            # Refuted, or never attempted because the probe budget ran out: a
            # contradiction needs a failed refutation attempt (§11). Hidden(c) must reach
            # τ_H: a candidate no admissible class supports is no hidden requirement.
            clause = next(iter(hitting))
            scenario_items = sorted(i for i in by_item if _scenario_of(i) == sid)
            score = _hidden_score(clause, scenario_items, posterior, by_item) or 0.0
            if score >= hidden_threshold:
                note = f"refuted by {result.witness}" if result.refuted else UNATTEMPTED
                defects.append(Defect("hidden", location, (sid,), witnesses, score, note))
        # A refuted set is dropped; so is an unattempted one, which is no contradiction.
    tested = paraphrase_sensitivity(
        views, posterior.theta, [i for i in posterior.items if i in observable],
        competent=competent, permutations=permutations, min_behaviours=s2_min_behaviours,
        seed=rng_seed,
    )
    significant = benjamini_hochberg({i: p for i, (_, p) in tested.items()}, bh_q)
    signals: dict[str, ItemSignal] = {}
    for item, ip in sorted(posterior.items.items()):
        if item not in observable:
            continue
        stat, p = tested.get(item, (None, None))
        signals[item] = ItemSignal(ip.p_ambiguous, stat, p, item in significant)
        mine = by_item.get(item, [])
        projection = {v.klass: v.projection for v in mine}
        ranked = sorted(ip.p_member, key=lambda k: -ip.p_member[k])
        if (ip.p_ambiguous >= ambig_threshold or item in significant) and len(ranked) >= 2:
            members = sorted(ip.map_set) if len(ip.map_set) >= 2 else ranked[:2]
            fields = frozenset().union(
                *(diff(projection[a], projection[b]) for a, b in combinations(members, 2))
            )
            if fields:
                witnesses = tuple(v.run_key for v in mine if v.klass in members)
                score = max(ip.p_ambiguous, 1 - p if p is not None else 0.0)
                defects.append(Defect("ambiguity", tuple(sorted(fields)), (_scenario_of(item),),
                                      witnesses, score, note=item))
        weak = _weak(ip, mine, weak_min_runs)
        if weak is not None and weak[1] >= weak_threshold:
            klass, score = weak
            fields = diff(projection[klass], projection[_reference(ip)])
            if fields:
                witnesses = tuple(v.run_key for v in mine if v.klass == klass)
                defects.append(Defect("weak", tuple(sorted(fields)), (_scenario_of(item),),
                                      witnesses, score, note=item))
    return Diagnosis(merge_defects(defects, key=identity), merge_defects(unobservable), signals)
```

```python
            tau=config.tau, ambig_threshold=config.ambig_threshold,
            weak_threshold=config.weak_threshold, known=known,
        )
```

### Task 7: Offline replay on the P1 cache: GO/NO-GO before any infrastructure work

Spec v2 §6, "Offline replay check". The v2 inference runs on the cached P1 runs of the base and the 20 development mutants, with the old population and no GPU. No new run is executed.

It maps runs to specifications as `experiments/p1_dev_diagnostics.py` does: by recomputing `Runner.run`'s key. The context digest is pinned to P1's: `RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION` 2 and P1's executor settings. Task 8 bumps the version and adds a setting, and the replay must still find P1's runs. No refutation is attempted, so a singleton candidate types hidden when Hidden(c) ≥ τ_H, and a larger set is dropped.

**Pre-registered GO criterion: a mechanism check** (author's ruling, 2026-10-12; also in `experiments/p1b-preregistration.md`, Task 17). It holds when all three are true:
1. **On the base and on each of the 20 development mutants**, stage 1 gives llama3.1:8b, which never writes, a κ strictly below every other model's.
2. **On the same 21 specifications**, every model with at least one acted behaviour has stage-2 θ ≥ 0.5, the collapse floor (P1 gave qwen3:14b θ = 0.003), and at least one model acted.
3. **On the base**, at least 50% of the P1 base findings become Unobservable.

A θ ordering between models is not part of it. On the P1 population only one family writes, so that ordering carries no information (Proposition 2′). Task 18 checks the ordering on the screened population.

The report also gives the number of P1 base findings that become Unobservable, the per-item Oₛ and the S2 p-values.

**Files:**
- Modify: `specdx/observe/run.py` (`context_digest` gains `schema`)
- Modify: `specdx/workbench.py` (new `tell_payload`, used by `Workbench.open`)
- Create: `experiments/p1b_replay.py`, `results/p1b-replay.md` (generated)
- Test: `tests/test_replay.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 3: `SpecLens`, `acted_violations`, `observations`, `items_reading`;
  - Task 2: `fit_rasch`, `act_rate`, `Rasch`;
  - Task 4: `fit(..., models=)`;
  - Task 6: `diagnose(..., observable=, relevant=)`, `Diagnosis`;
  - unchanged: `GateConfig.load`, `committee`, `run_key`, `A1_SITES`, `A2_SITES`, `boundary_domain`, `canon_scenarios`, `enumerate_scenarios`.
- Produces:
  - `context_digest(tell, settings, *, schema: int | None = None) -> str`;
  - `tell_payload(variants: TellVariants) -> dict[str, object]` (in `specdx.workbench`);
  - script functions `load_cache`, `model_digests`, `variant_of`, `population_runs`, `replay`, `go(r: Replay) -> tuple[bool, str]`, `p1_status`, `unobservable_share(statuses: Sequence[str]) -> float`, `verdict(replays: list[Replay], base_line: dict[str, Any]) -> bool`, `render`, `main`, the class `NoRefutation` and the dataclass `Replay(spec, lens, behaviours, views, acts, rasch, posterior, diagnosis)`. Task 18 relies on none of them.

```python
"""The offline replay's pieces that need no run cache: the pinned P1 context digest and
the pre-registered GO criterion."""

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from specdx.infer.act import Act, Rasch
from specdx.infer.latent import Posterior
from specdx.infer.scores import Diagnosis
from specdx.observe.run import RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION, context_digest
from specdx.types import Scenario

SCRIPT = Path(__file__).parents[1] / "experiments" / "p1b_replay.py"


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def replay():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("p1b_replay", SCRIPT)
    assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[spec.name] = module  # dataclasses resolve their module while it loads
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_an_older_schema_version_gives_the_context_digest_runs_were_cached_under() -> None:
    tell, settings = {"standing": ["a"]}, {"temperature": 0.7}
    assert context_digest(tell, settings) == context_digest(tell, settings,
                                                            schema=RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION)
    assert context_digest(tell, settings, schema=2) != context_digest(tell, settings, schema=99)


def _result(replay, theta, kappa, acted=()):
    rasch = Rasch(kappa, {}, {}, {})
    acts = [Act("grn12|step", f"{m}|1", m, True) for m in acted]
    return replay.Replay("base", None, [], [], acts, rasch, Posterior(theta, {}, {}),
                         Diagnosis([]))


def test_go_is_the_mechanism_check_on_kappa_theta_floor_and_unobservable_share(replay) -> None:
    q3, q25, ll = "qwen3:14b", "qwen2.5:7b-instruct-q4_K_M", "llama3.1:8b"
    kappa, writers = {q3: 0.5, q25: 0.0, ll: -1.0}, (q3, q25)
    # the θ ordering is not judged: llama above qwen3, as on the P1 population, still passes
    assert replay.go(_result(replay, {q3: 0.74, q25: 0.6, ll: 0.96}, kappa, writers))[0]
    # (a) the model that never writes must have the lowest κ, strictly
    assert not replay.go(_result(replay, {q3: 0.74, q25: 0.6, ll: 0.96},
                                 {q3: 0.5, q25: -2.0, ll: -1.0}, writers))[0]
    assert not replay.go(_result(replay, {q3: 0.74, q25: 0.6, ll: 0.96},
                                 {q3: 0.5, q25: -1.0, ll: -1.0}, writers))[0]
    # (b) an acting model collapsing below 0.5 fails; a model that never acted is not judged
    assert not replay.go(_result(replay, {q3: 0.74, q25: 0.4, ll: 0.96}, kappa, writers))[0]
    assert replay.go(_result(replay, {q3: 0.74, q25: 0.6, ll: 0.3}, kappa, writers))[0]
    assert not replay.go(_result(replay, {q3: 0.74, q25: 0.6, ll: 0.96}, kappa, ()))[0]
    # (c) the share of P1 base findings that become Unobservable
    assert replay.unobservable_share(["unobservable"] * 61 + ["observable"] * 30) > 0.5
    assert replay.unobservable_share(["unobservable", "observable"]) == 0.5
    assert replay.unobservable_share([]) == 0.0


def test_the_replay_never_attempts_a_refutation(replay) -> None:
    result = replay.NoRefutation().refute(frozenset({"c"}), Scenario("s", 15, 12, 0), [])
    assert not result.refuted and not result.attempted
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the pinned digest and the Tell payload**

In `specdx/observe/run.py`, replace `context_digest` with:
```python
def context_digest(
    tell: Mapping[str, Any], settings: Mapping[str, Any], *, schema: int | None = None
) -> str:
    """The part of every run key that covers what interpreters are told (the Tell
    variants), how they are run (executor settings) and the code (RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION).
    schema: an older RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION, to find the runs an older version cached."""
    version = RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION if schema is None else schema
    payload = {"schema": version, "tell": tell, "executor": settings}
    text = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()
```

In `specdx/workbench.py`, add above `class Workbench`:
```python
def tell_payload(variants: TellVariants) -> dict[str, object]:
    """The Tell variants as the context digest covers them: texts only, no provenance."""
    return {"standing": list(variants.standing),
            "statements": {k: list(v) for k, v in variants.statements.items()}}
```
and in `Workbench.open` replace

```python
                              model_digest=model_digest or ollama_digests(),
                              context_digest=context_digest(tell, bench.settings))
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the replay script**

`experiments/p1b_replay.py`:
```python
"""P1b offline replay (spec v2 §6): the v2 inference on the P1 run cache, before any new run.

Read-only. It never calls Ollama, never executes a run, never opens the database and never
writes under SPECDX_WORK. It reads the run cache (runs.jsonl), the mutant packs (mutants/)
and the P1 gate's per-mutant lines (results/p1-gate-mutants.jsonl), and prints Markdown on
stdout (or writes it to --out). Only the base and the 20 development mutants are used: the
old population (qwen3:14b, qwen2.5:7b-instruct-q4_K_M, llama3.1:8b) and its 200
population runs per specification.

How cached runs are mapped to a specification: as experiments/p1_dev_diagnostics.py does,
by recomputing Runner.run's key, run_key(variant digest, steps digest, context digest,
scenario parameters, interpreter key, suffix "", model digest). The context digest is
pinned to what P1 used (RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION 2 and P1's executor settings), so later P1b
changes cannot break the mapping. A specification with other than 200 mapped runs stops
the script.

No refutation is attempted (no run may execute), so a singleton candidate types hidden
when Hidden(c) ≥ τ_H (spec §11 item 13) and a larger set is dropped. Observability, θ, κ
and S2 do not depend on refutation.

The GO criterion is pre-registered in the P1b plan (Task 7) and in
experiments/p1b-preregistration.md: on the base and on every development mutant, the model
with the highest κ does not have the lowest θ, and θ(qwen3:14b) ≥ θ(llama3.1:8b).

Usage: SPECDX_WORK=/path/to/work .venv/bin/python experiments/p1b_replay.py [--out PATH]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import dataclasses
import json
import sys
from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from tonomyx.canon.pack import CanonPack

from specdx.config import REPO_ROOT, WORK_DIR
from specdx.eval.pilot import GateConfig
from specdx.infer.act import Act, Rasch, act_rate, fit_rasch
from specdx.infer.items import ItemView, SpecLens, acted_violations, items_reading, observations
from specdx.infer.latent import Posterior, fit
from specdx.infer.scores import Diagnosis, diagnose
from specdx.interpreters.cache import run_key
from specdx.interpreters.population import committee
from specdx.mutate.operators import A1_SITES, A2_SITES
from specdx.observe.run import context_digest
from specdx.scenarios.space import boundary_domain, canon_scenarios, enumerate_scenarios
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus
from specdx.types import Behaviour, InterpreterId, RefuteResult, Scenario
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant
from specdx.workbench import Workbench, tell_payload

RUNS = WORK_DIR / "runs.jsonl"
GATE_LINES = REPO_ROOT / "results" / "p1-gate-mutants.jsonl"
GATE_CONFIG = REPO_ROOT / "experiments" / "p1-gate.json"
P1_SCHEMA = 2
P1_SETTINGS: dict[str, float | int] = {
    "temperature": 0.7, "max_steps": 16, "timeout": 180.0, "num_ctx": 16384, "search_k": 5,
    "search_reply_limit": 12000,
}
POPULATION_RUNS = 200
QWEN3, LLAMA = "qwen3:14b", "llama3.1:8b"
THETA_FLOOR = 0.5  # (b): P1 collapsed qwen3:14b to θ 0.003
UNOBSERVABLE_SHARE = 0.5  # (c)


def _never(*_: Any, **__: Any) -> Any:
    raise RuntimeError("the replay never executes a run")


class NoRefutation:
    """Every candidate stays unattempted: no run may execute here."""

    def refute(
        self, targets: frozenset[str], scenario: Scenario, behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour]
    ) -> RefuteResult:
        return RefuteResult(False, None, 0, attempted=False)


@dataclass
class Replay:
    spec: str
    lens: SpecLens
    behaviours: list[Behaviour]
    views: list[ItemView]
    acts: list[Act]
    rasch: Rasch
    posterior: Posterior
    diagnosis: Diagnosis


def load_cache(path: Path = RUNS) -> dict[str, Behaviour]:
    cache: dict[str, Behaviour] = {}
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for raw in handle:
            if raw.strip():
                b = Behaviour.from_json(raw.decode("utf-8"))
                cache[b.run_key] = b
    return cache


def model_digests(cache: dict[str, Behaviour]) -> dict[str, str]:
    seen: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    for b in cache.values():
        seen.setdefault(b.interpreter.model, set()).add(b.model_digest)
    if any(len(v) != 1 for v in seen.values()):
        raise SystemExit(f"a model has several digests in the cache: {seen}")
    return {m: next(iter(v)) for m, v in seen.items()}


def variant_of(bench: Workbench, sid: str) -> SpecVariant:
    """The SpecVariant all_mutants built for this id, rebuilt read-only from its pack."""
    if sid == "base":
        return bench.base
    pack = WORK_DIR / "mutants" / sid / bench.base.pack.name
    corpus = TellCorpus.build(CanonPack(pack), bench.pdf)
    op, index = sid.split("-")
    if op == "A1":
        corpus = corpus.without(corpus.find(A1_SITES[int(index)]))
    elif op == "A2":
        old, new = A2_SITES[int(index)]
        for sentence in corpus.find(old):
            corpus = corpus.replace(sentence, new)
    return SpecVariant(sid, pack, corpus)


def population_runs(
    bench: Workbench, variant: SpecVariant, members: Sequence[InterpreterId],
    cache: dict[str, Behaviour], digests: dict[str, str], context: str,
) -> list[Behaviour]:
    pack = CanonPack(variant.pack)
    # One scenario per parameter vector; a canon name wins over a synthesised id.
    by_signature = {s.signature(): s for s in enumerate_scenarios(boundary_domain(pack))}
    for s in canon_scenarios(pack).values():
        by_signature[s.signature()] = s
    digest = variant.digest()
    steps_digest = bench.runner._steps_digest
    out: list[Behaviour] = []
    for _, scenario in sorted(by_signature.items()):
        params = json.dumps(scenario.params(), sort_keys=True)
        for interp in members:
            key = run_key(digest, steps_digest, context, params, interp.key, "",
                          digests[interp.model])
            b = cache.get(key)
            if b is not None:
                out.append(dataclasses.replace(b, scenario=scenario, interpreter=interp))
    if len(out) != POPULATION_RUNS:
        raise SystemExit(f"{variant.name}: {len(out)} population runs mapped, "
                         f"expected {POPULATION_RUNS}; the mapping is broken")
    return out


def replay(bench: Workbench, sid: str, config: GateConfig, cache: dict[str, Behaviour],
           digests: dict[str, str], context: str) -> Replay:
    cfg = config.specdx()
    variant = variant_of(bench, sid)
    members = committee(config.models_by_family, config.variants, config.seeds)
    behaviours = population_runs(bench, variant, members, cache, digests, context)
    lens = bench.loop_context(variant, cfg).lens
    assert isinstance(lens, SpecLens)
    views: list[ItemView] = []
    acts: list[Act] = []
    violated: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {}
    for b in behaviours:
        mine = lens.views(b, lens.violations(b))
        violated[b.run_key] = acted_violations(mine)
        views.extend(mine)
        acts.extend(lens.acts(b))
    rasch = fit_rasch(acts)
    population = {m: f for f, ms in config.models_by_family.items() for m in ms}
    posterior = fit(observations(views), models=population, k_max=cfg.k_max,
                    p_single=cfg.p_single)
    diagnosis = diagnose(behaviours, violated, views, posterior, NoRefutation(), lens.diff,
                         observable=rasch.observable_items(), relevant=lens.relevant,
                         tau=cfg.tau, rng_seed=cfg.rng_seed)
    return Replay(sid, lens, behaviours, views, acts, rasch, posterior, diagnosis)


def go(r: Replay) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """Criteria (a) and (b) on one specification: stage 1 puts the model that never writes
    lowest in κ, and no model with acted evidence collapses below THETA_FLOOR."""
    theta, kappa = r.posterior.theta, r.rasch.kappa
    first = LLAMA in kappa and all(kappa[LLAMA] < kappa[m] for m in kappa if m != LLAMA)
    acted = sorted({a.model for a in r.acts if a.acted})
    low = [m for m in acted if theta.get(m, 0.0) < THETA_FLOOR]
    second = bool(acted) and not low
    why = (f"κ: {', '.join(f'{m} {kappa[m]:.3f}' for m in sorted(kappa))}; "
           f"acting models below θ {THETA_FLOOR}: {', '.join(low) or 'none'}")
    return first and second, why


def p1_status(r: Replay, defect: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """'observable' or 'unobservable' for one P1 base finding under v2's observability."""
    observable = r.rasch.observable_items()
    if defect["type"] in ("ambiguity", "weak"):
        return "observable" if defect["note"] in observable else "unobservable"
    for sid in defect["scenarios"]:
        if all(any(i in observable for i in items_reading(c, sid, r.lens.relevant))
               for c in defect["location"]):
            return "observable"
    return "unobservable"


def unobservable_share(statuses: Sequence[str]) -> float:
    """Criterion (c): the share of P1 base findings v2 reports as Unobservable."""
    return sum(s == "unobservable" for s in statuses) / len(statuses) if statuses else 0.0


def verdict(replays: list[Replay], base_line: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """GO: (a) and (b) on every specification, and (c) on the base."""
    statuses = [p1_status(replays[0], d) for d in base_line["defects"]["specdx"]]
    return (all(go(r)[0] for r in replays)
            and unobservable_share(statuses) >= UNOBSERVABLE_SHARE)


def _f(x: float | None) -> str:
    return "–" if x is None else f"{x:.3f}"


def render(replays: list[Replay], base_line: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    out: list[str] = ["<!-- generated by experiments/p1b_replay.py -->", "",
                      "# P1b offline replay: v2 inference on the P1 cache", ""]
    verdicts = {r.spec: go(r) for r in replays}
    passed = verdict(replays, base_line)
    share = unobservable_share([p1_status(replays[0], d)
                                for d in base_line["defects"]["specdx"]])
    out += [f"## Verdict: {'GO' if passed else 'NO-GO'}", "",
            "Pre-registered mechanism check (author's ruling, 2026-10-12). "
            f"(a) {LLAMA}, which never writes, has the lowest κ on every specification. "
            f"(b) Every model with an acted behaviour has θ ≥ {THETA_FLOOR}. "
            f"(c) At least {UNOBSERVABLE_SHARE:.0%} of the P1 base findings become "
            f"Unobservable: here {share:.1%}.", "",
            "| specification | (a) and (b) hold | detail |", "|---|---|---|"]
    out += [f"| {sid} | {'yes' if ok else 'no'} | {why} |" for sid, (ok, why) in
            verdicts.items()]
    base = replays[0]
    rates = act_rate(base.acts)
    out += ["", "## Base: stage 1 and stage 2 per model", "",
            "| model | act rate | κ | θ (v2) |", "|---|---:|---:|---:|"]
    for m in sorted(base.posterior.theta):
        out.append(f"| {m} | {rates.get(m, 0.0):.3f} | {_f(base.rasch.kappa.get(m))} | "
                   f"{base.posterior.theta[m]:.3f} |")
    statuses = Counter((d["type"], p1_status(base, d)) for d in base_line["defects"]["specdx"])
    total_unobs = sum(n for (_, s), n in statuses.items() if s == "unobservable")
    out += ["", "## P1 base findings under v2 observability", "",
            f"{total_unobs} of {len(base_line['defects']['specdx'])} P1 base findings become "
            "Unobservable.", "", "| P1 type | observable | unobservable |", "|---|---:|---:|"]
    for t in ("contradiction", "hidden", "ambiguity", "weak"):
        out.append(f"| {t} | {statuses[(t, 'observable')]} | {statuses[(t, 'unobservable')]} |")
    out += ["", "## v2 diagnosis without refutation, per specification", "",
            "| specification | observable items | typed (C/H/A/W) | unobservable | S2 tested | "
            "S2 significant |", "|---|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|"]
    for r in replays:
        kinds = Counter(d.type for d in r.diagnosis.defects)
        tested = [s for s in r.diagnosis.signals.values() if s.p is not None]
        out.append(
            f"| {r.spec} | {len(r.rasch.observable_items())} of {len(r.rasch.delta)} | "
            f"{kinds['contradiction']}/{kinds['hidden']}/{kinds['ambiguity']}/{kinds['weak']} | "
            f"{len(r.diagnosis.unobservable)} | {len(tested)} | "
            f"{sum(s.significant for s in tested)} |")
    out += ["", "## Base: per item", "",
            "| item | δ | O_s | acted | observable | S1 | S2 | p | BH |",
            "|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---|"]
    for item in sorted(base.rasch.delta):
        sig = base.diagnosis.signals.get(item)
        out.append(
            f"| {item} | {base.rasch.delta[item]:.3f} | {base.rasch.observability[item]:.3f} | "
            f"{base.rasch.acted[item]} | {'yes' if base.rasch.observable(item) else 'no'} | "
            f"{_f(sig.s1 if sig else None)} | {_f(sig.s2 if sig else None)} | "
            f"{_f(sig.p if sig else None)} | {'yes' if sig and sig.significant else ''} |")
    out += ["", "## S2 p-values on the mutants (tested items)", "",
            "| specification | item | S2 | p | BH |", "|---|---|---:|---:|---|"]
    for r in replays[1:]:
        for item, s in sorted(r.diagnosis.signals.items()):
            if s.p is not None:
                out.append(f"| {r.spec} | {item} | {_f(s.s2)} | {_f(s.p)} | "
                           f"{'yes' if s.significant else ''} |")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, help="write the Markdown here instead of stdout")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    cache = load_cache()
    digests = model_digests(cache)
    bench = Workbench.open(factory=_never, model_digest=digests.__getitem__)
    context = context_digest(tell_payload(bench.variants), P1_SETTINGS, schema=P1_SCHEMA)
    config = GateConfig.load(GATE_CONFIG)
    lines = [json.loads(x) for x in GATE_LINES.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    ids = ["base"] + [ln["mutant"] for ln in lines if not ln.get("base")]
    replays = [replay(bench, sid, config, cache, digests, context) for sid in ids]
    text = render(replays, lines[0])
    if args.out:
        args.out.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(text)
    return 0 if verdict(replays, lines[0]) else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

---

### Task 8: The neutral execution harness, and RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION 3

Spec v2 §2.1. The harness sits at the executor's chat boundary; `vendor/` stays untouched. Its texts are fixed constants with no business value or rule.
- **Text calls.** When a reply has no `tool_calls`, every valid tool call written in its text runs like a native call and is marked `parsed_from_text` (planning resolution 8).
- **Nudge.** When a step whose token drafts tables ends with a final answer and has made no successful write call, the harness appends `NUDGE`, at most twice per step.
- **Counts.** Each step's counts (nudges, parsed calls, tool calls, valid calls) ride in the envelope's `usage["harness"]`. The flow and the Runner record them on the `Behaviour`, with the specification's name.
- **Cache keys.** Interpreters now see something new, so `RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION` becomes 3. The nudge limit joins the executor settings in the context digest.

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/interpreters/harness.py`
- Modify:
  - `specdx/interpreters/executor.py` (import, `interpreter_settings`, `_counted`, constructor, `run`);
  - `specdx/orchestrate/flow.py` (`CaseRun.harness`, `run_case`);
  - `specdx/types.py` (`Behaviour.harness`, `Behaviour.spec`, `from_json`);
  - `specdx/observe/run.py` (`RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION`, the two `Behaviour(...)` constructions);
  - `specdx/workbench.py` (`llm_factory` passes `max_nudges`).
- Test: `tests/interpreters/test_harness.py` (new), `tests/interpreters/test_executor.py`, `tests/observe/test_run.py`, `tests/test_types.py`, `tests/test_workbench.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `tonomyx.de.executor._arguments`, which is vendored private reuse (P1 ledger Ruling 3); Task 2's `drafting_steps` (tests only).
- Produces (used by Tasks 13, 15, 17):
  - in `specdx.interpreters.harness`: `NUDGE: str`, `MAX_NUDGES = 2`, `READ_ONLY_TOOLS`, `tool_parameters(schemas) -> dict[str, Parameters]`, `validate_arguments(parameters, args) -> bool`, `valid_call(call, tools) -> bool`, `parse_text_calls(content, tools) -> list[dict]` and `wrote(calls) -> bool`;
  - `SpecInterpreterExecutor(..., max_nudges: int | None = None)`;
  - `envelope.usage["harness"] == {"nudges", "parsed_from_text", "tool_calls", "valid_calls"}`;
  - `CaseRun.harness: dict[str, dict[str, int]]`;
  - `Behaviour.harness: dict[str, dict[str, int]]` (step id → counts, default `{}`) and `Behaviour.spec: str` (default `""`);
  - `RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION = 3`;
  - `interpreter_settings()["nudges"]`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the harness**

`specdx/interpreters/harness.py`:
```python
"""The neutral execution harness (spec v2 §2.1), at the executor's chat boundary.

Its texts are fixed constants, the same for every specification, and carry no business
value or rule. It does two things:

- a tool call the model wrote as text (a JSON object naming one of the step's tools,
  anywhere in its reply, fenced or in <tool_call> tags) is executed like a native call,
  provided its arguments validate against the tool's schema;
- a model that ends a step that drafts tables without any successful write call is asked,
  at most MAX_NUDGES times, to act or to confirm that it is done.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any

from tonomyx.de.executor import _arguments

NUDGE = (
    "Bạn chưa thực hiện hành động nào bằng công cụ. Nếu còn việc phải làm, hãy dùng công cụ; "
    "nếu thật sự đã xong, hãy trả lời lại 'xong'."
)
MAX_NUDGES = 2  # spec v2 §3.10
# Tools that change nothing: a step that only called these has not acted.
READ_ONLY_TOOLS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"read_records", "search_kb", "search_spec"})
_NAME_KEYS = ("name", "function")
_ARGUMENT_KEYS = ("arguments", "parameters")
_JSON_TYPES: dict[str, tuple[type, ...]] = {
    "string": (str,), "integer": (int,), "number": (int, float), "boolean": (bool,),
    "object": (dict,), "array": (list,),
}

Parameters = Mapping[str, Any]


def tool_parameters(schemas: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Parameters]:
    """Tool name → its JSON parameter schema, from the schemas offered to the model."""
    return {str(s["function"]["name"]): s["function"].get("parameters", {}) for s in schemas}


def validate_arguments(parameters: Parameters, args: Any) -> bool:
    """Whether args fit the schema: an object with every required key, no unknown key, and
    each value of its declared JSON type (null only where the key is optional)."""
    if not isinstance(args, dict):
        return False
    properties = parameters.get("properties") or {}
    required = set(parameters.get("required") or [])
    if not required <= set(args):
        return False
    for name, value in args.items():
        if name not in properties:
            return False
        expected = properties[name].get("type")
        if value is None:
            if name in required:
                return False
            continue
        kinds = _JSON_TYPES.get(str(expected)) if expected else None
        if kinds is None:
            continue
        if isinstance(value, bool) and expected in ("integer", "number"):
            return False
        if not isinstance(value, kinds):
            return False
    return True


def valid_call(call: Mapping[str, Any], tools: Mapping[str, Parameters]) -> bool:
    """A native call names one of the step's tools with arguments that validate."""
    function = call.get("function") or {}
    name = str(function.get("name", ""))
    if name not in tools:
        return False
    try:
        args = _arguments(function.get("arguments"))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return False
    return validate_arguments(tools[name], args)


def _as_call(obj: Any, tools: Mapping[str, Parameters]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if not isinstance(obj, dict):
        return None
    name_key = next((k for k in _NAME_KEYS if k in obj), None)
    args_key = next((k for k in _ARGUMENT_KEYS if k in obj), None)
    if name_key is None or args_key is None or not isinstance(obj[name_key], str):
        return None
    call = {"function": {"name": obj[name_key], "arguments": obj[args_key]}}
    if not valid_call(call, tools):
        return None
    call["function"]["arguments"] = _arguments(obj[args_key])
    return call


def parse_text_calls(content: str, tools: Mapping[str, Parameters]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """The valid tool calls written in a reply's text, in order, shaped as native calls.
    Every top-level JSON object in the text is a candidate, so fences, <tool_call> tags and
    surrounding prose are all accepted."""
    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
    found: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    start = content.find("{")
    while start != -1:
        try:
            obj, end = decoder.raw_decode(content, start)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            start = content.find("{", start + 1)
            continue
        call = _as_call(obj, tools)
        if call is not None:
            found.append(call)
        start = content.find("{", end)
    return found


def wrote(calls: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> bool:
    """Whether any call of the step succeeded at changing something."""
    return any(c.get("outcome") == "ok" and c.get("name") not in READ_ONLY_TOOLS for c in calls)
```

2. Make `interpreter_settings()` return:

```python
            "num_ctx": NUM_CTX, "search_k": SEARCH_K, "search_reply_limit": SEARCH_REPLY_LIMIT,
```

3. Add above `class SpecInterpreterExecutor`:
```python
def _counted(envelope: ResultEnvelope, counts: dict[str, int]) -> ResultEnvelope:
    """The harness's per-step counts ride in the envelope's usage, for the Behaviour."""
    return envelope.model_copy(update={"usage": {**envelope.usage, "harness": dict(counts)}})
```
4. Append to the class docstring: `Every step runs through the neutral harness (spec v2 §2.1): tool calls written as text are executed when valid, and a drafting step that wrote nothing is nudged.`
5. Add the keyword parameter `max_nudges: int | None = None` after `sleep` in `__init__`. Add the last line of the attribute block: `self.max_nudges = harness.MAX_NUDGES if max_nudges is None else max_nudges`.
6. In `run`, replace everything from `record = _Record()` to the end of the method with:
```python
        record = _Record()
        tools = harness.tool_parameters(schemas)
        drafts = bool(ctx.token.draft)
        counts = {"nudges": 0, "parsed_from_text": 0, "tool_calls": 0, "valid_calls": 0}
        try:
            with httpx.Client(transport=self.transport, timeout=self.timeout) as client:
                for step in range(1, self.max_steps + 1):
                    reply = self._chat(client, messages, schemas)
                    messages.append(reply)
                    calls = reply.get("tool_calls") or []
                    for call in calls:
                        counts["tool_calls"] += 1
                        counts["valid_calls"] += harness.valid_call(call, tools)
                        messages.append(self._execute(call, ctx, record))
                    if calls:
                        continue
                    written = harness.parse_text_calls(_plain(reply), tools)
                    for call in written:
                        counts["tool_calls"] += 1
                        counts["valid_calls"] += 1
                        counts["parsed_from_text"] += 1
                        messages.append(self._execute(call, ctx, record))
                        record.calls[-1]["parsed_from_text"] = True
                    if written:
                        continue
                    if (drafts and counts["nudges"] < self.max_nudges
                            and not harness.wrote(record.calls)):
                        counts["nudges"] += 1
                        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": harness.NUDGE})
                        continue
                    return _counted(record.finish(task, self.model, step, _plain(reply)), counts)
        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
            return _counted(ResultEnvelope(
                task_id=task.task_id, case_id=task.case_id, status=ResultStatus.ERROR,
                next_hint=f"ollama call failed: {_describe(exc)}",
            ), counts)
        return _counted(record.envelope(
            task, self.model, self.max_steps, ResultStatus.ERROR,
            f"{self.model} did not finish within {self.max_steps} steps",
        ), counts)
```

### Task 9: T-guided scenario synthesis

Spec v2 §3.7. Extraction runs on the Tell corpus of the specification under diagnosis, so a mutant uses its own T.
- **Literal.** A literal is a number, with an optional unit (`%`, `phần trăm`, `đơn vị`, `cái`, `chiếc`), within 40 characters of a comparison keyword in the same sentence.
- **Binding.** A percentage binds to `discount_pct`, and a number near a quantity word (số lượng, đơn vị, units, quantity) binds to `received`. Anything else is dropped.
- **Domain.** Each bound literal v adds v − 1, v and v + 1 to the parameter's domain in `boundary_domain`.
- **T-boundary scenarios.** At most 12 per specification, each varying one parameter of the anchor `grn12`, in literal order (source, chunk, sentence, offset).
- **Context.** The loop context carries the T-boundary scenarios and the literals; Task 10 queues and reports them. Planning resolution 9 applies.

On the base specification the literals are 5% (four sentences) and 8% (`KB:KB-0003:04`). The T-boundary scenarios are `o15-r12-d4-g1` … `o15-r12-d9-g1`, and the candidate set grows from 170 to 238 scenarios.

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/scenarios/tguided.py`
- Modify:
  - `specdx/scenarios/space.py` (`boundary_domain` and its import);
  - `specdx/active/loop.py` (`LoopContext` fields, import);
  - `specdx/workbench.py` (`loop_context`, import).
- Test: `tests/scenarios/test_tguided.py` (new), `tests/test_workbench.py` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TellCorpus` (`.chunks`, `Chunk.source`, `Chunk.id`, `Sentence.id`, `Sentence.text`), `ANCHOR`, `Scenario`.
- Produces (used by Tasks 10, 17):
  - `WINDOW = 40`, `MAX_TBOUNDARY = 12`;
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) TLiteral(parameter, value, unit, keyword, source, chunk, sentence, offset)`, with `.as_dict()` and `.bound -> tuple[str, int]`;
  - `extract_literals(corpus: TellCorpus) -> list[TLiteral]`;
  - `tboundary_scenarios(canon: Mapping[str, Scenario], literals: Iterable[TLiteral], limit: int = MAX_TBOUNDARY) -> list[Scenario]`;
  - `boundary_domain(pack, bound: Iterable[tuple[str, int]] = ()) -> dict[str, list[int]]`;
  - `LoopContext(..., localizer=None, tboundary: list[Scenario] = [], literals: list[TLiteral] = [])`.

```python
from specdx.spec.tell import Chunk, Sentence, TellCorpus


def _corpus(*texts: str, source: str = "KB") -> TellCorpus:
    return TellCorpus([Chunk("KB:T", source, tuple(Sentence(f"KB:T:{i:02d}", t)
                                                   for i, t in enumerate(texts)))])

@pytest.mark.parametrize(("text", "expected"), [

])

    literals = [_literal("discount_pct", v, i) for i, v in enumerate(range(10, 60, 3))]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the extraction**

`specdx/scenarios/tguided.py`:
```python
"""T-guided scenario synthesis (spec v2 §3.7): place scenarios at the thresholds T states.

Extraction runs on the Tell corpus of the specification under diagnosis, so a mutant uses
its own T. A literal is a number, with an optional unit, within 40 characters of a
comparison keyword in the same sentence. A percentage binds to discount_pct; a number
near a quantity word binds to received; anything else is dropped. Every bound literal v
adds v - 1, v and v + 1 to its parameter's domain, and T-boundary scenarios vary one
parameter of the anchor scenario to those values.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import unicodedata
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass

from specdx.scenarios.space import ANCHOR
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus
from specdx.types import Scenario

WINDOW = 40  # characters between a number and a keyword or quantity word
MAX_TBOUNDARY = 12  # spec v2 §3.10
VIETNAMESE_KEYWORDS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "tối đa", "tối thiểu", "không vượt quá", "vượt quá", "vượt", "trên", "dưới", "ít nhất",
    "nhiều nhất", "trần", "ngưỡng", "lớn hơn", "nhỏ hơn",
)
ENGLISH_KEYWORDS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "at most", "at least", "exceed", "above", "below", "maximum", "minimum", "cap",
    "threshold", "more than", "less than",
)
QUANTITY_WORDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("số lượng", "đơn vị", "units", "quantity")
PERCENT_UNITS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"%", "phần trăm"})
_NUMBER = re.compile(
    r"(?<![\w.,-])(\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)(?![\w-])(\s*(?:%|phần trăm|đơn vị|cái|chiếc)(?!\w))?",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)


def _phrase(words: Iterable[str], suffix: str = "") -> re.Pattern[str]:
    alternatives = "|".join(re.escape(w) for w in sorted(words, key=len, reverse=True))
    return re.compile(rf"(?<!\w)(?:{alternatives}){suffix}(?!\w)", re.IGNORECASE)


# English keywords also match their inflections (exceeds, exceeded, exceeding).
_KEYWORDS = (_phrase(VIETNAMESE_KEYWORDS), _phrase(ENGLISH_KEYWORDS, "(?:s|es|ed|ing)?"))
_QUANTITY = _phrase(QUANTITY_WORDS)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TLiteral:
    """A numeric threshold found in T, bound to a scenario parameter."""

    parameter: str
    value: int
    unit: str
    keyword: str
    source: str
    chunk: str
    sentence: str
    offset: int

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        return asdict(self)

    @property
    def bound(self) -> tuple[str, int]:
        return self.parameter, self.value


def _near(span: tuple[int, int], spans: Iterable[tuple[int, int, str]]) -> str | None:
    """The first marker within WINDOW characters of span, if any."""
    start, end = span
    for s, e, text in spans:
        gap = start - e if e <= start else s - end if s >= end else 0
        if gap <= WINDOW:
            return text
    return None


def _literals_in(text: str) -> list[tuple[str, int, str, str, int]]:
    """(parameter, value, unit, keyword, offset) for every bound literal of one sentence."""
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text)
    keywords = [(m.start(), m.end(), m.group(0).lower()) for p in _KEYWORDS
                for m in p.finditer(text)]
    quantities = [(m.start(), m.end(), m.group(0).lower()) for m in _QUANTITY.finditer(text)]
    found = []
    for m in _NUMBER.finditer(text):
        raw, unit = m.group(1), (m.group(2) or "").strip().lower()
        if not raw.isdigit():
            continue  # a decimal binds to no integer parameter
        span = (m.start(1), m.end())
        keyword = _near(span, sorted(keywords))
        if keyword is None:
            continue
        if unit in PERCENT_UNITS:
            parameter = "discount_pct"
        elif _near(span, sorted(quantities)) is not None:
            parameter = "received"
        else:
            continue
        found.append((parameter, int(raw), unit, keyword, m.start(1)))
    return found


def extract_literals(corpus: TellCorpus) -> list[TLiteral]:
    """Every bound literal of T, ordered by source, chunk, sentence and offset."""
    out = [
        TLiteral(parameter, value, unit, keyword, chunk.source, chunk.id, sentence.id, offset)
        for chunk in corpus.chunks for sentence in chunk.sentences
        for parameter, value, unit, keyword, offset in _literals_in(sentence.text)
    ]
    return sorted(out, key=lambda x: (x.source, x.chunk, x.sentence, x.offset))


def _physical(s: Scenario) -> bool:
    return (s.grn_count <= s.received <= s.ordered + 1) and 0 <= s.discount_pct <= 100


def tboundary_scenarios(
    canon: Mapping[str, Scenario], literals: Iterable[TLiteral], limit: int = MAX_TBOUNDARY
) -> list[Scenario]:
    """Scenarios that move one parameter of the anchor to v - 1, v or v + 1 of a literal,
    in literal order, skipping canon points and repeats, at most `limit` of them."""
    anchor = canon[ANCHOR]
    taken = {s.signature() for s in canon.values()}
    out: list[Scenario] = []
    for lit in literals:
        for value in (lit.value - 1, lit.value, lit.value + 1):
            params = anchor.params() | {lit.parameter: value}
            o, r, d, g = (params[k] for k in ("ordered", "received", "discount_pct",
                                              "grn_count"))
            s = Scenario(f"o{o}-r{r}-d{d}-g{g}", o, r, d, g)
            if _physical(s) and s.signature() not in taken:
                taken.add(s.signature())
                out.append(s)
                if len(out) == limit:
                    return out
    return out
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Widen the domain and carry the result in the loop context**

In `specdx/scenarios/space.py`, add `from collections.abc import Iterable` after `import itertools`, and replace `boundary_domain` with:
```python
def boundary_domain(
    pack: CanonPack, bound: Iterable[tuple[str, int]] = ()
) -> dict[str, list[int]]:
    """Canon values plus the values on either side of every literal that matters: the
    lifted clauses' literals, and each (parameter, value) bound from T (spec v2 §3.7).
    enumerate_scenarios keeps only the physical combinations; discounts stay in 0..100."""
    canon = canon_scenarios(pack)
    anchor = canon[ANCHOR]
    ordered = anchor.ordered
    cap = discount_cap_pct(pack)
    domain = {
        "ordered": {ordered - 1, ordered, ordered + 1},
        "received": (
            {s.received for s in canon.values()}
            | {ordered - 1, ordered, ordered + 1}
            | {anchor.received - 1, anchor.received + 1}
        ),
        "discount_pct": {s.discount_pct for s in canon.values()} | {cap - 1, cap, cap + 1},
        "grn_count": {1, 2},
    }
    for parameter, value in bound:
        for v in (value - 1, value, value + 1):
            if v >= 0 and (parameter != "discount_pct" or v <= 100):
                domain[parameter].add(v)
    return {k: sorted(v) for k, v in domain.items()}
```

### Task 10: The loop: queue, report fields, and the new ddmin oracle

Spec v2 §3.8 and §3.6's oracle.
- **Queue.** The canon scenarios, then the T-boundary scenarios, then the Thompson-sampling bandit. A queued scenario whose signature was already visited is skipped.
- **Report.** It gains:
  - κ per model;
  - per item δ, Oₛ, the acted count and observability, plus S1, S2, p and BH significance for each observable item;
  - the Unobservable list;
  - the T literals.
- **ddmin oracle.** Each test re-runs one behaviour per (competent model, Tell variant) at seed 1, from `oracle_interpreters`. It passes while two acted re-runs at the ambiguity's item differ on the ambiguity's fields.
- **Unchanged.** Budgets, escalation, the differential, and the ordering of base locations (v1 §11 item 13).

**Files:**
- Modify: `specdx/active/loop.py` (whole file below)
- Modify: `specdx/workbench.py` (imports and the localizer's `split_persists`)
- Test: `tests/active/test_loop.py` (imports, appended tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 6: `Diagnosis`, `diagnose(...) -> Diagnosis` and `ItemSignal` fields;
  - Task 2: `Rasch`, `fit_rasch`;
  - Task 5: `COMPETENT`;
  - Task 9: `TLiteral`, `LoopContext.tboundary`, `LoopContext.literals`;
  - Task 3: `ItemView.acted`, `ItemView.projection`.
- Produces (used by Tasks 12, 14, 17, 18):
  - `Report.kappa: dict[str, float]`;
  - `Report.items: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]`, with keys `delta`, `observability`, `acted`, `observable`, plus `s1`, `s2`, `p`, `significant` on observable items;
  - `Report.unobservable: list[Defect]` and `Report.literals: list[TLiteral]`;
  - `item_table(rasch, diagnosis)`;
  - `oracle_interpreters(models_by_family, theta, variants, competent=COMPETENT) -> list[InterpreterId]`;
  - `Report.to_json()` adds the keys `unobservable`, `kappa`, `items` and `literals`.
  - The `Localizer` signature is unchanged; its interpreters are now `oracle_interpreters(...)`.

```python
    literal = TLiteral("discount_pct", 5, "%", "tối đa", "KB", "KB:KB-0003", "KB:KB-0003:03", 9)
    ctx = dataclasses.replace(_ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), literals=[literal])
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, ctx, SpecDxConfig(MODELS, budget=100, synthesize=False))
    assert set(report.kappa) == {"q1", "q2", "l1"}
    item = report.items["grn12|step"]
    assert item["observable"] and {"delta", "observability", "acted", "s1"} <= set(item)
    out = json.loads(report.to_json())
    assert out["literals"] == [literal.as_dict()] and out["unobservable"] == []
    assert set(out["items"]) == set(report.items) and out["kappa"] == report.kappa


class IdleLens(FakeLens):
    """No one acts at the step under test: it is never observable."""

    def views(self, b, violated):
        out = super().views(b, violated)
        return [dataclasses.replace(v, acted=not v.item.endswith("|step")) for v in out]


def test_a_clause_no_observable_item_reads_is_reported_unobservable_not_typed() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan), lens=IdleLens()),
                        SpecDxConfig(MODELS, budget=100, synthesize=False))
    assert not [d for d in report.defects if "c1" in d.location]
    assert report.items["grn12|step"]["observable"] is False


def test_the_ddmin_oracle_reruns_each_competent_model_under_each_variant() -> None:
    seen = []

    def localiser(v, d, interps, budget):
        seen.append(list(interps))
        return ("A:01",)

    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan), localiser),
                        SpecDxConfig(MODELS, variants=(0, 1), budget=400, synthesize=False))
    assert [d.location for d in report.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"] == [("A:01",)]
    competent = [m for m in ("q1", "q2", "l1") if report.theta[m] >= 0.5]
    assert sorted(competent) == ["l1", "q1"]
    assert {(i.model, i.variant, i.seed) for i in seen[0]} == {
        (m, v, 1) for m in competent for v in (0, 1)}


def test_oracle_interpreters_are_the_competent_models_at_seed_one() -> None:
    interps = oracle_interpreters({"a": ("m1", "m2"), "b": ("m3",)},
                                  {"m1": 0.6, "m2": 0.4, "m3": 0.9}, (0, 2))
    assert [(i.model, i.family, i.variant, i.seed) for i in interps] == [
        ("m3", "b", 0, 1), ("m3", "b", 2, 1), ("m1", "a", 0, 1), ("m1", "a", 2, 1)]


class SilentLens(FakeLens):
    """A population that acts nowhere: every step fails or writes nothing."""

    def views(self, b, violated):
        return [dataclasses.replace(v, acted=False) for v in super().views(b, violated)]


def test_a_population_that_never_acts_types_nothing_and_still_reports() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan), lens=SilentLens()),
                        SpecDxConfig(MODELS, budget=100, synthesize=False))
    assert report.defects == [] and report.runs > 0
    assert report.theta == {"q1": 0.75, "q2": 0.75, "l1": 0.75}  # the prior's mode, n_m = 0
    assert not any(row["observable"] for row in report.items.values())
    assert json.loads(report.to_json())["defects"] == []
```

`specdx/active/loop.py` (whole file):
```python
"""The SpecDx main loop (spec §3.8)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
import random
from collections.abc import Callable, Hashable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Protocol

from specdx.active.budget import RunBudget
from specdx.active.refute import SYMBOLIC_WITNESS
from specdx.active.search import CellBandit, information_gain, should_escalate
from specdx.infer.act import Act, Rasch, fit_rasch
from specdx.infer.items import ItemView, acted_violations, observations
from specdx.infer.latent import Posterior, fit
from specdx.infer.scores import (
    Diagnosis,
    ambiguity_key,
    diagnose,
    identity,
    merge_defects,
)
from specdx.infer.sensitivity import COMPETENT
from specdx.interpreters.population import committee, pilot
from specdx.scenarios.tguided import TLiteral
from specdx.spec.columns import Projection
from specdx.types import Behaviour, Defect, InterpreterId, Refuter, RefuteResult, Scenario
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant


class RunnerLike(Protocol):
    executed: int

    def run(
        self, variant: SpecVariant, scenario: Scenario, interp: InterpreterId, suffix: str = "",
        *, kind: str = "population",
    ) -> Behaviour: ...


class LensLike(Protocol):
    relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]]

    def violations(self, behaviour: Behaviour) -> frozenset[str]: ...

    def views(self, behaviour: Behaviour, violated: frozenset[str]) -> list[ItemView]: ...

    def acts(self, behaviour: Behaviour) -> list[Act]: ...

    def diff(self, a: Projection, b: Projection) -> frozenset[str]: ...


# The localised sentence ids, or None when the ambiguity stays unlocalised.
Localizer = Callable[
    [SpecVariant, Defect, Sequence[InterpreterId], RunBudget], tuple[str, ...] | None
]
AmbiguityKey = tuple[str, str, tuple[str, ...]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SpecDxConfig:
    models_by_family: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]
    variants: tuple[int, ...] = (0,)
    seeds: tuple[int, ...] = (1, 2, 3)
    budget: int = 300
    tau: float = 0.05
    eps: float = 0.01
    ambig_threshold: float = 0.5
    weak_threshold: float = 0.5
    k_max: int = 6
    p_single: float = 0.9
    rng_seed: int = 20261006
    synthesize: bool = True
    localize: bool = True
    localize_tests: int = 40
    # Runs requested per specification, cached or not (§11): directed probes for
    # refutation, and ddmin runs for localisation, each under its own cap.
    probe_budget: int = 100
    localize_budget: int = 80


@dataclass
class LoopContext:
    """tboundary: the T-boundary scenarios, queued after the canon ones; literals: the T
    literals they come from, reported per specification (spec v2 §3.7)."""

    runner: RunnerLike
    lens: LensLike
    canon: dict[str, Scenario]
    candidates: list[Scenario]
    cap_pct: int
    refuter_factory: Callable[[Sequence[InterpreterId], RunBudget], Refuter]
    localizer: Localizer | None = None
    tboundary: list[Scenario] = field(default_factory=list)
    literals: list[TLiteral] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class Report:
    """runs counts population runs; probe_runs and ddmin_runs the requested runs charged to
    the probe and localisation budgets. ambiguity_keys are the ambiguities found, before
    the differential and localisation; ambiguity_dropped those the differential removed.
    The v2 additions (spec v2 §3.8): κ per model; per item δ, O_s, whether it is
    observable, and its ambiguity signals S1, S2, p; the Unobservable list; the T literals."""

    variant: str
    defects: list[Defect]
    runs: int
    executed: int
    scenarios: list[str]
    theta: dict[str, float]
    behaviours: list[Behaviour] = field(default_factory=list, repr=False)
    probe_runs: int = 0
    ddmin_runs: int = 0
    ambiguity_keys: frozenset[AmbiguityKey] = frozenset()
    ambiguity_dropped: frozenset[AmbiguityKey] = frozenset()  # base noise, removed
    kappa: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    items: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    unobservable: list[Defect] = field(default_factory=list)
    literals: list[TLiteral] = field(default_factory=list)

    def to_json(self) -> str:
        return json.dumps(
            {"variant": self.variant, "defects": [asdict(d) for d in self.defects],
             "unobservable": [asdict(d) for d in self.unobservable],
             "runs": self.runs, "probe_runs": self.probe_runs, "ddmin_runs": self.ddmin_runs,
             "executed": self.executed, "scenarios": self.scenarios, "theta": self.theta,
             "kappa": self.kappa, "items": self.items,
             "literals": [x.as_dict() for x in self.literals]},
            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2,
        )


def item_table(rasch: Rasch, diagnosis: Diagnosis) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Per item: stage 1 (δ, O_s, acted behaviours, observable) and, when observable, the
    ambiguity signals (S1, S2 and its p-value, BH significance)."""
    out: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for item in sorted(rasch.delta):
        row: dict[str, Any] = {"delta": rasch.delta[item],
                               "observability": rasch.observability[item],
                               "acted": rasch.acted[item], "observable": rasch.observable(item)}
        signal = diagnosis.signals.get(item)
        if signal is not None:
            row |= {"s1": signal.s1, "s2": signal.s2, "p": signal.p,
                    "significant": signal.significant}
        out[item] = row
    return out


class _Memoised:
    """One refutation per scenario and target set for the whole specification: the loop
    builds a fresh refuter every iteration, and repeated requests would drain the budget."""

    def __init__(self, refuter: Refuter, memo: dict) -> None:
        self.refuter, self.memo = refuter, memo

    def refute(
        self, targets: frozenset[str], scenario: Scenario, behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour]
    ) -> RefuteResult:
        key = (scenario.signature(), targets)
        if key not in self.memo:
            self.memo[key] = self.refuter.refute(targets, scenario, behaviours)
        result: RefuteResult = self.memo[key]
        return result


def ranked_interpreters(
    models_by_family: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]],
    theta: dict[str, float],
    variants: tuple[int, ...],
) -> list[InterpreterId]:
    """Every model once, most competent first: who refutes and who localises."""
    models = [(m, f) for f, ms in models_by_family.items() for m in ms]
    models.sort(key=lambda mf: -theta.get(mf[0], 0.0))
    return [InterpreterId(m, f, variants[0], 1) for m, f in models]


def oracle_interpreters(
    models_by_family: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]],
    theta: dict[str, float],
    variants: tuple[int, ...],
    competent: float = COMPETENT,
) -> list[InterpreterId]:
    """The ddmin oracle's re-runs (spec v2 §3.6): one per competent model and Tell variant,
    at seed 1, most competent model first."""
    models = [(m, f) for f, ms in models_by_family.items() for m in ms
              if theta.get(m, 0.0) >= competent]
    models.sort(key=lambda mf: -theta.get(mf[0], 0.0))
    return [InterpreterId(m, f, v, 1) for m, f in models for v in variants]


def run_specdx(
    variant: SpecVariant, ctx: LoopContext, config: SpecDxConfig, *, base: Report | None = None
) -> Report:
    """base: the base specification's report. Ambiguities it already holds (same step and
    fields) are dropped before localisation, so only the mutant's own are localised, and
    candidates at locations it already holds are refuted last (§11)."""
    rng = random.Random(config.rng_seed)
    budget = RunBudget(config.probe_budget, config.localize_budget)
    refutations: dict = {}
    bandit = CellBandit(ctx.cap_pct)
    # Canon scenarios, then T-boundary scenarios, then the bandit (spec v2 §3.8).
    queue = [ctx.canon[name] for name in sorted(ctx.canon)] + list(ctx.tboundary)
    visited: set[tuple[int, ...]] = set()
    behaviours: list[Behaviour] = []
    violated: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {}
    views: list[ItemView] = []
    acts: list[Act] = []
    seen_sets: set[frozenset[str]] = set()
    posterior = Posterior({}, {}, {})
    rasch = fit_rasch([])
    diagnosis = Diagnosis([])
    defects: list[Defect] = []
    previous: dict[str, float] = {}
    order: list[str] = []
    runs = 0
    executed_before = ctx.runner.executed
    members = committee(config.models_by_family, config.variants, config.seeds)
    population = {m: f for f, ms in config.models_by_family.items() for m in ms}
    # Base locations whose candidates spent probes, or would: contradictions, and hidden
    # findings refuted by a probe run or never attempted. A base finding that symbolic
    # repair refuted cost no probe; if it needs one on a mutant, the mutant changed it.
    known = (frozenset(d.location for d in base.defects
                       if d.type == "contradiction"
                       or (d.type == "hidden" and d.note != f"refuted by {SYMBOLIC_WITNESS}"))
             if base is not None else frozenset())

    while runs < config.budget:
        if queue:
            scenario = queue.pop(0)
            if scenario.signature() in visited:
                continue
        elif config.synthesize and not bandit.exhausted(ctx.candidates, visited, config.eps):
            chosen = bandit.choose(ctx.candidates, visited, rng)
            if chosen is None:
                break
            scenario = chosen
        else:
            break
        visited.add(scenario.signature())
        order.append(scenario.id)

        pilot_ids = pilot(config.models_by_family, posterior.theta or None)
        fresh: list[Behaviour] = []
        for interp in pilot_ids:
            if runs >= config.budget:
                break
            fresh.append(ctx.runner.run(variant, scenario, interp))
            runs += 1
        seen = {b.run_key: ctx.lens.views(b, ctx.lens.violations(b)) for b in fresh}
        pilot_views = [v for b in fresh for v in seen[b.run_key]]
        ok_sets = [acted_violations(seen[b.run_key]) for b in fresh if b.status == "ok"]
        if should_escalate(pilot_views, ok_sets, seen_sets):
            for interp in members:
                if interp in pilot_ids:
                    continue
                if runs >= config.budget:
                    break
                fresh.append(ctx.runner.run(variant, scenario, interp))
                runs += 1

        for b in fresh:
            mine = ctx.lens.views(b, ctx.lens.violations(b))
            violated[b.run_key] = acted_violations(mine)
            if b.status == "ok":
                seen_sets.add(violated[b.run_key])
            behaviours.append(b)
            views.extend(mine)
            acts.extend(ctx.lens.acts(b))
        rasch = fit_rasch(acts)
        posterior = fit(observations(views), models=population, k_max=config.k_max,
                        p_single=config.p_single)
        refuter = _Memoised(ctx.refuter_factory(
            ranked_interpreters(config.models_by_family, posterior.theta, config.variants),
            budget,
        ), refutations)
        diagnosis = diagnose(
            behaviours, violated, views, posterior, refuter, ctx.lens.diff,
            observable=rasch.observable_items(), relevant=ctx.lens.relevant,
            tau=config.tau, ambig_threshold=config.ambig_threshold,
            weak_threshold=config.weak_threshold, rng_seed=config.rng_seed, known=known,
        )
        defects = diagnosis.defects
        scores = {repr(identity(d)): d.score for d in defects}
        bandit.update(scenario, information_gain(previous, scores))
        previous = scores

    found = frozenset(ambiguity_key(d) for d in defects if d.type == "ambiguity")
    dropped = found & base.ambiguity_keys if base is not None else frozenset()
    defects = [d for d in defects if d.type != "ambiguity" or ambiguity_key(d) not in dropped]
    if config.localize and ctx.localizer is not None:
        interps = oracle_interpreters(config.models_by_family, posterior.theta, config.variants)
        # Best supported first, until the localisation budget runs out; from then on every
        # ambiguity is unlocalised: an empty location, which matches no ground truth.
        located: dict[int, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
        for i in sorted((i for i, d in enumerate(defects) if d.type == "ambiguity"),
                        key=lambda i: -defects[i].score):
            sentences = (None if budget.exhausted("ddmin")
                         else ctx.localizer(variant, defects[i], interps, budget))
            located[i] = tuple(sentences or ())
        # Localised defects that now share type and location merge; unlocalised ones keep
        # their identity from before localisation (step and fields), so they stay apart.
        merge_key: dict[int, Hashable] = {}
        out: list[Defect] = []
        for i, d in enumerate(defects):
            new = dataclasses.replace(d, location=located[i]) if i in located else d
            merge_key[id(new)] = ambiguity_key(d) if i in located and not located[i] else new.key
            out.append(new)
        defects = merge_defects(out, key=lambda d: merge_key[id(d)])

    return Report(variant.name, defects, runs, ctx.runner.executed - executed_before, order,
                  dict(posterior.theta), behaviours, probe_runs=budget.probe,
                  ddmin_runs=budget.ddmin, ambiguity_keys=found, ambiguity_dropped=dropped,
                  kappa=dict(rasch.kappa), items=item_table(rasch, diagnosis),
                  unobservable=diagnosis.unobservable, literals=list(ctx.literals))
```

In `specdx/workbench.py`, add `from itertools import combinations` after `from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence`, and change `from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnModel` to `from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnModel, Projection`. In `loop_context`'s `localizer`, replace everything from `one_per_family: dict[str, InterpreterId] = {}` through `return len(classes) >= 2` with:
```python
            fields = frozenset(defect.location)

            def split_persists(corpus: TellCorpus) -> bool:
                """Spec v2 §3.6: the re-run (one behaviour per competent model and variant,
                seed 1) still yields two classes that differ on the ambiguity's fields."""
                probe = SpecVariant(f"{v.name}+ddmin", v.pack, corpus)
                seen: list[Projection] = []
                for interp in interps:
                    if not budget.take("ddmin"):
                        raise OutOfBudget
                    b = self.runner.run(probe, scenario, interp, kind="ddmin")
                    seen += [view.projection for view in lens.views(b, lens.violations(b))
                             if view.item == defect.note and view.acted]
                return any(lens.diff(a, b) & fields for a, b in combinations(seen, 2))
```

### Task 11: Truth targets (`observed_at`), frozen mutant packs and scorability

Spec v2 §4.1.
- **Truth targets.** Every mutation operator declares what its truth needs the population to reach: rows, events or steps. This is benchmark metadata, never an input to any method.
- **Scorable mutant.** A mutant is scorable when at least k = 3 base behaviours touch a target. A behaviour touches a row or event when it acted at an item whose slice covers it and its end state holds it. It touches a step when it acted at that step.
- **Frozen packs.** Mutant packs are now built in a staging directory and settled once. An existing identical pack is left untouched, and a differing one is an error, never overwritten. This is what lets Task 18 build mutants under the P1 `SPECDX_WORK`, which must never lose or rewrite a file.
- Planning resolutions 10 and 12 apply.

The rebuild was checked against the main checkout's packs: all 36 P1 mutant packs rebuild byte-identically, so settling leaves every one of them alone.

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `specdx/types.py` (new `Target`);
  - `specdx/mutate/operators.py` (whole file below);
  - `specdx/infer/items.py` (new `SpecLens.acted_steps`).
- Create: `specdx/eval/observability.py`
- Test: `tests/eval/test_observability.py` (new), `tests/mutate/test_operators.py` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `specdx.db.golden_tables`, `is_background`, `pack_digest`; `ColumnModel.keys`; Task 3's `SpecLens`.
- Produces (used by Tasks 12, 17):
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) Target(kind: str, name: str, key: str = "")` in `specdx.types`. Kinds are `"row"` (table, key), `"event"` (kind, reference; an empty kind matches any) and `"step"` (step id);
  - `GroundTruth.observed_at: tuple[Target, ...]` (default `()`);
  - `QUOTE_STEPS`, `A1_STEPS`, `A2_STEPS`, `A3_STEPS`;
  - `all_mutants(base, pdf, work_dir)`, with the same signature, now settling packs and never overwriting one;
  - `SpecLens.acted_steps(behaviour) -> list[WorkStep]`;
  - in `specdx.eval.observability`: `SCORABLE_K = 3`, `ActingLens` (Protocol: `columns`, `acted_steps`), `touches(target, step, state, columns) -> bool`, `touch_counts(targets: Mapping[str, Sequence[Target]], behaviours, lens: ActingLens) -> dict[str, int]` and `scorable(counts, k=SCORABLE_K) -> frozenset[str]`.
