`tests/eval/test_observability.py`:
```python
import pytest

from specdx.config import BASE_PACK
from specdx.db import golden_tables, split_background
from specdx.eval.observability import scorable, touch_counts, touches
from specdx.infer.act import drafting_steps
from specdx.infer.items import SpecLens
from specdx.mutate.operators import all_mutants, dev_split
from specdx.orchestrate.work_orders import load_work_orders
from specdx.spec.clauses import ClauseSet
from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnModel
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus
from specdx.types import Behaviour, InterpreterId, Scenario, State, Target
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant

STEPS = load_work_orders()
GRN12 = Scenario("grn12", 15, 12, 0)
STEP = {s.id: s for s in STEPS}


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def lens(pack) -> SpecLens:
    background, _ = split_background(golden_tables(pack))
    return SpecLens(ClauseSet.from_pack(pack, STEPS), STEPS, ColumnModel.of(pack),
                    drafting=drafting_steps(pack, STEPS), initial=background)


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def dev(pack, tmp_path_factory):
    base = SpecVariant("base", BASE_PACK, TellCorpus.build(pack, []))
    return dev_split(all_mutants(base, [], tmp_path_factory.mktemp("work")), 5, 20261006)


def _runs(pack, n: int, tables=None) -> list[Behaviour]:
    state = State(golden_tables(pack) if tables is None else tables, {},
                  {s.id: "done" for s in STEPS})
    return [Behaviour(f"r{i}", InterpreterId("m", "f", 0, i), GRN12, state) for i in range(n)]


def test_every_mutant_declares_what_its_truth_needs(dev) -> None:
    assert all(m.truth.observed_at for m in dev)
    observed = {m.id: m.truth.observed_at for m in dev}
    assert observed["H1-02"] == (Target("row", "inv", "INV-0031"),)
    assert observed["W2-01"] == (Target("event", "FLAGGED", "ITEM-0012"),)
    assert observed["C2-00"] == (Target("row", "po", "PO-0015"),)
    assert Target("step", "P2:decide_quote") in observed["A3-00"]
    assert observed["W1-04"] == (Target("row", "vinv", "VINV-0015"),)
    assert observed["H2-00"] == (Target("event", "", "PAY-0031"),)  # any event about PAY-0031


def test_a_target_is_touched_only_from_a_step_whose_slice_covers_it(pack) -> None:
    state, columns = State(golden_tables(pack)), ColumnModel.of(pack)
    po = Target("row", "po", "PO-0015")
    assert touches(po, STEP["P6:decide_po"], state, columns)
    assert not touches(po, STEP["DE-SM-02:draft_quote"], state, columns)
    assert not touches(po, STEP["P6:decide_po"], State({}), columns)  # the row is not there
    flag = Target("event", "FLAGGED", "ITEM-0012")
    assert touches(flag, STEP["DE-OP-01:check_availability"], state, columns)
    assert touches(Target("step", "P2:decide_quote"), STEP["P2:decide_quote"], State({}), columns)


def test_golden_runs_make_every_development_mutant_scorable(pack, lens, dev) -> None:
    counts = touch_counts({m.id: m.truth.observed_at for m in dev}, _runs(pack, 3), lens)
    assert counts == {m.id: 3 for m in dev} and scorable(counts) == {m.id for m in dev}
    two = touch_counts({m.id: m.truth.observed_at for m in dev}, _runs(pack, 2), lens)
    assert scorable(two) == frozenset()  # fewer than k = 3 behaviours


def test_runs_that_write_nothing_reach_only_the_steps_that_draft_nothing(pack, lens, dev) -> None:
    counts = touch_counts({m.id: m.truth.observed_at for m in dev}, _runs(pack, 3, {}), lens)
    assert scorable(counts) == {"A1-01", "A2-01", "A2-02", "A3-00"}
```

Append to `tests/mutate/test_operators.py`:
```python
def _stamps(root) -> dict[str, tuple[int, int]]:
    return {str(p.relative_to(root)): (p.stat().st_ino, p.stat().st_mtime_ns)
            for p in sorted(root.rglob("*")) if p.is_file()}


def test_a_second_build_leaves_every_existing_pack_untouched(pack, tmp_path) -> None:
    base = SpecVariant("base", BASE_PACK, TellCorpus.build(pack, []))
    first = all_mutants(base, [], tmp_path)
    before = _stamps(tmp_path / "mutants")
    again = all_mutants(base, [], tmp_path)
    assert _stamps(tmp_path / "mutants") == before  # nothing rewritten, nothing deleted
    assert [m.variant.digest() for m in again] == [m.variant.digest() for m in first]
    assert all(m.variant.pack.is_relative_to(tmp_path / "mutants") for m in again)


def test_a_pack_that_differs_from_what_the_code_builds_is_refused(pack, tmp_path) -> None:
    base = SpecVariant("base", BASE_PACK, TellCorpus.build(pack, []))
    all_mutants(base, [], tmp_path)
    edited = tmp_path / "mutants" / "C1-00" / BASE_PACK.name / "invariants.json"
    edited.write_text(edited.read_text(encoding="utf-8") + " ", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="differs"):
        all_mutants(base, [], tmp_path)
    assert edited.read_text(encoding="utf-8").endswith(" ")  # left as it was
```

`specdx/mutate/operators.py` (whole file):
```python
"""Mutation operators for an executable business specification (spec §4.3, §11 item 6).

Each operator copies the base pack, applies one edit, and states its ground truth. State
operators also give a kill witness: a state that proves the defect is real.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import random
import shutil
import tempfile
from collections import defaultdict
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from tonomyx.canon.pack import CanonPack

from specdx.db import golden_tables, is_background, pack_digest
from specdx.mutate.pack_copy import (
    append_text,
    copy_pack,
    edit_csv_rows,
    edit_json,
    regenerate_sql,
    replace_text,
)
from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnModel
from specdx.spec.tell import Chunk, TellCorpus
from specdx.types import State, Target
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant

OPERATOR_TYPES: dict[str, str] = {
    "C1": "contradiction", "C2": "contradiction", "H1": "hidden", "H2": "hidden",
    "A1": "ambiguity", "A2": "ambiguity", "A3": "ambiguity", "W1": "weak", "W2": "weak",
}
C1_SITES = ("po-15", "grn-12", "vinv-cankiem", "apr0102-approved", "apr0155-approved",
            "apr0210-pending")
C2_SITES = (
    ("po", "ma_po", "PO-0015", "Đã duyệt", ("golden:po:PO-0015:trang_thai",)),
    ("apr", "ma_apr", "APR-0102", "Đã duyệt",
     ("golden:apr:APR-0102:trang_thai", "inv:apr0102-approved")),
    ("apr", "ma_apr", "APR-0155", "Đã duyệt",
     ("golden:apr:APR-0155:trang_thai", "inv:apr0155-approved")),
)
H1_SITES = (
    ("vinv", "ma_vinv", "VINV-0015", "trang_thai", "Nháp"),
    ("inv", "ma_inv", "INV-0031", "doi_soat", "Khớp"),
    ("inv", "ma_inv", "INV-0031", "trang_thai", "Chờ duyệt"),
    ("pay", "ma_pay", "PAY-0031", "trang_thai", "Nháp"),
    ("apr", "ma_apr", "APR-0210", "muc_tu_hanh", "L2"),
)
H2_SITES = (("PAID", "PAY-0031"), ("REJECTED", "APR-0155"))
A1_SITES = (
    "**mức tối đa 5%**.",
    "Yêu cầu vượt trần (vd 8%) phải gắn cờ ngoại lệ và dừng ở cổng P2 (APR-0102);",
    "GRN lệch / lô lỗi / giao trễ: P4 xử lý ngoại lệ — KHÔNG mở APR chuẩn.",
)
A2_SITES = (
    ("từ 100 sản phẩm giảm 5%;", "từ 100 sản phẩm giảm ở mức hợp lý;"),
    ("**mức tối đa 5%**.", "**mức tối đa hợp lý**."),
    ("CASE-0031 có ĐÚNG 3 cổng: APR-0102/P2, APR-0155/P6 (maker-checker), "
     "APR-0210/P5 (đối soát lệch).", "CASE-0031 có một số cổng phê duyệt phù hợp."),
)
# The steps each edited rule governs (spec v2 §4.1), one tuple per site above. The
# discount rules govern quoting and its approval; the GRN-exception rule governs the
# receipt, the reconciliation and the payment that could open an approval; the gate
# count governs every step that opens or decides one of the three gates.
QUOTE_STEPS = ("DE-SM-02:draft_quote", "P2:decide_quote")
A1_STEPS: tuple[tuple[str, ...], ...] = (
    QUOTE_STEPS,
    QUOTE_STEPS,
    ("DE-OP-02:record_goods_receipt", "DE-FN-02:reconcile", "DE-FN-03:draft_payment"),
)
A2_STEPS: tuple[tuple[str, ...], ...] = (
    ("DE-SM-02:draft_quote",),
    QUOTE_STEPS,
    (*QUOTE_STEPS, "DE-FN-04:draft_purchase_order", "P6:decide_po", "DE-FN-03:draft_payment"),
)
A3_STEPS: tuple[str, ...] = QUOTE_STEPS
A3_ARTICLE = (
    "---\nkb_id: KB-0009\nversion: 1\neffective_date: 2026-09-01\ndomain: sales\n"
    "title: Chính sách giá & chiết khấu (cập nhật)\n---\n"
    "Mức chiết khấu tối đa là 7%; yêu cầu đến 7% được áp dụng trực tiếp, "
    "không cần dừng ở cổng duyệt.\n"
)
W1_SITES = (
    ("soline", "chiet_khau", "chietkhau-0"), ("soline", "don_gia", "soline-dongia-blank"),
    ("po", "don_gia", "po-dongia-blank"), ("inv", "so_tien", "inv-sotien-blank"),
    ("vinv", "so_tien", "vinv-sotien-blank"), ("pay", "so_tien", "pay-sotien-blank"),
    ("apr", "gia_tri", "apr-giatri-blank"),
)
W2_SITES = (("DRAFTED", "SO-0031"), ("FLAGGED", "ITEM-0012"), ("DRAFTED", "PO-0015"),
            ("RECEIVED", "GRN-0015"), ("RECONCILED", "VINV-0015"), ("DRAFTED", "PAY-0031"))
_TRIGGER = """
-- specdx mutant C2: forbid a status the golden state requires
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION fn_specdx_c2() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.{key} = '{value}' AND NEW.trang_thai = '{status}' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'specdx mutant: % may not be %', NEW.{key}, NEW.trang_thai;
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END; $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_specdx_c2 ON {table};
CREATE TRIGGER trg_specdx_c2 BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON {table}
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION fn_specdx_c2();
"""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GroundTruth:
    """observed_at: what the truth needs the population to reach (spec v2 §4.1). It is
    benchmark metadata for scorability, never an input to any method."""

    mutant_id: str
    type: str
    location: frozenset[str]
    location_kind: str
    observed_at: tuple[Target, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Mutant:
    id: str
    operator: str
    site: str
    variant: SpecVariant
    truth: GroundTruth


def _kb_files_with(pack: Path, text: str) -> list[str]:
    return [f"kb/{p.name}" for p in sorted((pack / "kb").glob("*.md"))
            if text in p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")]


def _edit_principles(pack: Path, old: str, new: str | None) -> None:
    def fn(meta: dict) -> dict:
        items = [p for p in meta["nguyen_tac"] if p != old]
        if new is not None and len(items) < len(meta["nguyen_tac"]):
            items = [new if p == old else p for p in meta["nguyen_tac"]]
        return {**meta, "nguyen_tac": items}
    edit_json(pack, "canon.meta.json", fn)


def _corpus(pack: Path, pdf: Sequence[Chunk]) -> TellCorpus:
    return TellCorpus.build(CanonPack(pack), pdf)


def _steps(ids: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[Target, ...]:
    return tuple(Target("step", s) for s in ids)


def _rows(pack: CanonPack, table: str) -> tuple[Target, ...]:
    """The golden case rows of a table (background rows belong to no case)."""
    keys = ColumnModel.of(pack).keys[table]
    return tuple(Target("row", table, ":".join(r[k] for k in keys))
                 for r in golden_tables(pack)[table] if not is_background(table, r))


def _settle(staged: Path, final: Path) -> Path:
    """A mutant pack is written once. A later build must reproduce it byte for byte, and is
    then left alone, so runs cached against it stay valid; a differing build is an error,
    never an overwrite."""
    if final.exists():
        if pack_digest(final) != pack_digest(staged):
            raise ValueError(f"{final} differs from the pack this code builds; it was made by "
                             "other code or edited by hand. Move it aside to rebuild it.")
        return final
    final.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    shutil.copytree(staged, final)
    return final


def all_mutants(base: SpecVariant, pdf: Sequence[Chunk], work_dir: Path) -> list[Mutant]:
    """Every mutant, its pack under work_dir/mutants/<id>. Packs are built in a staging
    directory and settled there once (_settle)."""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as staging:
        return _build(base, pdf, work_dir, Path(staging))


def _build(base: SpecVariant, pdf: Sequence[Chunk], work_dir: Path, staging: Path) -> list[Mutant]:
    out: list[Mutant] = []
    base_pack = CanonPack(base.pack)

    def fresh(mid: str) -> Path:
        return copy_pack(base.pack, staging / mid / base.pack.name)

    def _mutant(mid: str, op: str, site: str, pack: Path, corpus: TellCorpus,
                location: frozenset[str], kind: str, observed_at: tuple[Target, ...]) -> Mutant:
        final = _settle(pack, work_dir / "mutants" / mid / base.pack.name)
        return Mutant(mid, op, site, SpecVariant(mid, final, corpus),
                      GroundTruth(mid, OPERATOR_TYPES[op], location, kind, observed_at))

    for i, site in enumerate(C1_SITES):
        mid, pack = f"C1-{i:02d}", fresh(f"C1-{i:02d}")
        inv = next(x for x in CanonPack(pack).invariants if x["id"] == site)
        value = str(inv["assert"]["eq"])
        alt = str(int(value) + 3) if value.isdigit() else f"{value} (khác)"
        added = {"id": f"mut-c1-{site}", "desc": f"{inv['desc']} (đột biến)",
                 "table": inv["table"], "where": copy.deepcopy(inv["where"]),
                 "assert": {"col": inv["assert"]["col"], "eq": alt}}
        edit_json(pack, "invariants.json",
                  lambda d, a=added: {**d, "invariants": [*d["invariants"], a]})
        # Both invariants read the row the site's invariant selects.
        row = Target("row", inv["table"], ":".join(str(v) for v in inv["where"].values()))
        out.append(_mutant(mid, "C1", site, pack, _corpus(pack, pdf),
                           frozenset({f"inv:{site}", f"inv:mut-c1-{site}"}), "conflict", (row,)))

    for i, (table, key, value, status, location) in enumerate(C2_SITES):
        mid, pack = f"C2-{i:02d}", fresh(f"C2-{i:02d}")
        append_text(pack, "targets/postgres/guardrails.sql",
                    _TRIGGER.format(key=key, value=value, status=status, table=table))
        out.append(_mutant(mid, "C2", value, pack, _corpus(pack, pdf),
                           frozenset(location), "conflict", (Target("row", table, value),)))

    for i, (table, key, ident, column, value) in enumerate(H1_SITES):
        mid, pack = f"H1-{i:02d}", fresh(f"H1-{i:02d}")

        def change(rows, key=key, ident=ident, column=column, value=value):
            for r in rows:
                if r[key] == ident:
                    r[column] = value
            return rows

        edit_csv_rows(pack, table, change)
        regenerate_sql(pack)
        out.append(_mutant(mid, "H1", f"{table}.{column}", pack, _corpus(pack, pdf),
                           frozenset({f"golden:{table}:{ident}:{column}"}), "clause",
                           (Target("row", table, ident),)))

    for i, (kind, ref) in enumerate(H2_SITES):
        mid, pack = f"H2-{i:02d}", fresh(f"H2-{i:02d}")
        added = {"id": f"mut-h2-{kind}-{ref}", "desc": f"Có sự kiện {kind} cho {ref}",
                 "table": "evt", "where": {"kind": kind, "ref": ref}, "assert": {"count": 1}}
        edit_json(pack, "invariants.json",
                  lambda d, a=added: {**d, "invariants": [*d["invariants"], a]})
        # The demanded event is one T never motivates, so no admissible run writes it: the
        # truth is reached where events about its reference are written (any kind).
        out.append(_mutant(mid, "H2", f"{kind}:{ref}", pack, _corpus(pack, pdf),
                           frozenset({f"inv:mut-h2-{kind}-{ref}"}), "clause",
                           (Target("event", "", ref),)))

    for i, text in enumerate(A1_SITES):
        mid, pack = f"A1-{i:02d}", fresh(f"A1-{i:02d}")
        if not base.corpus.find(text):
            raise ValueError(f"A1 site matches no Tell sentence: {text!r}")
        for rel in _kb_files_with(pack, text):
            replace_text(pack, rel, text, "")
        _edit_principles(pack, text, None)
        corpus = _corpus(pack, pdf)
        corpus = corpus.without(corpus.find(text))
        chunks = frozenset(TellCorpus.chunk_of(s) for s in base.corpus.find(text))
        if not chunks:
            raise ValueError(f"{mid} has an empty ground truth")
        out.append(_mutant(mid, "A1", text, pack, corpus, chunks, "chunk", _steps(A1_STEPS[i])))

    for i, (old, new) in enumerate(A2_SITES):
        mid, pack = f"A2-{i:02d}", fresh(f"A2-{i:02d}")
        matched = False
        for rel in _kb_files_with(pack, old):
            if replace_text(pack, rel, old, new):
                matched = True
        old_canon_meta = (pack / "canon.meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        _edit_principles(pack, old, new)
        new_canon_meta = (pack / "canon.meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if old_canon_meta != new_canon_meta:
            matched = True
        corpus = _corpus(pack, pdf)
        if corpus.find(old):
            matched = True
        for sid in corpus.find(old):
            corpus = corpus.replace(sid, new)
        truth = frozenset(corpus.find(new))
        if not matched or not truth:
            raise ValueError(f"A2 site matches nothing: {old!r}")
        out.append(_mutant(mid, "A2", old, pack, corpus, truth, "sentence", _steps(A2_STEPS[i])))

    mid, pack = "A3-00", fresh("A3-00")
    (pack / "kb" / "KB-0009.md").write_text(A3_ARTICLE, encoding="utf-8")
    corpus = _corpus(pack, pdf)
    truth = frozenset(corpus.sentence_ids(["KB:KB-0009"]))
    if not truth:
        raise ValueError(f"{mid} has an empty ground truth")
    out.append(_mutant(mid, "A3", "KB-0009", pack, corpus, truth, "sentence", _steps(A3_STEPS)))

    for i, (table, column, inv_id) in enumerate(W1_SITES):
        mid, pack = f"W1-{i:02d}", fresh(f"W1-{i:02d}")
        edit_json(pack, "invariants.json", lambda d, x=inv_id: {
            **d, "invariants": [v for v in d["invariants"] if v["id"] != x]})

        def unblank(meta, table=table, column=column):
            blank = dict(meta["cot_de_trong_khong_canon"])
            blank[table] = [c for c in blank[table] if c.split()[0] != column]
            return {**meta, "cot_de_trong_khong_canon": blank}

        edit_json(pack, "canon.meta.json", unblank)
        out.append(_mutant(mid, "W1", f"{table}.{column}", pack, _corpus(pack, pdf),
                           frozenset({f"{table}.{column}"}), "field", _rows(base_pack, table)))

    for i, (kind, ref) in enumerate(W2_SITES):
        mid, pack = f"W2-{i:02d}", fresh(f"W2-{i:02d}")
        edit_csv_rows(pack, "evt", lambda rows, k=kind, r=ref: [
            x for x in rows if not (x["kind"] == k and x["ref"] == r)])
        regenerate_sql(pack)
        out.append(_mutant(mid, "W2", f"{kind}:{ref}", pack, _corpus(pack, pdf),
                           frozenset({f"evt:{kind}:{ref}"}), "field",
                           (Target("event", kind, ref),)))
    return out


def dev_split(mutants: Sequence[Mutant], per_type: int, seed: int) -> list[Mutant]:
    by_type: dict[str, list[Mutant]] = defaultdict(list)
    for m in sorted(mutants, key=lambda m: m.id):
        by_type[m.truth.type].append(m)
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    chosen: list[Mutant] = []
    for kind in sorted(by_type):
        pool = by_type[kind]
        rng.shuffle(pool)
        chosen += pool[:per_type]
    return sorted(chosen, key=lambda m: m.id)


def witness_state(mutant: Mutant, golden: State) -> State | None:
    """A state proving a state-level defect: the canon's own end state, edited if needed."""
    tables = {t: [dict(r) for r in rows] for t, rows in golden.tables.items()}
    if mutant.operator in ("C1", "H1", "H2"):
        return State(tables, dict(golden.derived), dict(golden.steps))
    if mutant.operator == "W1":
        table, column = mutant.site.split(".")
        for r in tables[table]:
            r[column] = "1"
        return State(tables, dict(golden.derived), dict(golden.steps))
    if mutant.operator == "W2":
        kind, ref = mutant.site.split(":")
        tables["evt"] = [e for e in tables["evt"] if not (e["kind"] == kind and e["ref"] == ref)]
        return State(tables, dict(golden.derived), dict(golden.steps))
    return None
```

In `specdx/infer/items.py`, add this method to `SpecLens`, before `acts`:

```python
    def acted_steps(self, behaviour: Behaviour) -> list[WorkStep]:
        """The interpreter steps at which this behaviour acted."""
        return [step for step in self.steps
                if self._acted(behaviour, step,
                               self.columns.project(behaviour.state.tables, step.slice))]
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement scorability**

`specdx/eval/observability.py`:
```python
"""Observability-aware scoring (spec v2 §4.1): which mutants the population can score.

A mutant is scorable when, in the base specification's runs, at least k acted behaviours
touch its observed_at targets. A behaviour touches a row or an event when it acted at an
item whose step's slice covers it and its end state holds it; it touches a step when it
acted at that step. The scorable set is computed once, from base runs only, and is the
same for SpecDx, B2 and B3.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Protocol

from specdx.orchestrate.work_orders import WorkStep
from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnModel
from specdx.types import Behaviour, State, Target

SCORABLE_K = 3  # spec v2 §3.10


class ActingLens(Protocol):
    columns: ColumnModel

    def acted_steps(self, behaviour: Behaviour) -> list[WorkStep]: ...


def _covers(step: WorkStep, table: str, key: str) -> bool:
    return any(entry == table or entry == f"{table}={key}" for entry in step.slice)


def touches(target: Target, step: WorkStep, state: State, columns: ColumnModel) -> bool:
    """Whether a behaviour that acted at this step touches the target."""
    if target.kind == "step":
        return target.name == step.id
    if target.kind == "event":
        return _covers(step, "evt", target.key) and any(
            target.name in ("", e.get("kind")) and e.get("ref") == target.key
            for e in state.tables.get("evt", []))
    if target.kind == "row":
        keys = columns.keys[target.name]
        return _covers(step, target.name, target.key) and any(
            ":".join(str(r.get(k, "")) for k in keys) == target.key
            for r in state.tables.get(target.name, []))
    raise ValueError(f"unknown target kind {target.kind!r}")


def touch_counts(
    targets: Mapping[str, Sequence[Target]], behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour], lens: ActingLens
) -> dict[str, int]:
    """For each mutant id, how many base behaviours touch at least one of its targets."""
    acted = {b.run_key: lens.acted_steps(b) for b in behaviours if b.status == "ok"}
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for mid, wanted in targets.items():
        counts[mid] = sum(
            1 for b in behaviours if b.status == "ok"
            and any(touches(t, s, b.state, lens.columns) for s in acted[b.run_key]
                    for t in wanted)
        )
    return counts


def scorable(counts: Mapping[str, int], k: int = SCORABLE_K) -> frozenset[str]:
    return frozenset(mid for mid, n in counts.items() if n >= k)
```

```bash
EOF
```
Expected: `36 mutants; differing from the P1 packs: []`. The script builds into a temporary directory and only reads the P1 packs. If any pack differs, stop and report: Task 18 would refuse to build that mutant.

---

### Task 12: Gate v2: the screened population, scorability and the two tables

Spec v2 §4.1–§4.2. The gate runs on the population the capability screen froze. It refuses an empty population, and one that differs from the screen file the configuration names.
- **Scorability.** After the base run and before any mutant runs, it counts the base behaviours touching each mutant's targets (Task 11).
- **Tables.** The primary table scores the scorable mutants and decides the verdict; a type with fewer than 3 scorable mutants is a loss. The secondary table (`scores`, `untyped`) scores every mutant, as P1 did, and the markdown reports the number excluded per type. The literal typed-against-typed table stays.
- **Per-mutant lines.** They gain `touches`, `scorable`, `unobservable` and `delta_shift` (each item's δ on the mutant minus on the base, spec v2 §3.9). The base line gains `touches`, `theta`, `kappa`, `items` and `unobservable`.
- **Baselines.** B2's best interpreter is the highest stage-2 θ, which is already `report.theta`.
- **Output name.** The per-mutant file is named after `--out`: `results/p1b-gate.json` gives `results/p1b-gate-mutants.jsonl`.

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `specdx/eval/matching.py` (`MIN_SCORABLE`, `gate_verdict(..., scorable=, min_scorable=)`);
  - `specdx/eval/pilot.py` (whole file below);
  - `specdx/cli.py` (`mutants_path`).
- Test: `tests/eval/test_pilot.py` (whole file below), `tests/test_cli.py` (import, appended test)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 11's `touch_counts`, `scorable`, `SCORABLE_K`, `GroundTruth.observed_at`, `Target`, `SpecLens.acted_steps` and `.columns` (through `base_ctx.lens`); Task 10's `Report.items`, `.kappa`, `.unobservable`.
- Produces (used by Tasks 13, 17, 18):
  - `MIN_SCORABLE = 3`;
  - `load_population(path: Path) -> dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]`. It reads `{"population": {...}, "stop": bool}` and raises `ValueError` when `stop` is set;
  - `GateConfig.screen: Path | None` (JSON key `"screen"`) and `GateConfig.check_population() -> None`, which raises `ValueError` on an empty or unscreened population;
  - `GateResult` gains `primary`, `primary_untyped`, `touches: dict[str, int]`, `types: dict[str, str]`, the property `.scorable` and the method `.counts(only_scorable: bool) -> dict[str, int]`;
  - `delta_shift(base: Report, report: Report) -> dict[str, float]`;
  - the markdown header `# P1b gate v2: <headline>`;
  - `mutants_path(out: Path) -> Path` in `specdx.cli`.

```python
import psycopg
import pytest

from specdx.active.loop import LoopContext

def test_capability_needs_a_declared_and_a_real_tool_call() -> None:
    def handle(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        if request.url.path == "/api/show":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"capabilities": ["completion", "tools"]})
        call = {"function": {"name": "echo", "arguments": {"text": "ok"}}}
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"message": {"role": "assistant", "content": "",
                                                     "tool_calls": [call]}})

    cap = check_model("m", base_url="http://x", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handle))
    assert cap.usable


def test_a_model_without_the_tools_capability_is_not_usable() -> None:
    def handle(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"capabilities": ["completion"]})

    cap = check_model("m", base_url="http://x", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handle))
    assert not cap.declares_tools and not cap.usable


def test_a_model_ollama_does_not_have_is_an_error_not_unusable() -> None:
    def handle(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        return httpx.Response(404, json={"error": "model 'missing:7b' not found"})

    with pytest.raises(httpx.HTTPStatusError):
        check_model("missing:7b", base_url="http://x", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handle))


def test_a_failing_chat_call_is_an_error_too() -> None:
    def handle(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        if request.url.path == "/api/show":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"capabilities": ["completion", "tools"]})
        return httpx.Response(500, json={"error": "llama runner process has terminated"})

    with pytest.raises(httpx.HTTPStatusError):
        check_model("m", base_url="http://x", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handle))


CANON = {"grn12": Scenario("grn12", 15, 12, 0)}


class _Runner:
    def __init__(self, broken: bool) -> None:
        self.broken, self.executed = broken, 0

    def run(self, variant, scenario, interp, suffix="", *, kind="population"):
        self.executed += 1
        key = f"{variant.name}|{interp.key}"
        if variant.name == "outage":
            raise InfrastructureError("Ollama failed 5 times: ConnectError")
        if self.broken:
            return Behaviour(key, interp, scenario, State.empty(), status="error", error="bad SQL")
        bad = "c1" if variant.name.startswith("H1-") else ""
        return Behaviour(key, interp, scenario, State({}, {"v": bad}, {}))


STEP = WorkStep("s", "de", "A", "i", ("x",))


class _Lens:
    """Every behaviour acts at the one step "s" and touches its targets."""

    relevant = {"s": frozenset({"c1"})}
    columns = None

    def acted_steps(self, b):
        return [STEP] if b.status == "ok" else []

    def violations(self, b):
        return frozenset(x for x in b.state.derived.get("v", "").split(",") if x)

    def views(self, b, violated):
        if b.status != "ok":
            return []
        return [ItemView(f"{b.scenario.id}|s", b.run_key, b.interpreter.model,
                         b.interpreter.family, "K", violated, ())]

    def acts(self, b):
        return [Act(v.item, v.run_key, v.model, True) for v in self.views(b, frozenset())]

    def diff(self, a, b):
        return frozenset()


class _Refute:
    def refute(self, targets, scenario, behaviours):
        return RefuteResult(True, "w", 1)


class _Bench:
    base = SpecVariant("base", Path("."), TellCorpus([]))

    def loop_context(self, variant, config):
        return LoopContext(_Runner(variant.name == "broken"), _Lens(), CANON,
                           list(CANON.values()), 5, lambda i, b: _Refute())


def _mutant(mid, kind, location, step="s"):
    return Mutant(mid, mid[:2], "s", SpecVariant(mid, Path("."), TellCorpus([])),
                  GroundTruth(mid, kind, frozenset(location), "clause", (Target("step", step),)))


def _config(tmp_path) -> GateConfig:

    path.write_text(json.dumps({"models_by_family": {"q": ["m1"], "l": ["m2"]},
                                "variants": [0], "seeds": [1, 2], "budget": 10,
                                "dev_per_type": 1, "rng_seed": 1}), encoding="utf-8")
    return GateConfig.load(path)


def test_the_gate_configuration_carries_both_run_budgets(tmp_path) -> None:
    assert (_config(tmp_path).specdx().probe_budget,
            _config(tmp_path).specdx().localize_budget) == (100, 80)

    assert (gate.specdx().probe_budget, gate.specdx().localize_budget) == (100, 80)

def test_every_mutant_and_the_base_are_written_one_per_line(tmp_path) -> None:
    out = tmp_path / "results" / "p1-gate-mutants.jsonl"

    assert hit["base"] is False and hit["type"] == "hidden" and hit["failure"] is None
    assert hit["truth"] == {"location": ["c1"], "kind": "clause"}

    assert set(hit["matches"]["b2"]) == {"typed", "untyped"}
    assert hit["defects"]["specdx"][0]["location"] == ["c1"]
    assert set(hit["runs"]) == {"population", "probe", "ddmin", "executed"}
    assert hit["ambiguity_keys"] == [] and hit["ambiguity_dropped"] == []
    assert base["ambiguity_keys"] == []
    assert "bad SQL" in broken["failure"] and broken["matches"] is None

@pytest.mark.parametrize("error", [

def test_database_and_transport_failures_stop_the_gate(tmp_path, error) -> None:
    class _Down(_Bench):

def test_scorability_comes_from_the_base_runs_and_only_scorable_mutants_count(tmp_path) -> None:
    reached = _mutant("H1-00", "hidden", {"c1"})
    unreached = _mutant("H1-01", "hidden", {"c1"}, step="elsewhere")
    out = tmp_path / "lines.jsonl"
    result = run_gate(_config(tmp_path), _Bench(), [reached, unreached], mutants_out=out)
    assert result.touches == {"H1-00": 4, "H1-01": 0}  # 2 models x 2 seeds on grn12
    assert result.scorable == {"H1-00"}
    assert result.primary["specdx"]["hidden"].tp == 1
    assert result.primary["specdx"]["hidden"].fn == 0  # H1-01 is excluded, not missed
    assert result.scores["specdx"]["hidden"].tp == 2  # the secondary table keeps both
    assert not result.wins["hidden"]  # one scorable hidden mutant: fewer than 3 is a loss
    base, *mutants = [json.loads(x) for x in out.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    assert base["touches"] == {"H1-00": 4, "H1-01": 0}
    assert [(x["mutant"], x["scorable"]) for x in mutants] == [("H1-00", True), ("H1-01", False)]
    assert all(x["unobservable"] == [] and x["delta_shift"] == {"grn12|s": 0.0} for x in mutants)
    report = gate_markdown(result)
    assert "| hidden | 1 of 2 |" in report and "| H1-01 | hidden | 0 | no |" in report


def test_a_type_with_fewer_than_three_scorable_mutants_is_a_loss() -> None:
    perfect = {t: Prf(tp=3) for t in ("contradiction", "hidden", "ambiguity", "weak")}
    none = {t: Prf(fn=3) for t in perfect}
    typed = {"specdx": perfect, "b2": none, "b3": none}
    passed, wins = gate_verdict(typed, typed)
    assert passed and all(wins.values())
    counts = {"contradiction": 3, "hidden": 2, "ambiguity": 3, "weak": 0}
    passed, wins = gate_verdict(typed, typed, scorable=counts)
    assert not passed and wins == {"contradiction": True, "hidden": False, "ambiguity": True,
                                   "weak": False}

    if screen is not None:
        raw["screen"] = str(screen)
    path = tmp_path / "gate.json"

    screen = tmp_path / "screen.json"

                      encoding="utf-8")
    run_gate(_gate_file(tmp_path, {"q": ["m1"], "l": ["m2"]}, screen), _Bench(), [])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="differs from the population"):
        run_gate(_gate_file(tmp_path, {"q": ["m1"]}, screen), _Bench(), [])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="empty"):
        run_gate(_gate_file(tmp_path, {}), _Bench(), [])
    screen.write_text(json.dumps({"population": {"q": ["m1"]}, "stop": True}), encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="fewer than 2 families"):
        load_population(screen)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the verdict rule**

In `specdx/eval/matching.py`, add above `def gate_verdict`:
```python
# A type with fewer scorable mutants than this is a loss (spec v2 §4.2).
MIN_SCORABLE = 3
```
and replace `gate_verdict` with:
```python
def gate_verdict(
    typed: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Prf]],
    untyped: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Prf]],
    *,
    method: str = "specdx",
    baselines: Sequence[str] = ("b2", "b3"),
    required: int = 3,
    scorable: Mapping[str, int] | None = None,
    min_scorable: int = MIN_SCORABLE,
) -> tuple[bool, dict[str, bool]]:
    """Spec §4.8 as clarified in §11 item 12. Contradiction and hidden: SpecDx's typed F1
    must exceed every baseline's untyped F1 (the baselines report hidden only, so their
    typed F1 is no bar). Ambiguity and weak: the baselines cannot locate those truths at
    all, so SpecDx's typed F1 must reach ABSOLUTE_BAR. The gate needs `required` wins.
    scorable: scorable mutants per type (spec v2 §4.2); a type with fewer than
    min_scorable of them is a loss whatever its scores."""
    compared = _wins(typed[method], [untyped[b] for b in baselines])
    wins = {t: reaches_bar(typed[method][t]) if t in BASELINE_BLIND else compared[t]
            for t in DEFECT_TYPES}
    if scorable is not None:
        wins = {t: won and scorable.get(t, 0) >= min_scorable for t, won in wins.items()}
    return sum(wins.values()) >= required, wins
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement gate v2**

`specdx/eval/pilot.py` (whole file):
```python
"""The capability pilot (spec §4.2) and the go/no-go gate (spec §4.8; v2 §4.1–§4.2).

Gate v2 runs on the population the capability screen froze. Before any mutant runs, it
computes from the base runs which mutants the population can score (eval.observability).
The primary table scores those only and decides the verdict; a type with fewer than three
scorable mutants is a loss. The secondary table scores all mutants, as P1 did.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Protocol

import httpx
import psycopg

from specdx.active.loop import LoopContext, Report, SpecDxConfig, run_specdx
from specdx.baselines.b2_single import b2_report
from specdx.baselines.b3_irt import b3_report
from specdx.config import OLLAMA_URL
from specdx.eval.matching import (
    ABSOLUTE_BAR,
    MIN_SCORABLE,
    Prf,
    evaluate_reports,
    gate_rule,
    gate_verdict,
    literal_wins,
    match_findings,
)
from specdx.eval.observability import SCORABLE_K, scorable, touch_counts
from specdx.mutate.operators import GroundTruth, Mutant
from specdx.types import DEFECT_TYPES, Defect, InfrastructureError
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant

# Faults of the machinery, not of a mutant: the gate stops rather than score around them.
INFRASTRUCTURE_ERRORS: tuple[type[BaseException], ...] = (
    psycopg.OperationalError, psycopg.InterfaceError, httpx.TransportError, InfrastructureError,
)

ECHO = {"type": "function", "function": {
    "name": "echo", "description": "Trả lại văn bản.",
    "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {"text": {"type": "string"}},
                   "required": ["text"]}}}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Capability:
    model: str
    declares_tools: bool
    calls_tool: bool

    @property
    def usable(self) -> bool:
        return self.declares_tools and self.calls_tool


def check_model(
    model: str, *, base_url: str = OLLAMA_URL, transport: httpx.BaseTransport | None = None
) -> Capability:
    """An HTTP error (a model Ollama does not have, a crashed runner) is raised: only a
    model that answers can be judged unusable."""
    with httpx.Client(transport=transport, timeout=300.0) as client:
        response = client.post(f"{base_url}/api/show", json={"model": model})
        response.raise_for_status()
        show = response.json()
        if "tools" not in show.get("capabilities", []):
            return Capability(model, False, False)
        body: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": model, "stream": False, "tools": [ECHO], "options": {"temperature": 0},
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "Gọi công cụ echo với text là 'ok'."}],
        }
        if model.startswith("qwen3"):
            body["think"] = False
        reply = client.post(f"{base_url}/api/chat", json=body)
        reply.raise_for_status()
        message = reply.json().get("message", {})
    calls = message.get("tool_calls") or []
    return Capability(model, True, any((c.get("function") or {}).get("name") == "echo"
                                       for c in calls))


def load_population(path: Path) -> dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]:
    """The population a capability screen froze (its "population"), refusing a screen that
    ended in the stop rule."""
    raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if raw.get("stop"):
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: the screen admitted fewer than 2 families; P1b stops")
    return {f: tuple(ms) for f, ms in raw["population"].items()}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GateConfig:
    """screen: the capability screen's JSON; when set, the population must be the one it
    froze (spec v2 §2.2)."""

    models_by_family: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]
    variants: tuple[int, ...]
    seeds: tuple[int, ...]
    budget: int
    dev_per_type: int
    rng_seed: int
    probe_budget: int = SpecDxConfig.probe_budget
    localize_budget: int = SpecDxConfig.localize_budget
    screen: Path | None = None

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: Path) -> GateConfig:
        raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        return cls(
            models_by_family={f: tuple(ms) for f, ms in raw["models_by_family"].items()},
            variants=tuple(raw["variants"]), seeds=tuple(raw["seeds"]), budget=int(raw["budget"]),
            dev_per_type=int(raw["dev_per_type"]), rng_seed=int(raw["rng_seed"]),
            probe_budget=int(raw.get("probe_budget", SpecDxConfig.probe_budget)),
            localize_budget=int(raw.get("localize_budget", SpecDxConfig.localize_budget)),
            screen=Path(raw["screen"]) if raw.get("screen") else None,
        )

    def check_population(self) -> None:
        """The gate runs only on a non-empty population, the screen's when one is named."""
        if not self.models_by_family or not any(self.models_by_family.values()):
            raise ValueError("models_by_family is empty: run `specdx screen` and freeze its "
                             "population into the gate configuration first")
        if self.screen is not None and load_population(self.screen) != self.models_by_family:
            raise ValueError(f"models_by_family differs from the population {self.screen} "
                             "froze; the gate runs on the screened population only")

    def specdx(self) -> SpecDxConfig:
        return SpecDxConfig(self.models_by_family, variants=self.variants, seeds=self.seeds,
                            budget=self.budget, rng_seed=self.rng_seed,
                            probe_budget=self.probe_budget, localize_budget=self.localize_budget)


class BenchLike(Protocol):
    base: SpecVariant

    def loop_context(self, variant: SpecVariant, config: SpecDxConfig) -> LoopContext: ...


@dataclass
class GateResult:
    """scores and untyped: the secondary tables, over every mutant that ran. primary and
    primary_untyped: the same over the scorable mutants only, which decide the verdict.
    touches: per mutant, the base behaviours that touch its observed_at targets."""

    scores: dict[str, dict[str, Prf]]
    untyped: dict[str, dict[str, Prf]]
    passed: bool
    wins: dict[str, bool]
    failed: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    literal_wins: dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)
    primary: dict[str, dict[str, Prf]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    primary_untyped: dict[str, dict[str, Prf]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    touches: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    types: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def scorable(self) -> frozenset[str]:
        return scorable(self.touches)

    def counts(self, only_scorable: bool) -> dict[str, int]:
        """Mutants per type, all of them or the scorable ones."""
        out = {t: 0 for t in DEFECT_TYPES}
        for mid, t in self.types.items():
            if not only_scorable or mid in self.scorable:
                out[t] += 1
        return out

    @property
    def headline(self) -> str:
        """No verdict is given while any mutant failed to run."""
        if self.failed:
            return "INCOMPLETE"
        return "PASSED" if self.passed else "NOT PASSED"

    def to_json(self) -> str:
        def table(s: dict[str, dict[str, Prf]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
            return {m: {t: p.as_dict() for t, p in by.items()} for m, by in s.items()}
        return json.dumps({"headline": self.headline, "passed": self.passed, "wins": self.wins,
                           "literal_wins": self.literal_wins, "failed": self.failed,
                           "primary": {"typed": table(self.primary),
                                       "untyped": table(self.primary_untyped)},
                           "typed": table(self.scores), "untyped": table(self.untyped),
                           "touches": self.touches, "scorable": sorted(self.scorable),
                           "scorable_per_type": self.counts(True),
                           "mutants_per_type": self.counts(False)},
                          ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)


def _baselines(report_behaviours, ctx: LoopContext, theta, canon_ids):
    violated = {b.run_key: ctx.lens.violations(b) for b in report_behaviours}
    return (b2_report(report_behaviours, violated, theta, canon_ids),
            b3_report(report_behaviours, violated))


def _defects(defects: Sequence[Defect]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [asdict(d) for d in defects]


def _runs(report: Report) -> dict[str, int]:
    return {"population": report.runs, "probe": report.probe_runs,
            "ddmin": report.ddmin_runs, "executed": report.executed}


def _keys(keys: frozenset[tuple[str, str, tuple[str, ...]]]) -> list[list[Any]]:
    """Ambiguity identities (type, step, fields), sorted, as JSON."""
    return [[t, step, list(fields)] for t, step, fields in sorted(keys)]


def delta_shift(base: Report, report: Report) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Each item's difficulty δ on the mutant minus on the base (spec v2 §3.9): a shift
    shows where the mutant changed who acts, which abstention assumes it does not."""
    return {item: row["delta"] - base.items[item]["delta"]
            for item, row in sorted(report.items.items()) if item in base.items}


def _mutant_line(
    m: Mutant,
    found: dict[str, list[Defect]] | None,
    bases: dict[str, list[Defect]],
    report: Report | None,
    failure: str | None,
    touched: int = 0,
    base: Report | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    matches = None
    if found is not None:
        matches = {
            method: {
                ("typed" if typed else "untyped"): [
                    [d.type, list(d.location)]
                    for d in match_findings(found[method], m.truth, bases[method],
                                            typed=typed)[1]
                ]
                for typed in (True, False)
            }
            for method in found
        }
    return {
        "base": False, "mutant": m.id, "operator": m.operator, "site": m.site,
        "type": m.truth.type,
        "truth": {"location": sorted(m.truth.location), "kind": m.truth.location_kind},
        "defects": None if found is None else {k: _defects(v) for k, v in found.items()},
        "matches": matches,
        "runs": None if report is None else _runs(report),
        # before the differential, and the base noise it removed (spec §11 item 9)
        "ambiguity_keys": None if report is None else _keys(report.ambiguity_keys),
        "ambiguity_dropped": None if report is None else _keys(report.ambiguity_dropped),
        "touches": touched, "scorable": touched >= SCORABLE_K,
        "unobservable": None if report is None else _defects(report.unobservable),
        "delta_shift": None if report is None or base is None else delta_shift(base, report),
        "failure": failure,
    }


def run_gate(
    config: GateConfig,
    bench: BenchLike,
    mutants: Sequence[Mutant],
    *,
    mutants_out: Path | None = None,
) -> GateResult:
    """mutants_out, if given, receives one JSON line for the base and one per mutant,
    written as each finishes."""
    config.check_population()
    cfg = config.specdx()
    base_ctx = bench.loop_context(bench.base, cfg)
    # The base report is the differential's reference: its ambiguities are compared on
    # step and fields, before localisation, so localising them would be wasted runs.
    base = run_specdx(bench.base, base_ctx, dataclasses.replace(cfg, localize=False))
    # Scorability comes from the base runs alone, before any mutant runs (spec v2 §4.1).
    touched = touch_counts({m.id: m.truth.observed_at for m in mutants}, base.behaviours,
                           base_ctx.lens)
    canon_ids = set(base_ctx.canon)
    base_b2, base_b3 = _baselines(base.behaviours, base_ctx, base.theta, canon_ids)
    bases = {"specdx": base.defects, "b2": base_b2, "b3": base_b3}
    reports: dict[str, dict[str, list[Defect]]] = {"specdx": {}, "b2": {}, "b3": {}}
    truths: dict[str, GroundTruth] = {}
    failed: dict[str, str] = {}
    lines = None
    if mutants_out is not None:
        mutants_out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        lines = mutants_out.open("w", encoding="utf-8")

    def write(line: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if lines is not None:
            lines.write(json.dumps(line, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
            lines.flush()

    try:
        write({"base": True, "variant": base.variant,
               "defects": {k: _defects(v) for k, v in bases.items()}, "runs": _runs(base),
               "ambiguity_keys": _keys(base.ambiguity_keys),
               "unobservable": _defects(base.unobservable), "theta": base.theta,
               "kappa": base.kappa, "items": base.items, "touches": touched})
        for m in mutants:
            report: Report | None = None
            try:
                ctx = bench.loop_context(m.variant, cfg)
                report = run_specdx(m.variant, ctx, cfg, base=base)
            except INFRASTRUCTURE_ERRORS:
                raise
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - one broken mutant must not stop the gate
                failed[m.id] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                write(_mutant_line(m, None, bases, report, failed[m.id], touched[m.id], base))
                continue
            if report.behaviours and all(b.status == "error" for b in report.behaviours):
                failed[m.id] = report.behaviours[0].error
                write(_mutant_line(m, None, bases, report, failed[m.id], touched[m.id], base))
                continue
            truths[m.id] = m.truth
            reports["specdx"][m.id] = report.defects
            reports["b2"][m.id], reports["b3"][m.id] = _baselines(
                report.behaviours, ctx, report.theta, canon_ids
            )
            found = {n: reports[n][m.id] for n in reports}
            write(_mutant_line(m, found, bases, report, None, touched[m.id], base))
    finally:
        if lines is not None:
            lines.close()
    scores = {n: evaluate_reports(reports[n], truths, bases[n]) for n in reports}
    untyped = {n: evaluate_reports(reports[n], truths, bases[n], typed=False) for n in reports}
    keep = scorable(touched)

    def primary_of(typed: bool) -> dict[str, dict[str, Prf]]:
        return {n: evaluate_reports({k: v for k, v in reports[n].items() if k in keep},
                                    truths, bases[n], typed=typed) for n in reports}

    result = GateResult(scores, untyped, False, {}, failed, literal_wins(scores),
                        primary_of(True), primary_of(False), touched,
                        {m.id: m.truth.type for m in mutants})
    passed, wins = gate_verdict(result.primary, result.primary_untyped,
                                scorable=result.counts(True), min_scorable=MIN_SCORABLE)
    return dataclasses.replace(result, passed=passed and not failed, wins=wins)


def gate_markdown(result: GateResult) -> str:
    def f1(table: dict[str, dict[str, Prf]], method: str, t: str) -> str:
        return f"{table[method][t].f1:.2f}"

    def win(w: dict[str, bool], t: str) -> str:
        return "yes" if w.get(t) else "no"

    lines = [f"# P1b gate v2: {result.headline}", ""]
    if result.failed:
        lines += [f"{len(result.failed)} mutant(s) could not run, so no verdict is given.", ""]
    scorable_n, all_n = result.counts(True), result.counts(False)
    lines += [
        "## Verdict (primary table: scorable mutants)",
        "",
        "The gate needs 3 wins of 4 (spec §4.8, §11 item 12, v2 §4.2). Contradiction and "
        "hidden: SpecDx's typed F1 must exceed both baselines' untyped F1, since the "
        "baselines cannot assign a type. Ambiguity and weak: the baselines report clauses, "
        "which can never locate those truths, so SpecDx's typed F1 must reach "
        f"{ABSOLUTE_BAR:.2f}. A type with fewer than {MIN_SCORABLE} scorable mutants is a "
        "loss.",
        "",
        "| type | scorable | rule | SpecDx typed | B2 untyped | B3 untyped | SpecDx untyped | "
        "SpecDx wins |",
        "|---|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|",
    ]
    for t in DEFECT_TYPES:
        lines.append(
            f"| {t} | {scorable_n[t]} of {all_n[t]} | {gate_rule(t)} | "
            f"{f1(result.primary, 'specdx', t)} | {f1(result.primary_untyped, 'b2', t)} | "
            f"{f1(result.primary_untyped, 'b3', t)} | "
            f"{f1(result.primary_untyped, 'specdx', t)} | {win(result.wins, t)} |"
        )
    lines += [
        "",
        "## Secondary table: every mutant",
        "",
        "| type | excluded (not scorable) | SpecDx typed | B2 untyped | B3 untyped | "
        "SpecDx untyped |",
        "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|",
    ]
    for t in DEFECT_TYPES:
        lines.append(
            f"| {t} | {all_n[t] - scorable_n[t]} | {f1(result.scores, 'specdx', t)} | "
            f"{f1(result.untyped, 'b2', t)} | {f1(result.untyped, 'b3', t)} | "
            f"{f1(result.untyped, 'specdx', t)} |"
        )
    lines += [
        "",
        "## Literal comparison (typed against typed, every mutant), for transparency only",
        "",
        "| type | SpecDx typed | B2 typed | B3 typed | SpecDx wins |",
        "|---|---:|---:|---:|---|",
    ]
    for t in DEFECT_TYPES:
        lines.append(
            f"| {t} | {f1(result.scores, 'specdx', t)} | {f1(result.scores, 'b2', t)} | "
            f"{f1(result.scores, 'b3', t)} | {win(result.literal_wins, t)} |"
        )
    lines += ["", "## Scorability (base runs touching each mutant's truth targets)", "",
              "| mutant | type | base behaviours touching | scorable |", "|---|---|---:|---|"]
    lines += [f"| {mid} | {result.types[mid]} | {n} | "
              f"{'yes' if mid in result.scorable else 'no'} |"
              for mid, n in sorted(result.touches.items())]
    if result.failed:
        lines += ["", "## Mutants that could not run", ""]
        lines += [f"- `{k}`: {v}" for k, v in sorted(result.failed.items())]
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
```

---

### Task 13: The capability screen and `specdx screen`

Spec v2 §2.2–§2.3. The screen measures the capability to act, never agreement with C.
- **Runs.** Each candidate runs the base specification on canon scenario `happy`, Tell variants {0, 1, 2} × seeds {1, 2, 3}: 9 runs through the harness.
- **Act rate:** the fraction of (run, interpreter step) pairs with aᵦₛ = 1.
- **Valid-call rate:** the valid calls over all tool calls, from `Behaviour.harness` (Task 8).
- **Admission:** act rate ≥ 0.6 and valid-call rate ≥ 0.8.
- **Population.** Admitted models are ranked by act rate, then valid-call rate, then name. The screen keeps at most 2 per family and 4 in all, and needs 2 families. Below that, the command writes STOP and exits with status 3, and the thresholds are never lowered.
- **Not installed.** A candidate Ollama does not list is recorded as `not installed` and is not admitted.
- **Profiles.** `load_profile` reads a hardware profile, by name under `experiments/profiles/` or by path. The four profile files ship in Task 15.

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/eval/screen.py`
- Modify: `specdx/cli.py` (the `screen` subcommand)
- Test: `tests/eval/test_screen.py` (new), `tests/test_cli.py` (help list, appended test)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3's `SpecLens.acts` (through `ctx.lens`), Task 8's `Behaviour.harness`, `Runner.model_digest` (an attribute of the Runner), and Task 12's `load_population` (tests).
- Produces (used by Tasks 14, 15, 17, 18):
  - in `specdx.eval.screen`: `SCREEN_SCENARIO = "happy"`, `SCREEN_VARIANTS = (0, 1, 2)`, `SCREEN_SEEDS = (1, 2, 3)`, `ACT_RATE_MIN = 0.6`, `VALID_RATE_MIN = 0.8`, `PER_FAMILY = 2`, `MAX_MODELS = 4`, `MIN_FAMILIES = 2` and `PROFILES`;
  - `Profile(name, workers, ollama_num_parallel, candidates: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]])` and `load_profile(name_or_path: str) -> Profile`;
  - `ScreenResult(model, family, runs, act_rate, valid_rate, note="")`, with `.admitted`;
  - `Screen(results, population)`, with `.stop` and `.to_json()`. The JSON holds `population`, `stop`, `rules` and `results`, which Task 12's `load_population` reads;
  - `screen_model(...)`, `choose_population(results) -> dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]`, `run_screen(runner, variant, lens, canon, candidates) -> Screen` and `screen_markdown(screen, profile) -> str`;
  - CLI: `specdx screen --profile NAME|PATH --out PREFIX` writes `PREFIX.md` and `PREFIX.json`, and exits 0, or 3 on STOP.

`tests/eval/test_screen.py`:
```python
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from specdx.eval.pilot import load_population
from specdx.eval.screen import (
    ScreenResult,
    choose_population,
    load_profile,
    run_screen,
    screen_markdown,
)
from specdx.infer.act import Act
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus
from specdx.types import Behaviour, Scenario, State
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant

BASE = SpecVariant("base", Path("."), TellCorpus([]))
CANON = {"happy": Scenario("happy", 15, 15, 0), "grn12": Scenario("grn12", 15, 12, 0)}
# model -> (acted steps of 9, tool calls, valid calls) for each of its runs
PLAN = {"good": (7, 10, 9), "edge": (6, 10, 8), "sloppy": (9, 10, 7), "idle": (2, 4, 4)}


class _Runner:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[tuple[str, str, int, int, str]] = []

    def model_digest(self, model: str) -> str:
        if model == "absent":
            raise KeyError(f"Ollama does not list model {model!r}")
        return f"d-{model}"

    def run(self, variant, scenario, interp, suffix="", *, kind="population"):
        self.calls.append((variant.name, scenario.id, interp.variant, interp.seed, kind))
        acted, calls, valid = PLAN[interp.model]
        counts = {"nudges": 0, "parsed_from_text": 0, "tool_calls": calls, "valid_calls": valid}
        return Behaviour(f"{interp.key}", interp, scenario, State({}, {"acted": str(acted)}),
                         harness={"step": counts})


class _Lens:
    def acts(self, b):
        n = int(b.state.derived["acted"])
        return [Act(f"s|{i}", b.run_key, b.interpreter.model, i < n) for i in range(9)]


def test_each_candidate_runs_nine_times_on_happy_and_is_measured() -> None:
    runner = _Runner()
    screen = run_screen(runner, BASE, _Lens(), CANON, {"a": ("good", "edge"), "b": ("sloppy",),
                                                       "c": ("idle", "absent")})
    results = {r.model: r for r in screen.results}
    assert sorted({c[:2] for c in runner.calls}) == [("base", "happy")]
    assert {c[4] for c in runner.calls} == {"screen"} and len(runner.calls) == 36
    assert results["good"].runs == 9 and results["good"].act_rate == pytest.approx(7 / 9)
    assert results["good"].valid_rate == pytest.approx(0.9)
    assert results["edge"].admitted  # 6/9 and 0.8 exactly: the thresholds are inclusive
    assert not results["sloppy"].admitted  # acts, but 0.7 of its calls are valid
    assert not results["idle"].admitted
    assert results["absent"].note == "not installed" and not results["absent"].admitted
    assert screen.population == {"a": ("good", "edge")} and screen.stop


def _r(model, family, act, valid=1.0):
    return ScreenResult(model, family, 9, act, valid)


def test_the_population_keeps_two_per_family_and_four_in_all_in_rank_order() -> None:
    results = [_r("q1", "qwen", 0.9), _r("q2", "qwen", 0.95), _r("q3", "qwen", 0.99),
               _r("o1", "openai", 0.8), _r("m1", "meta", 0.7, 0.9), _r("m2", "meta", 0.7, 0.95),
               _r("g1", "ibm", 0.65)]
    assert choose_population(results) == {"qwen": ("q3", "q2"), "openai": ("o1",),
                                          "meta": ("m2",)}


def test_ties_break_by_valid_call_rate_then_name() -> None:
    results = [_r("b", "x", 0.7), _r("a", "y", 0.7), _r("c", "z", 0.7, 0.9)]
    assert list(choose_population(results)) == ["y", "x", "z"]


def test_the_screen_json_is_what_the_gate_freezes(tmp_path) -> None:
    runner = _Runner()
    screen = run_screen(runner, BASE, _Lens(), CANON, {"a": ("good",), "b": ("edge",)})
    path = tmp_path / "screen.json"
    path.write_text(screen.to_json(), encoding="utf-8")
    assert not screen.stop and load_population(path) == {"a": ("good",), "b": ("edge",)}
    assert json.loads(path.read_text())["rules"]["act_rate_min"] == 0.6
    assert "| good | a | 9 |" in screen_markdown(screen, "test")


def test_a_profile_loads_by_path(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "p.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps({"profile": "p", "workers": 2, "ollama_num_parallel": 2,
                                "candidates": {"qwen": ["qwen3:14b"]}}), encoding="utf-8")
    profile = load_profile(str(path))
    assert (profile.name, profile.workers, profile.candidates) == ("p", 2, {"qwen": ("qwen3:14b",)})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the screen**

`specdx/eval/screen.py`:
```python
"""The capability screen (spec v2 §2.2): which candidate models enter the population.

It measures the capability to act, never agreement with C: scoring against C would be
circular while the specification under diagnosis may itself be defective. Each candidate
runs the base specification on the canon scenario happy, Tell variants 0-2 x seeds 1-3,
through the harness. A model is admitted with act rate ≥ 0.6 and valid-call rate ≥ 0.8.
The population keeps the best admitted models, at most 2 per family and 4 in all, and
needs 2 families; with fewer, P1b stops and the thresholds are never lowered.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Protocol

from specdx.config import REPO_ROOT
from specdx.infer.act import Act
from specdx.types import Behaviour, InterpreterId, Scenario
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant

SCREEN_SCENARIO = "happy"
SCREEN_VARIANTS: tuple[int, ...] = (0, 1, 2)
SCREEN_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = (1, 2, 3)
ACT_RATE_MIN = 0.6  # spec v2 §3.10
VALID_RATE_MIN = 0.8
PER_FAMILY = 2
MAX_MODELS = 4
MIN_FAMILIES = 2
PROFILES = REPO_ROOT / "experiments" / "profiles"


class ScreenRunner(Protocol):
    def model_digest(self, model: str) -> str: ...

    def run(
        self, variant: SpecVariant, scenario: Scenario, interp: InterpreterId, suffix: str = "",
        *, kind: str = "population",
    ) -> Behaviour: ...


class ActLens(Protocol):
    def acts(self, behaviour: Behaviour) -> list[Act]: ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Profile:
    """A hardware profile (spec v2 §2.3): candidate models by family and the suggested
    parallelism. It only widens the candidate list; the screen decides admission."""

    name: str
    workers: int
    ollama_num_parallel: int
    candidates: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]


def load_profile(name_or_path: str) -> Profile:
    """A profile by name (experiments/profiles/<name>.json) or by path."""
    path = Path(name_or_path)
    if not path.suffix:
        path = PROFILES / f"{name_or_path}.json"
    raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return Profile(raw["profile"], int(raw["workers"]), int(raw["ollama_num_parallel"]),
                   {f: tuple(ms) for f, ms in raw["candidates"].items()})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScreenResult:
    model: str
    family: str
    runs: int
    act_rate: float
    valid_rate: float
    note: str = ""

    @property
    def admitted(self) -> bool:
        return self.act_rate >= ACT_RATE_MIN and self.valid_rate >= VALID_RATE_MIN


@dataclass
class Screen:
    results: list[ScreenResult]
    population: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def stop(self) -> bool:
        """The stop rule: fewer than MIN_FAMILIES families were admitted."""
        return len(self.population) < MIN_FAMILIES

    def to_json(self) -> str:
        return json.dumps({
            "population": {f: list(ms) for f, ms in self.population.items()},
            "stop": self.stop,
            "rules": {"act_rate_min": ACT_RATE_MIN, "valid_rate_min": VALID_RATE_MIN,
                      "per_family": PER_FAMILY, "max_models": MAX_MODELS,
                      "min_families": MIN_FAMILIES, "scenario": SCREEN_SCENARIO,
                      "variants": list(SCREEN_VARIANTS), "seeds": list(SCREEN_SEEDS)},
            "results": [asdict(r) | {"admitted": r.admitted} for r in self.results],
        }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)


def screen_model(
    runner: ScreenRunner, variant: SpecVariant, lens: ActLens, scenario: Scenario,
    model: str, family: str,
) -> ScreenResult:
    """Nine runs of one model; a model Ollama does not list is recorded, not admitted."""
    try:
        runner.model_digest(model)
    except KeyError:
        return ScreenResult(model, family, 0, 0.0, 0.0, "not installed")
    behaviours = [runner.run(variant, scenario, InterpreterId(model, family, v, s), kind="screen")
                  for v in SCREEN_VARIANTS for s in SCREEN_SEEDS]
    acts = [a for b in behaviours for a in lens.acts(b)]
    counts = [c for b in behaviours for c in b.harness.values()]
    calls = sum(c.get("tool_calls", 0) for c in counts)
    valid = sum(c.get("valid_calls", 0) for c in counts)
    act_rate = sum(a.acted for a in acts) / len(acts) if acts else 0.0
    return ScreenResult(model, family, len(behaviours), act_rate, valid / calls if calls else 0.0)


def choose_population(results: Sequence[ScreenResult]) -> dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]:
    """Admitted models by act rate, then valid-call rate, then name: at most PER_FAMILY
    per family and MAX_MODELS in all."""
    ranked = sorted((r for r in results if r.admitted),
                    key=lambda r: (-r.act_rate, -r.valid_rate, r.model))
    chosen: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    per_family: Counter[str] = Counter()
    for r in ranked:
        if per_family[r.family] >= PER_FAMILY or sum(per_family.values()) >= MAX_MODELS:
            continue
        per_family[r.family] += 1
        chosen.setdefault(r.family, []).append(r.model)
    return {f: tuple(ms) for f, ms in chosen.items()}


def run_screen(
    runner: ScreenRunner, variant: SpecVariant, lens: ActLens, canon: Mapping[str, Scenario],
    candidates: Mapping[str, Sequence[str]],
) -> Screen:
    scenario = canon[SCREEN_SCENARIO]
    results = [screen_model(runner, variant, lens, scenario, m, f)
               for f, models in candidates.items() for m in models]
    return Screen(results, choose_population(results))


def screen_markdown(screen: Screen, profile: str) -> str:
    chosen = {m for ms in screen.population.values() for m in ms}
    lines = [
        f"# P1b capability screen ({profile})", "",
        f"Base specification, scenario `{SCREEN_SCENARIO}`, Tell variants {list(SCREEN_VARIANTS)}"
        f" x seeds {list(SCREEN_SEEDS)}. Admission: act rate ≥ {ACT_RATE_MIN} and valid-call "
        f"rate ≥ {VALID_RATE_MIN}. Population: at most {PER_FAMILY} per family and "
        f"{MAX_MODELS} in all, at least {MIN_FAMILIES} families.", "",
        "| model | family | runs | act rate | valid-call rate | admitted | chosen | note |",
        "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|---|",
    ]
    for r in screen.results:
        lines.append(f"| {r.model} | {r.family} | {r.runs} | {r.act_rate:.3f} | "
                     f"{r.valid_rate:.3f} | {'yes' if r.admitted else 'no'} | "
                     f"{'yes' if r.model in chosen else ''} | {r.note} |")
    lines += ["", "## Population", ""]
    if screen.stop:
        lines.append(f"STOP: fewer than {MIN_FAMILIES} families were admitted. P1b stops and "
                     "reports; the thresholds are never lowered.")
    else:
        lines += [f"- {family}: {', '.join(models)}"
                  for family, models in screen.population.items()]
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
```

---

### Task 14: Parallel workers (`--workers N`) with a byte-identical report

Spec v2 §5.1. The independent runs of one loop step go out as one batch: the pilot, the committee escalation, the DirectedProbe attempts for one candidate, and the runs of one ddmin test.
- **Budgets** are charged before a batch is dispatched.
- **Workers.** Each worker owns a database `<name>_w<i>` in the same server, created on demand, with its own `PackDatabase` and advisory lock.
- **Dispatch and collection.** `ParallelRunner` dispatches each batch grouped by model and collects results in submission order. It is the run cache's only writer. Inference stays sequential.
- **One worker.** The plain `Runner` keeps the main database and runs the batch in order, with the same charging. The report of 1 and 3 workers is therefore byte-identical, and a database test enforces it with scripted executors.
- **DirectedProbe.** It now charges and dispatches all of a candidate's attempts at once (planning resolution 11), so a probe that refutes costs every attempt.

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/parallel.py`
- Modify:
  - `specdx/observe/run.py` (`RunRequest`; `Runner.key`, `.execute`, `.run_batch`);
  - `specdx/active/loop.py` (batches);
  - `specdx/active/refute.py` (`DirectedProbe.search` batch);
  - `specdx/workbench.py` (workers, ddmin batch);
  - `specdx/eval/screen.py` (batch);
  - `specdx/cli.py` (`--workers`).
- Test:
  - `tests/test_parallel.py` (new);
  - `run_batch` in the fakes of `tests/active/test_loop.py`, `tests/active/test_refute.py`, `tests/eval/test_pilot.py` and `tests/eval/test_screen.py`;
  - `tests/active/test_refute.py` (memo test), `tests/test_cli.py` (screen test).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RunCache.get`/`put`, `PackDatabase(url)`, Task 10's loop, and Task 13's screen.
- Produces (used by Tasks 15, 18):
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) RunRequest(variant, scenario, interp, suffix: str = "", kind: str = "population")` in `specdx.observe.run`;
  - `Runner.key(variant, scenario, interp, suffix="") -> str`, `Runner.execute(request, key) -> Behaviour` (never touches the cache) and `Runner.run_batch(requests) -> list[Behaviour]`. `Runner.run` is unchanged;
  - in `specdx.parallel`: `Worker` (Protocol), `worker_url(url, index) -> str`, `ensure_database(url, name) -> None` and `ParallelRunner(workers, cache)`, with `.run`, `.run_batch`, `.executed`, `.model_digest` and `.close()`;
  - every `RunnerLike` protocol (loop, refute, screen) requires `run_batch`;
  - `Workbench.open(..., workers: int = 1)`, with `bench.runner: Runner | ParallelRunner`;
  - CLI `--workers N` on `run`, `gate` and `screen`.

```python
import psycopg
import pytest
from psycopg.conninfo import conninfo_to_dict

from specdx.active.loop import run_specdx
from specdx.eval.pilot import GateConfig

from specdx.parallel import ParallelRunner, worker_url
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus

from specdx.variant import SpecVariant
from specdx.workbench import Workbench
from tests.test_end_to_end import Deviant

VARIANT = SpecVariant("v", Path("."), TellCorpus([]))
S = Scenario("s", 15, 12, 0)


class _Worker:
    """Runs take a random while; every execution is logged in the order it starts."""

    model_digest = staticmethod(lambda model: f"d-{model}")

    def __init__(self, started: list[str], rng: random.Random, broken: str = "") -> None:
        self.started, self.rng, self.broken = started, rng, broken

    def key(self, variant, scenario, interp, suffix=""):
        return f"{scenario.id}|{interp.key}|{suffix}"

    def execute(self, request, key):
        self.started.append(key)
        time.sleep(self.rng.random() / 50)
        if request.interp.model == self.broken:
            raise InfrastructureError("Ollama failed 5 times")
        return Behaviour(key, request.interp, request.scenario, State({}))


def _requests(*models: str) -> list[RunRequest]:
    return [RunRequest(VARIANT, S, InterpreterId(m, "f", 0, i)) for i, m in enumerate(models)]


def test_worker_databases_live_in_the_same_server() -> None:
    info = conninfo_to_dict(worker_url("postgresql://u:p@localhost:55434/tonomyx", 2))
    assert (info["dbname"], info["port"], info["host"]) == ("tonomyx_w2", "55434", "localhost")


def test_a_batch_comes_back_in_submission_order_and_is_cached_by_one_writer(tmp_path) -> None:
    started: list[str] = []
    cache = RunCache(tmp_path / "runs.jsonl")
    runner = ParallelRunner([_Worker(started, random.Random(i)) for i in range(3)], cache)
    requests = _requests("b", "a", "b", "c", "a", "c")
    out = runner.run_batch(requests)
    assert [b.interpreter for b in out] == [r.interp for r in requests]
    lines = (tmp_path / "runs.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    assert [json.loads(x)["run_key"] for x in lines] == [b.run_key for b in out]
    again = runner.run_batch(requests[:2] + requests[:1])
    assert runner.executed == 6 and [b.run_key for b in again] == [
        out[0].run_key, out[1].run_key, out[0].run_key]


def test_one_worker_executes_the_batch_grouped_by_model(tmp_path) -> None:
    started: list[str] = []
    runner = ParallelRunner([_Worker(started, random.Random(0))], RunCache(tmp_path / "r.jsonl"))
    runner.run_batch(_requests("b", "a", "b", "a"))
    assert [key.split("|")[1] for key in started] == ["a", "a", "b", "b"]


def test_an_infrastructure_failure_stops_the_batch_and_is_never_cached(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "r.jsonl"
    runner = ParallelRunner([_Worker([], random.Random(i), broken="x") for i in range(2)],
                            RunCache(path))
    with pytest.raises(InfrastructureError):
        runner.run_batch(_requests("a", "x", "b"))
    cached = [json.loads(x)["interpreter"]["model"] for x in path.read_text().splitlines()]
    assert cached == ["a"]  # collected before the failure; nothing after it is written


CONFIG = GateConfig(

def _report(work_dir: Path, workers: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
    bench = Workbench.open(
        work_dir, workers=workers, model_digest=lambda m: f"digest-{m}",
        factory=lambda i, c, s: Deviant() if i.model == "deviant" else reference_executor(),
    )
    config = CONFIG.specdx()
    report = run_specdx(bench.base, bench.loop_context(bench.base, config), config)
    return report.to_json(), report.executed


@pytest.mark.db
def test_three_workers_give_a_byte_identical_report(db, tmp_path) -> None:
    one, executed_one = _report(tmp_path / "one", 1)
    three, executed_three = _report(tmp_path / "three", 3)
    assert one == three and executed_one == executed_three > 0
    with psycopg.connect(db.url) as conn:
        names = {r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT datname FROM pg_database").fetchall()}
    assert {"tonomyx_w0", "tonomyx_w1", "tonomyx_w2"} <= names
```
