- [ ] **Step 3: Split the Runner into key, execute and run_batch**

In `specdx/observe/run.py`, add `from dataclasses import dataclass` after `from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence`, and replace the whole `class Runner` with:
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRequest:
    """One run a caller wants: what run_batch takes. kind (population, probe, ddmin or
    screen) only labels the log line; it is not in the run key."""

    variant: SpecVariant
    scenario: Scenario
    interp: InterpreterId
    suffix: str = ""
    kind: str = "population"


class Runner:
    def __init__(
        self,
        db: PackDatabase,
        cache: RunCache,
        factory: ExecutorFactory,
        steps: Sequence[WorkStep],
        *,
        model_digest: Callable[[str], str] | None = None,
        context_digest: str = "",
    ) -> None:
        self.db, self.cache, self.factory, self.steps = db, cache, factory, tuple(steps)
        self.model_digest = model_digest or (lambda model: model)
        self.context_digest = context_digest
        self._steps_digest = hashlib.sha256(repr(self.steps).encode()).hexdigest()
        self.executed = 0

    def key(
        self, variant: SpecVariant, scenario: Scenario, interp: InterpreterId, suffix: str = ""
    ) -> str:
        return run_key(
            variant.digest(), self._steps_digest, self.context_digest,
            json.dumps(scenario.params(), sort_keys=True), interp.key,
            suffix, self.model_digest(interp.model),
        )

    def run(
        self, variant: SpecVariant, scenario: Scenario, interp: InterpreterId, suffix: str = "",
        *, kind: str = "population",
    ) -> Behaviour:
        key = self.key(variant, scenario, interp, suffix)
        cached = self.cache.get(key)
        if cached is not None:
            # The key holds the parameters, not the scenario's name or the family: the
            # caller's own scenario and interpreter are the ones that ran here.
            return dataclasses.replace(cached, scenario=scenario, interpreter=interp)
        self.executed += 1
        behaviour = self.execute(RunRequest(variant, scenario, interp, suffix, kind), key)
        self.cache.put(behaviour)
        return behaviour

    def run_batch(self, requests: Sequence[RunRequest]) -> list[Behaviour]:
        """One after another, in order: the sequential form of ParallelRunner.run_batch."""
        return [self.run(r.variant, r.scenario, r.interp, r.suffix, kind=r.kind)
                for r in requests]

    def execute(self, request: RunRequest, key: str) -> Behaviour:
        """Make one run on this Runner's database, outside the cache: the caller stores it.
        Infrastructure failures are raised, never returned as behaviour."""
        variant, scenario, interp, suffix = (request.variant, request.scenario, request.interp,
                                             request.suffix)
        digest = self.model_digest(interp.model)
        started = time.monotonic()
        executor: DeExecutor | None = None
        try:
            settings = self.db.use(variant.pack)
            executor = self.factory(interp, variant.corpus, suffix)
            if hasattr(executor, "infra_failure"):
                executor.infra_failure = None
            with self.db.isolated() as conn:
                runtime = build_runtime(conn, settings, executor=executor,
                                        clock=FixedClock(RUN_CLOCK))
                case = run_case(runtime, scenario, self.steps)
                # DeRuntime turns an Ollama outage into a step error: read the side channel.
                failure = getattr(executor, "infra_failure", None)
                if failure:
                    raise InfrastructureError(failure)
                state = capture_state(conn, runtime.pack, case.statuses)
            behaviour = Behaviour(
                run_key=key, interpreter=interp, scenario=scenario, state=state,
                refused=tuple(case.refused), claims=dict(case.claims), model_digest=digest,
                harness=dict(case.harness), spec=variant.name,
            )
        except (psycopg.OperationalError, psycopg.InterfaceError, InfrastructureError):
            raise
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a failed run is data, never dropped
            failure = getattr(executor, "infra_failure", None)
            if failure:  # Ollama had already failed: whatever broke next is not behaviour
                raise InfrastructureError(failure) from exc
            behaviour = Behaviour(
                run_key=key, interpreter=interp, scenario=scenario, state=State.empty(),
                status="error", error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", model_digest=digest,
                spec=variant.name,
            )
        log.info("run %s %s %s %s %s %.1f s", variant.name, scenario.id, interp.key,
                 request.kind, behaviour.status, time.monotonic() - started)
        return behaviour
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the worker pool**

`specdx/parallel.py`:
```python
"""Parallel workers (spec v2 §5.1): the independent runs of one loop step, at once.

Worker i owns database <name>_w<i> in the same PostgreSQL server, created on demand, with
its own PackDatabase and advisory lock, so per-run isolation is unchanged. The caller
charges budgets before it dispatches a batch. Runs are dispatched grouped by model, to
limit model swaps on the GPU, and collected in submission order; this runner is the run
cache's only writer. A report is therefore byte-identical to a sequential run's.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import queue
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from typing import Protocol

import psycopg
from psycopg import sql
from psycopg.conninfo import conninfo_to_dict, make_conninfo

from specdx.interpreters.cache import RunCache
from specdx.observe.run import RunRequest
from specdx.types import Behaviour, InterpreterId, Scenario
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant


class Worker(Protocol):
    model_digest: Callable[[str], str]

    def key(
        self, variant: SpecVariant, scenario: Scenario, interp: InterpreterId, suffix: str = ""
    ) -> str: ...

    def execute(self, request: RunRequest, key: str) -> Behaviour: ...


def worker_url(url: str, index: int) -> str:
    """The database URL of worker `index`: the same server, database <name>_w<index>."""
    name = conninfo_to_dict(url).get("dbname") or "tonomyx"
    return make_conninfo(url, dbname=f"{name}_w{index}")


def ensure_database(url: str, name: str) -> None:
    """Create database `name` in url's server unless it exists (url's own database is the
    one connected to)."""
    with psycopg.connect(url, autocommit=True) as conn:
        found = conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM pg_database WHERE datname = %s", (name,)).fetchone()
        if found is None:
            conn.execute(sql.SQL("CREATE DATABASE {}").format(sql.Identifier(name)))


class ParallelRunner:
    """The Runner interface over several workers."""

    def __init__(self, workers: Sequence[Worker], cache: RunCache) -> None:
        if not workers:
            raise ValueError("a ParallelRunner needs at least one worker")
        self.workers, self.cache = list(workers), cache
        self.model_digest = self.workers[0].model_digest
        self.executed = 0
        self._free: queue.Queue[Worker] = queue.Queue()
        for worker in self.workers:
            self._free.put(worker)
        self._pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(self.workers),
                                        thread_name_prefix="specdx-worker")

    def _execute(self, request: RunRequest, key: str) -> Behaviour:
        worker = self._free.get()
        try:
            return worker.execute(request, key)
        finally:
            self._free.put(worker)

    def run(
        self, variant: SpecVariant, scenario: Scenario, interp: InterpreterId, suffix: str = "",
        *, kind: str = "population",
    ) -> Behaviour:
        return self.run_batch([RunRequest(variant, scenario, interp, suffix, kind)])[0]

    def run_batch(self, requests: Sequence[RunRequest]) -> list[Behaviour]:
        keys = [self.workers[0].key(r.variant, r.scenario, r.interp, r.suffix) for r in requests]
        found: dict[str, Behaviour] = {}
        todo: dict[str, RunRequest] = {}
        for request, key in zip(requests, keys, strict=True):
            cached = self.cache.get(key)
            if cached is not None:
                found[key] = cached
            elif key not in todo:
                todo[key] = request
        # sorted() is stable: within a model, runs keep their submission order.
        dispatch = sorted(todo, key=lambda k: todo[k].interp.model)
        futures = {k: self._pool.submit(self._execute, todo[k], k) for k in dispatch}
        for key in todo:  # submission order: one writer, the same cache file every time
            try:
                behaviour = futures[key].result()
            except BaseException:
                for future in futures.values():
                    future.cancel()
                raise
            self.cache.put(behaviour)
            self.executed += 1
            found[key] = behaviour
        return [dataclasses.replace(found[k], scenario=r.scenario, interpreter=r.interp)
                for r, k in zip(requests, keys, strict=True)]

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._pool.shutdown(wait=True)
```

```python
            fresh += ctx.runner.run_batch([RunRequest(variant, scenario, i) for i in batch])
```

In `specdx/active/refute.py`, add `from specdx.observe.run import RunRequest` after `from specdx.db import PackDatabase`, and replace `RunnerLike` with:
```python
class RunnerLike(Protocol):
    def run_batch(self, requests: Sequence[RunRequest]) -> list[Behaviour]: ...
```
Then replace `DirectedProbe.search` with:
```python
    def search(
        self, targets: frozenset[str], scenario: Scenario, allowed: frozenset[str]
    ) -> tuple[Behaviour | None, int]:
        """A witness behaviour or None, and how many probe runs were requested. Every
        attempt for the candidate is charged, then dispatched as one batch (spec v2 §5.1);
        the witness is the first in submission order."""
        items = "; ".join(describe(self.clauses[c], scenario) for c in sorted(targets))
        suffix = GOAL.format(items=items)
        requests: list[RunRequest] = []
        for interp in self.interpreters[: self.width]:
            for attempt in range(self.attempts):
                if self.budget is not None and not self.budget.take("probe"):
                    break
                requests.append(RunRequest(self.variant, scenario,
                                           dataclasses.replace(interp, seed=100 + attempt),
                                           suffix, "probe"))
            else:
                continue
            break
        for b in self.runner.run_batch(requests):
            if b.status != "ok":
                continue
            bad = self.clauses.violations(b.state, scenario)
            if not (bad & targets) and bad <= allowed:
                return b, len(requests)
        return None, len(requests)
```

```python
                """Spec v2 §3.6: the re-run (one behaviour per competent model and variant,
                seed 1) still yields two classes that differ on the ambiguity's fields. The
                test's runs are charged, then dispatched as one batch."""
```

### Task 15: `specdx doctor`, `specdx cache-info`, hardware profiles and several Ollama hosts

Spec v2 §2.3, §5.1 (Ollama hosts) and §5.2.
- **Several hosts.** `SPECDX_OLLAMA_URL` accepts a comma-separated list. Worker i uses host i modulo the number of hosts. Every host must report the same digest for each model tag: the run-key lookup raises on a mismatch, and on a model missing from any host.
- **`doctor`** checks:
  - Python and the packages;
  - PostgreSQL: another project's port is refused before any connection, then reachability;
  - the vendor script, or a warning without a TonomyX checkout (`TONOMYX_SRC`);
  - every Ollama host: reachability, each candidate model, native tool calling and digest agreement;
  - free disk, and VRAM from a local `nvidia-smi`.

  It recommends a profile, prints the pull commands, and exits 1 on any failing check.
- **`cache-info`** prints the cached runs per model and digest, and per specification.
- **Profiles.** Four profiles ship in `experiments/profiles/`.

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/doctor.py`, `experiments/profiles/gpu16.json`, `gpu24.json`, `gpu48.json`, `gpu80.json`
- Modify:
  - `specdx/config.py` (`OLLAMA_URLS`);
  - `specdx/workbench.py` (`ollama_digests`, `Workbench.open(hosts=)`, `llm_factory(base_url=)`);
  - `specdx/interpreters/cache.py` (`__iter__`, `cache_summary`);
  - `specdx/cli.py` (`doctor`, `cache-info`);
  - `scripts/verify_vendor.sh` (`TONOMYX_SRC`).
- Test: `tests/test_doctor.py` (new), `tests/test_ollama_digests.py`, `tests/eval/test_screen.py`, `tests/interpreters/test_cache.py`, `tests/test_parallel.py`, `tests/test_cli.py` (appends)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 13's `load_profile`; Task 14's `Workbench.open(workers=)` and `ParallelRunner.workers`; `PackDatabase(url)`, which validates the port.
- Produces (used by Tasks 16–18):
  - `OLLAMA_URLS: tuple[str, ...]`; `OLLAMA_URL` is its first entry;
  - `ollama_digests(base_urls: str | Sequence[str] = OLLAMA_URLS, transport=None)`;
  - `Workbench.open(..., workers=1, hosts: Sequence[str] = OLLAMA_URLS)` and `Workbench.llm_factory(interp, corpus, suffix, base_url=OLLAMA_URL)`;
  - `RunCache.__iter__` and `cache_summary(cache: RunCache) -> str`;
  - in `specdx.doctor`: `Check(name, status, detail)`, `check_python`, `check_database`, `check_vendor`, `check_ollama(hosts, models, transport) -> tuple[list[Check], dict[str, list[str]]]`, `check_disk`, `read_vram(run) -> tuple[float | None, str]`, `recommend_profile(vram) -> str | None`, `pull_commands(missing, local) -> list[str]` and `run_doctor(profile, *, database_url, hosts, work_dir, transport=None, run=subprocess.run) -> tuple[str, bool]`;
  - CLI `specdx doctor [--profile P]` (exit 0 or 1) and `specdx cache-info`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/test_doctor.py`:
```python
import subprocess

import httpx

from specdx.doctor import (
    check_database,
    check_ollama,
    check_python,
    check_vendor,
    pull_commands,
    read_vram,
    recommend_profile,
    run_doctor,
)


def _ollama(hosts: dict[str, dict[str, str]], tools: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"m:1"})):
    """hosts: host name -> {model tag: digest}; a host absent from the dict is down."""
    def handle(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        host = request.url.host
        if host not in hosts:
            raise httpx.ConnectError("refused", request=request)
        if request.url.path == "/api/tags":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"models": [
                {"name": n, "digest": d} for n, d in hosts[host].items()]})
        model = request.read().decode()
        caps = ["completion", "tools"] if any(t in model for t in tools) else ["completion"]
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"capabilities": caps})
    return httpx.MockTransport(handle)


def _status(checks, name):
    return next(c.status for c in checks if c.name == name)


def test_hosts_that_agree_pass_and_a_missing_model_is_listed_for_pulling() -> None:
    transport = _ollama({"a": {"m:1": "d1", "n:2": "d2"}, "b": {"m:1": "d1"}})
    checks, missing = check_ollama(["http://a", "http://b"], ["m:1", "n:2"], transport)
    assert _status(checks, "digest m:1") == "ok" and _status(checks, "tools m:1 @ http://a") == "ok"
    assert _status(checks, "tools n:2 @ http://a") == "warn"  # no native tool calling
    assert missing == {"http://b": ["n:2"]}
    assert pull_commands(missing, "http://a") == ["OLLAMA_HOST=http://b ollama pull n:2"]
    assert pull_commands({"http://a": ["x"]}, "http://a") == ["ollama pull x"]


def test_hosts_that_disagree_on_a_digest_fail_and_a_down_host_fails() -> None:
    transport = _ollama({"a": {"m:1": "d1"}, "b": {"m:1": "other"}})
    checks, _ = check_ollama(["http://a", "http://b", "http://c"], ["m:1"], transport)
    assert _status(checks, "digest m:1") == "fail"
    assert _status(checks, "ollama http://c") == "fail"


def test_another_projects_port_fails_before_any_connection() -> None:
    check = check_database("postgresql://u:p@localhost:55432/x")
    assert check.status == "fail" and "55434" in check.detail
    assert check_database("postgresql://u:p@localhost:1/x").status == "fail"  # unreachable


def test_the_vendor_check_runs_the_script_or_warns_without_a_source(tmp_path) -> None:
    assert check_vendor(src=tmp_path / "absent").status == "warn"

    def run(argv, **kwargs):
        assert argv[-1].endswith("scripts/verify_vendor.sh")
        assert kwargs["env"]["TONOMYX_SRC"] == str(tmp_path)
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, 0, "vendor/tonomyx matches 34c1d2c", "")

    assert check_vendor(run, tmp_path).status == "ok"


def test_python_below_three_twelve_fails() -> None:
    assert check_python((3, 11))[0].status == "fail"
    assert check_python((3, 13))[0].status == "ok"


def _smi(mib: str, code: int = 0):
    def run(argv, **kwargs):
        if argv[0] != "nvidia-smi":
            return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, 0, "", "")
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, code, mib, "")
    return run


def test_vram_picks_a_profile_and_a_broken_nvidia_smi_is_reported() -> None:
    assert read_vram(_smi("16303\n")) == (16303 / 1024, "16 GiB")
    assert read_vram(_smi("8192\n24564\n"))[0] == 24564 / 1024  # the largest GPU
    broken = read_vram(_smi("Failed to initialize NVML: Driver/library version mismatch\n", 18))
    assert broken == (None, "nvidia-smi failed: Failed to initialize NVML: Driver/library "
                            "version mismatch")
    assert read_vram(_smi("N/A\n"))[0] is None

    def absent(argv, **kwargs):
        raise FileNotFoundError(argv[0])

    assert read_vram(absent) == (None, "no local nvidia-smi")
    assert [recommend_profile(v) for v in (15.9, 24.0, 47.5, 80.0, None)] == [
        "gpu16", "gpu24", "gpu48", "gpu80", None]


def test_the_doctor_reports_every_check_and_its_verdict(tmp_path) -> None:
    transport = _ollama({"a": {"qwen3:14b": "d"}})
    report, healthy = run_doctor("gpu16", database_url="postgresql://u:p@localhost:55433/x",
                                 hosts=["http://a"], work_dir=tmp_path, transport=transport,
                                 run=_smi("16303\n"))
    assert not healthy  # the database check fails on another project's port
    assert "Recommended profile: gpu16" in report and "| postgresql | fail |" in report
    assert "ollama pull llama3.1:8b" in report and "| disk | " in report
```

```python
def test_several_hosts_must_list_a_model_with_the_same_digest() -> None:

                           _hosts({"a": {"m:1": "d"}, "b": {"m:1": "d"}}))

    partial = ollama_digests(["http://a", "http://b"], _hosts({"a": {"m:1": "d"}, "b": {}}))
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="http://b"):
        partial("m:1")
```

In `specdx/workbench.py`:

- Replace `ollama_digests` with:
```python
def ollama_digests(
    base_urls: str | Sequence[str] = OLLAMA_URLS, transport: httpx.BaseTransport | None = None
) -> Callable[[str], str]:
    """Model name → Ollama digest, fetched from every host on first use, so the run cache
    never mixes two builds of one model. An unknown model, an unreachable host, or hosts
    that disagree on a digest are errors."""
    hosts = [base_urls] if isinstance(base_urls, str) else list(base_urls)
    listed: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {}

    def lookup(model: str) -> str:
        if not listed:
            with httpx.Client(transport=transport, timeout=10.0) as client:
                for host in hosts:
                    response = client.get(f"{host}/api/tags")
                    response.raise_for_status()
                    listed[host] = {str(m["name"]): str(m["digest"])
                                    for m in response.json().get("models", [])}
        name = model if ":" in model else f"{model}:latest"
        found = {host: listed[host].get(name) for host in hosts}
        absent = [host for host, digest in found.items() if digest is None]
        if absent:
            raise KeyError(f"Ollama does not list model {model!r} on {', '.join(absent)}")
        if len(set(found.values())) > 1:
            raise ValueError(f"model {model!r} has different digests across hosts: {found}")
        return str(found[hosts[0]])

    return lookup
```
- Give `Workbench.open` the keyword parameter `hosts: Sequence[str] = OLLAMA_URLS` after `workers`, with the docstring `"""workers: with 2 or more, runs go to that many worker databases at once (spec v2 §5.1), worker i on Ollama host i modulo the hosts; symbolic repair keeps the main database."""`. Replace the runner construction (from `digests = …` to the final `return bench`) with:
```python
        digests = model_digest or ollama_digests(hosts)
        context = context_digest(tell_payload(variants), bench.settings)

        def runner(db: PackDatabase, host: str) -> Runner:
            def on_host(i: InterpreterId, c: TellCorpus, s: str) -> DeExecutor:
                return bench.llm_factory(i, c, s, base_url=host)

            return Runner(db, bench.cache, factory or on_host, bench.steps,
                          model_digest=digests, context_digest=context)

        if workers <= 1:
            bench.runner = runner(bench.db, hosts[0])
            return bench
        pool = []
        for index in range(workers):
            url = worker_url(bench.db.url, index)
            ensure_database(bench.db.url, str(conninfo_to_dict(url)["dbname"]))
            pool.append(runner(PackDatabase(url), hosts[index % len(hosts)]))
        bench.runner = ParallelRunner(pool, bench.cache)
        return bench
```

```python
        self, interp: InterpreterId, corpus: TellCorpus, suffix: str, base_url: str = OLLAMA_URL
    ) -> DeExecutor:

            interp.model, build_registry(), corpus, self.variants,
            variant=interp.variant, seed=interp.seed, suffix=suffix, base_url=base_url,
            temperature=float(self.settings["temperature"]),
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The cache summary and the vendor script**

In `specdx/interpreters/cache.py`, add `from collections import Counter` and `from collections.abc import Iterator` after `import hashlib`, and append:
```python
    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[Behaviour]:
        return iter(self._items.values())


def cache_summary(cache: RunCache) -> str:
    """specdx cache-info (spec v2 §5.2): cached runs per model and digest, and per
    specification. Runs cached before P1b do not record their specification."""
    by_model = Counter((b.interpreter.model, b.model_digest, b.status) for b in cache)
    by_spec = Counter(b.spec or "(not recorded: a P1 run)" for b in cache)
    lines = [f"# Run cache: {cache.path} ({len(cache)} runs)", "",
             "| model | digest | status | runs |", "|---|---|---|---:|"]
    lines += [f"| {m} | {d or '(none)'} | {s} | {n} |" for (m, d, s), n in sorted(by_model.items())]
    lines += ["", "| specification | runs |", "|---|---:|"]
    lines += [f"| {spec} | {n} |" for spec, n in sorted(by_spec.items())]
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
```
(`__iter__` is a method of `RunCache`; put it after `__len__`.)

In `scripts/verify_vendor.sh`, replace `SRC=/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX` with `SRC=${TONOMYX_SRC:-/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX}`. This is not `vendor/`: it lets another machine point at its own TonomyX checkout.

- [ ] **Step 5: The doctor and the profiles**

`specdx/doctor.py`:
```python
"""specdx doctor (spec v2 §5.2): is this machine ready to run P1b?

Each check is ok, warn or fail. It checks the Python version and packages, PostgreSQL
(never on another project's port, and reachable), the vendor integrity script, every
Ollama host (reachable, each candidate model present, its native tool calling, and the
same digest on every host), free disk, and VRAM when nvidia-smi is local. It recommends a
hardware profile and prints the commands that pull missing models.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.metadata
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
import psycopg

from specdx.config import REPO_ROOT
from specdx.db import PackDatabase
from specdx.eval.screen import load_profile

PACKAGES: tuple[str, ...] = ("numpy", "httpx", "psycopg", "tonomyx", "specdx")
TONOMYX_SRC = Path(os.environ.get("TONOMYX_SRC", "/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX"))
MIN_FREE_GB = 10.0
# The largest GPU (GiB) each profile is meant for, smallest first; above the last: gpu80.
PROFILE_VRAM: tuple[tuple[str, float], ...] = (("gpu16", 22.0), ("gpu24", 44.0),
                                               ("gpu48", 76.0))

Run = Callable[..., subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Check:
    name: str
    status: str  # "ok", "warn" or "fail"
    detail: str


def check_python(version: tuple[int, int] = (sys.version_info.major, sys.version_info.minor)
                 ) -> list[Check]:
    status = "ok" if version >= (3, 13) else "warn" if version >= (3, 12) else "fail"
    out = [Check("python", status, f"{version[0]}.{version[1]} (3.13 is the tested version)")]
    for name in PACKAGES:
        try:
            out.append(Check(f"package {name}", "ok", importlib.metadata.version(name)))
        except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
            out.append(Check(f"package {name}", "fail", "not installed: run the setup step"))
    found = shutil.which("pdftotext")
    out.append(Check("pdftotext", "ok" if found else "warn",
                     found or "missing (poppler): needed unless work/tell is already built"))
    return out


def check_database(url: str) -> Check:
    try:
        PackDatabase(url)  # refuses another project's port before any connection
        with psycopg.connect(url, connect_timeout=5) as conn:
            conn.execute("SELECT 1")
    except ValueError as exc:
        return Check("postgresql", "fail", str(exc))
    except psycopg.Error as exc:
        return Check("postgresql", "fail", f"unreachable: {exc}".strip())
    return Check("postgresql", "ok", "reachable")


def check_vendor(run: Run = subprocess.run, src: Path = TONOMYX_SRC) -> Check:
    if not src.is_dir():
        return Check("vendor", "warn", f"{src} is absent: vendor/tonomyx cannot be verified "
                                       "here (set TONOMYX_SRC to a TonomyX checkout)")
    done = run(["bash", str(REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "verify_vendor.sh")], capture_output=True,
               text=True, env={**os.environ, "TONOMYX_SRC": str(src)})
    if done.returncode != 0:
        return Check("vendor", "fail", (done.stdout + done.stderr).strip()[-300:])
    return Check("vendor", "ok", done.stdout.strip())


def check_ollama(
    hosts: Sequence[str], models: Sequence[str], transport: httpx.BaseTransport | None = None
) -> tuple[list[Check], dict[str, list[str]]]:
    """The checks, and the candidate models each reachable host lacks."""
    out: list[Check] = []
    missing: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    digests: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {}
    with httpx.Client(transport=transport, timeout=30.0) as client:
        for host in hosts:
            try:
                response = client.get(f"{host}/api/tags")
                response.raise_for_status()
            except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
                out.append(Check(f"ollama {host}", "fail", f"unreachable: {exc}"))
                continue
            listed = {str(m["name"]): str(m["digest"]) for m in response.json().get("models", [])}
            out.append(Check(f"ollama {host}", "ok", f"{len(listed)} models"))
            for model in models:
                name = model if ":" in model else f"{model}:latest"
                if name not in listed:
                    missing.setdefault(host, []).append(model)
                    continue
                digests.setdefault(model, {})[host] = listed[name]
                show = client.post(f"{host}/api/show", json={"model": model})
                tools = show.is_success and "tools" in show.json().get("capabilities", [])
                out.append(Check(f"tools {model} @ {host}", "ok" if tools else "warn",
                                 "native tool calling" if tools
                                 else "no native tool calling: the screen will not admit it"))
    for model, by_host in sorted(digests.items()):
        agree = len(set(by_host.values())) == 1
        detail = (next(iter(by_host.values())) if agree
                  else f"hosts disagree: {by_host}; pull the same tag on every host")
        out.append(Check(f"digest {model}", "ok" if agree else "fail", detail))
    for host, absent in sorted(missing.items()):
        out.append(Check(f"models @ {host}", "warn", "missing: " + ", ".join(absent)))
    return out, missing


def check_disk(path: Path, min_free_gb: float = MIN_FREE_GB) -> Check:
    path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    free = shutil.disk_usage(path).free / 1024**3
    return Check("disk", "ok" if free >= min_free_gb else "warn", f"{free:.0f} GB free at {path}")


def read_vram(run: Run = subprocess.run) -> tuple[float | None, str]:
    """The largest local GPU's memory in GiB, or None, and what nvidia-smi said. A failing
    nvidia-smi (for example a driver/library version mismatch) is reported as such: Ollama
    may then be running on the CPU."""
    try:
        done = run(["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=memory.total", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
                   capture_output=True, text=True)
    except OSError:
        return None, "no local nvidia-smi"
    if done.returncode != 0:
        said = (done.stdout + done.stderr).strip().splitlines()
        return None, f"nvidia-smi failed: {said[0] if said else done.returncode}"
    try:
        sizes = [float(x) for x in done.stdout.split()]
    except ValueError:
        return None, f"nvidia-smi output not understood: {done.stdout.strip()[:80]}"
    if not sizes:
        return None, "nvidia-smi lists no GPU"
    return max(sizes) / 1024, f"{max(sizes) / 1024:.0f} GiB"


def recommend_profile(vram: float | None) -> str | None:
    if vram is None:
        return None
    return next((name for name, largest in PROFILE_VRAM if vram < largest), "gpu80")


def pull_commands(missing: Mapping[str, Sequence[str]], local: str) -> list[str]:
    """`ollama pull` for the first host; OLLAMA_HOST selects any other."""
    return [f"ollama pull {m}" if host == local else f"OLLAMA_HOST={host} ollama pull {m}"
            for host, models in sorted(missing.items()) for m in models]


def run_doctor(
    profile: str | None,
    *,
    database_url: str,
    hosts: Sequence[str],
    work_dir: Path,
    transport: httpx.BaseTransport | None = None,
    run: Run = subprocess.run,
) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    """The report, and whether every check passed (warnings allowed)."""
    vram, said = read_vram(run)
    recommended = recommend_profile(vram)
    chosen = load_profile(profile or recommended or "gpu16")
    models = [m for ms in chosen.candidates.values() for m in ms]
    checks = [*check_python(), check_database(database_url), check_vendor(run)]
    ollama, missing = check_ollama(hosts, models, transport)
    checks += [*ollama, check_disk(work_dir),
               Check("vram", "ok" if vram is not None else "warn", said)]
    lines = [f"# specdx doctor (candidates of profile {chosen.name})", "",
             "| check | status | detail |", "|---|---|---|"]
    lines += [f"| {c.name} | {c.status} | {c.detail.replace('|', '/')} |" for c in checks]
    lines += ["", "Recommended profile: " + (recommended or "unknown here; choose by the GPU of "
                                                         "the Ollama host (docs/RUNNING.md)")]
    pulls = pull_commands(missing, hosts[0])
    if pulls:
        lines += ["", "Pull the missing models:", "", *[f"    {p}" for p in pulls]]
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n", not any(c.status == "fail" for c in checks)
```

### Task 16: `docs/RUNNING.md` (English, with a Vietnamese quick-start)

Spec v2 §5.2. A step-by-step guide that takes another machine, a larger GPU or a remote Ollama to a finished gate. It covers:
- prerequisites and setup;
- choosing a profile, and remote or multi-GPU Ollama with `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL ≥ N`;
- the screen and the gate;
- pause and resume (`kill -STOP` / `kill -CONT`, never a second process on the same database);
- moving the cache, reading the results, and troubleshooting.

A test keeps the guide honest: every `specdx <command>` it shows must exist, and each topic the spec lists must be present.

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/RUNNING.md`
- Modify: `README.md` (a pointer to the guide)
- Test: `tests/test_docs.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the CLI subcommands of Tasks 13–15 and the environment variables `SPECDX_DATABASE_URL`, `SPECDX_OLLAMA_URL`, `SPECDX_WORK` and `TONOMYX_SRC`.
- Produces: `docs/RUNNING.md`, which Task 18 follows.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```python
import re
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from specdx.cli import main

RUNNING = Path(__file__).parents[1] / "docs" / "RUNNING.md"


def _subcommands(capsys) -> set[str]:
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        main(["--help"])
    match = re.search(r"\{([a-z,-]+)\}", capsys.readouterr().out)
    assert match is not None
    return set(match.group(1).split(","))


def test_every_command_the_guide_shows_exists(capsys) -> None:
    text = RUNNING.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    shown = set(re.findall(r"specdx ([a-z][a-z-]+)", text))
    assert shown and shown <= _subcommands(capsys)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("needle", [
    "OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL", "SPECDX_OLLAMA_URL", "SPECDX_WORK", "SPECDX_DATABASE_URL",
    "kill -STOP", "kill -CONT", "never start a second", "cache-info", "specdx doctor",
    "specdx screen", "setsid nohup", "## Bắt đầu nhanh (tiếng Việt)", "Troubleshooting",
])
def test_the_guide_covers_what_the_spec_asks(needle) -> None:
    assert needle.lower() in RUNNING.read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the guide**

`docs/RUNNING.md`:
````markdown
# Running SpecDx P1b

This guide takes a fresh machine to a finished P1b gate: setup, choosing a hardware profile, Ollama on this machine or on remote GPU hosts, the capability screen, the gate, pausing and resuming, moving the run cache, reading the results, and troubleshooting. A Vietnamese quick-start is at the end.

The design is in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-specdx-v2-design.md` (a delta on `2026-10-06-specdx-design.md`). Every threshold is pre-registered in `experiments/p1b-preregistration.md`.

## 1. Prerequisites

| What | Version | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Python | 3.13 | SpecDx and the vendored TonomyX |
| Docker | any recent | PostgreSQL 16 for SpecDx, on port 55434 |
| Ollama | 0.30 or later, on this machine or on GPU hosts | the interpreters; models need native tool calling |
| poppler (`pdftotext`) | any | extracts the Tell corpus from the requirement PDFs, once |
| git, jq | any | setup, and freezing the screened population |

SpecDx never uses ports 55432 or 55433: they belong to other projects on the author's machine, and SpecDx refuses them.

## 2. Setup

```bash
git clone <this repository> specdx && cd specdx
python3.13 -m venv .venv
.venv/bin/python -m pip install -e vendor/tonomyx -e ".[dev]"
docker compose up -d                      # PostgreSQL 16 on localhost:55434
TONOMYX_SRC=/path/to/TonomyX bash scripts/verify_vendor.sh   # optional: needs a TonomyX checkout
.venv/bin/python -m pytest                # every test; no model is called
```

Configuration comes from the environment:

| Variable | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `SPECDX_DATABASE_URL` | `postgresql://tonomyx:tonomyx@localhost:55434/tonomyx` | the SpecDx database; workers add databases `tonomyx_w0`, `tonomyx_w1`, … in the same server |
| `SPECDX_OLLAMA_URL` | `http://localhost:11434` | one Ollama host, or several separated by commas |
| `SPECDX_WORK` | `work/` in the checkout | the run cache (`runs.jsonl`), the Tell corpus cache (`tell/`) and the mutant packs (`mutants/`) |
| `TONOMYX_SRC` | `/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX` | a TonomyX checkout, only for the vendor check |

## 3. Check the machine and choose a profile

```bash
.venv/bin/specdx doctor                   # or: specdx doctor --profile gpu24
```

`doctor` checks Python and the packages, PostgreSQL, the vendor, every Ollama host (reachable, each candidate model present, native tool calling, the same digest on every host), free disk and, when `nvidia-smi` is local, VRAM. It prints a recommended profile and the `ollama pull` commands for missing models. A `fail` line must be fixed first; a `warn` line is information. After pulling, load one model and check that `ollama ps` reports `100% GPU`.

| Profile | GPU memory | Candidates (family: models) | Suggested `--workers` |
|---|---|---|---:|
| gpu16 | up to about 22 GB | qwen: qwen3:14b, qwen2.5:14b, qwen3:30b-a3b · openai: gpt-oss:20b · mistral: mistral-small3.2:24b · ibm: granite3.3:8b · meta: llama3.1:8b | 2 |
| gpu24 | up to about 44 GB | gpu16, plus qwen3:32b and google: gemma3:27b | 3 |
| gpu48 | up to about 76 GB | gpu24, plus llama3.3:70b and qwen2.5:72b (q4) | 4 |
| gpu80 | more | gpu48, plus gpt-oss:120b | 6 |

A profile only widens the list of candidates. The capability screen decides who enters the population, and no rule or threshold depends on the profile. The profiles are in `experiments/profiles/`.

## 4. Ollama on this machine, on a remote host, or on several GPUs

On every Ollama host:

```bash
OLLAMA_HOST=0.0.0.0:11434 OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL=2 ollama serve
```

- `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL` must be at least the number of workers sent to that host; otherwise the workers queue behind each other.
- Several hosts: list them all, as in `export SPECDX_OLLAMA_URL=http://gpu1:11434,http://gpu2:11434`. Worker *i* uses host *i* modulo the number of hosts.
- Every host must hold the same build of each model. Pull the same tags everywhere; `doctor` fails when two hosts report different digests, and so does every run.
- SpecDx and PostgreSQL can run on a CPU machine; only Ollama needs the GPU.

## 5. The capability screen

```bash
.venv/bin/specdx screen --profile gpu16 --out results/p1b-screen --workers 2
```

Each candidate runs the base specification on scenario `happy`, 3 Tell variants × 3 seeds, through the neutral harness. A model is admitted with act rate ≥ 0.6 and valid-call rate ≥ 0.8. The population keeps at most 2 models per family and 4 in all, and needs at least 2 families. With fewer, the command exits with status 3 and prints STOP: P1b stops and reports, and the thresholds are never lowered.

Freeze the population into the gate configuration:

```bash
jq --slurpfile s results/p1b-screen.json '.models_by_family = $s[0].population' \
  experiments/p1b-gate.json > work/p1b-gate.json && mv work/p1b-gate.json experiments/p1b-gate.json
```

The gate refuses to run on any other population than the one `results/p1b-screen.json` froze.

## 6. The gate

A short smoke run first:

```bash
jq '.budget = 60' experiments/p1b-gate.json > work/smoke.json
.venv/bin/specdx run --variant base --config work/smoke.json --out work/smoke-base.json --workers 2
```

Then the gate, detached from the terminal so that closing it does not stop the run:

```bash
setsid nohup .venv/bin/specdx gate --config experiments/p1b-gate.json \
  --out results/p1b-gate.json --workers 2 > work/p1b-gate.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &
echo $! > work/p1b-gate.pid
tail -f work/p1b-gate.log                 # one line per executed run
```

It runs the base specification, computes which mutants are scorable from the base runs alone, then runs the 20 development mutants. It writes `results/p1b-gate.json`, `results/p1b-gate.md` and `results/p1b-gate-mutants.jsonl`.

## 7. Pause, resume, and restart

- Pause: `kill -STOP $(cat work/p1b-gate.pid)`. Resume: `kill -CONT $(cat work/p1b-gate.pid)`.
- Never start a second SpecDx process on the same database. The advisory lock refuses it with "another process holds the SpecDx lock"; stop the first one, or wait for it.
- After a crash, a reboot or an Ollama outage, run the same command again. Every finished run is in the cache, so the gate resumes where it stopped and spends its budgets exactly as before.

## 8. Moving the run cache

The cache is portable. A run key is a digest of the specification, the scenario, the interpreter, the Tell texts, the executor settings and the Ollama model digest. Copy `work/` (or the directory `SPECDX_WORK` names) to the other machine; every run whose model digest matches is reused there.

```bash
rsync -a work/ other-host:specdx/work/
.venv/bin/specdx cache-info               # runs per model and digest, and per specification
```

## 9. Reading the results

| File | What it holds |
|---|---|
| `results/p1b-replay.md` | the offline replay on the P1 cache: κ, θ, the GO criterion, observability per item |
| `results/p1b-screen.md`, `.json` | each candidate's act rate and valid-call rate, and the population |
| `results/p1b-gate.md` | the verdict: the primary table (scorable mutants) decides; the secondary table scores all 20 and counts the excluded ones; the literal typed-against-typed table is for transparency |
| `results/p1b-gate-mutants.jsonl` | one line for the base and one per mutant: findings, matches, run counts, scorability, the Unobservable list, and the base-to-mutant shift of each item's difficulty δ |

The headline is PASSED, NOT PASSED or INCOMPLETE (a mutant could not run). After a second NOT PASSED, P1b stops; a third iteration on the development split needs the author's decision. The test split is never run during P1b.

## 10. Troubleshooting

| Symptom | Cause and fix |
|---|---|
| `another process holds the SpecDx lock` | A SpecDx process (or pytest) uses the database. Wait for it, or stop it. |
| `refusing …: ports 55432, 55433 …` | `SPECDX_DATABASE_URL` points at another project's database. Use port 55434. |
| `InfrastructureError: Ollama failed 5 times` | Ollama is down or out of memory. Nothing was cached; restart Ollama and rerun the same command. |
| `all calls timed out … Ollama may be wedged` | Restart Ollama; lower `--workers` or `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL` if the GPU is saturated. |
| `Ollama does not list model …` | Pull the model on every host (see `specdx doctor`). |
| `model … has different digests across hosts` | Pull the same tag again on every host. |
| `… differs from the pack this code builds` | A mutant pack in `work/mutants/` was edited or built by other code. Move it aside; it is rebuilt. |
| `pdftotext` not found | Install poppler, or copy a `work/tell/` from a machine that built it. |
| `nvidia-smi failed: … Driver/library version mismatch` | The NVIDIA driver was updated without a reboot, and Ollama cannot use the GPU. Reboot the GPU host (or reload the `nvidia` kernel modules) before any run. |
| Runs take many minutes each | Check that `ollama ps` shows `100% GPU` for the loaded model; a model larger than the GPU runs partly on the CPU. |

## Bắt đầu nhanh (tiếng Việt)

1. Cài Python 3.13, Docker, Ollama (0.30 trở lên) và poppler.
2. Cài đặt:
   ```bash
   python3.13 -m venv .venv && .venv/bin/python -m pip install -e vendor/tonomyx -e ".[dev]"
   docker compose up -d
   .venv/bin/python -m pytest
   ```
3. Kiểm tra máy và chọn cấu hình: `.venv/bin/specdx doctor`. Lệnh này gợi ý một profile (gpu16, gpu24, gpu48, gpu80) và in các lệnh `ollama pull` còn thiếu.
4. Nếu Ollama chạy ở máy GPU khác: trên máy đó chạy `OLLAMA_HOST=0.0.0.0:11434 OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL=2 ollama serve`; trên máy này đặt `export SPECDX_OLLAMA_URL=http://may-gpu:11434` (nhiều máy thì cách nhau bằng dấu phẩy).
5. Sàng lọc năng lực: `.venv/bin/specdx screen --profile gpu16 --out results/p1b-screen --workers 2`. Mã thoát 3 nghĩa là STOP: ít hơn 2 họ mô hình đạt, P1b dừng.
6. Ghi quần thể vào cấu hình gate (lệnh `jq` ở mục 5), rồi chạy gate nền: `setsid nohup .venv/bin/specdx gate --config experiments/p1b-gate.json --out results/p1b-gate.json --workers 2 > work/p1b-gate.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &`.
7. Tạm dừng: `kill -STOP <pid>`; chạy tiếp: `kill -CONT <pid>`. Không bao giờ chạy hai tiến trình trên cùng một cơ sở dữ liệu. Nếu bị ngắt, chạy lại đúng lệnh cũ: các lần chạy đã xong được lấy từ bộ nhớ đệm.
8. Kết quả nằm ở `results/p1b-gate.md`.
````

### Task 17: The pre-registration and the gate v2 configuration

Spec v2 §1: every threshold and rule is committed in `experiments/p1b-preregistration.md` before the capability screen runs, and nothing in it changes after any P1b result is seen. Two artefacts and one test pin it down:
- the pre-registration document, which also records the planning resolutions and the replay criterion;
- `experiments/p1b-gate.json`: the same development split and budgets as P1, Tell variants 0–2 × seeds 1–3, and the screen file it must match. Its population stays empty until Task 18 freezes the screen's population into it;
- a test pinning every pre-registered value to the constant the code uses.

**Files:**
- Create: `experiments/p1b-preregistration.md`, `experiments/p1b-gate.json`
- Test: `tests/test_preregistration.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `harness.MAX_NUDGES`;
  - the screen constants;
  - `act.RASCH_L2`, `act.OMEGA` and `act.M_MIN`;
  - `latent.ALPHA` and `latent.BETA`;
  - `sensitivity.COMPETENT`, `PERMUTATIONS`, `MIN_BEHAVIOURS` and `BH_Q`;
  - `scores.TAU_H`;
  - `tguided.MAX_TBOUNDARY` and `WINDOW`;
  - `observability.SCORABLE_K`;
  - `matching.MIN_SCORABLE` and `ABSOLUTE_BAR`;
  - `GateConfig.load` and `check_population`.
- Produces: `experiments/p1b-gate.json` (read by `specdx gate --config` in Task 18) and `experiments/p1b-preregistration.md`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`tests/test_preregistration.py`:
```python
"""Every pre-registered value (spec v2 §3.10, experiments/p1b-preregistration.md) is the
value the code uses. Changing one after a P1b result is seen breaks this test."""

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from specdx.eval import matching, observability, screen
from specdx.eval.pilot import GateConfig
from specdx.infer import act, latent, scores, sensitivity
from specdx.interpreters import harness
from specdx.scenarios import tguided

ROOT = Path(__file__).parents[1]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("value", "registered"), [
    (harness.MAX_NUDGES, 2),
    (screen.ACT_RATE_MIN, 0.6), (screen.VALID_RATE_MIN, 0.8),
    (len(screen.SCREEN_VARIANTS) * len(screen.SCREEN_SEEDS), 9),
    (screen.PER_FAMILY, 2), (screen.MAX_MODELS, 4), (screen.MIN_FAMILIES, 2),
    (act.RASCH_L2, 1.0), (act.OMEGA, 0.3), (act.M_MIN, 3),
    ((latent.ALPHA, latent.BETA), (7.0, 3.0)),
    (sensitivity.COMPETENT, 0.5), (scores.TAU_H, 0.5),
    (sensitivity.PERMUTATIONS, 2000), (sensitivity.MIN_BEHAVIOURS, 6), (sensitivity.BH_Q, 0.1),
    (tguided.MAX_TBOUNDARY, 12), (tguided.WINDOW, 40),
    (observability.SCORABLE_K, 3), (matching.MIN_SCORABLE, 3),
    (matching.ABSOLUTE_BAR, 0.4),
])
def test_the_code_uses_the_pre_registered_values(value, registered) -> None:
    assert value == registered


def test_the_gate_configuration_is_the_p1_split_and_budgets_on_the_screened_population(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.chdir(ROOT)  # the screen path is relative to the repository root
    config = GateConfig.load(ROOT / "experiments" / "p1b-gate.json")
    p1 = GateConfig.load(ROOT / "experiments" / "p1-gate.json")
    assert (config.dev_per_type, config.rng_seed) == (p1.dev_per_type, p1.rng_seed)
    assert (config.budget, config.probe_budget, config.localize_budget) == (200, 100, 80)
    assert (config.variants, config.seeds) == ((0, 1, 2), (1, 2, 3))
    assert config.screen == Path("results/p1b-screen.json")
    if config.models_by_family:
        config.check_population()  # frozen from the screen: it must equal its population
    else:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="empty"):  # before the screen has run
            config.check_population()


def test_the_pre_registration_states_each_value() -> None:
    text = (ROOT / "experiments" / "p1b-preregistration.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for needle in ("Beta(7, 3)", "τ_H = 0.5", "ω = 0.3", "m_min = 3", "λ = 1.0", "2000",
                   "q = 0.1", "k = 3", "≥ 0.6", "≥ 0.8", "at most 12", "GO criterion",
                   "mechanism check", "θ ≥ 0.5", "θ ordering"):
        assert needle in text, needle
```

`experiments/p1b-preregistration.md`:
````markdown
# P1b pre-registration

- **Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-specdx-v2-design.md` (v2), a delta on `2026-10-06-specdx-design.md` (v1).
- **Plan:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-specdx-p1b.md`.
- **Rule:** this file is committed before the capability screen runs. Nothing in it changes after any P1b result is seen. `tests/test_preregistration.py` pins every value below to the code.

## 1. Offline replay (before any new run)

The v2 inference runs on the P1 run cache: the base specification and the 20 development mutants, with the old population (qwen3:14b, qwen2.5:7b-instruct-q4_K_M, llama3.1:8b) and their 200 population runs each. No run is executed and no refutation is attempted.

- **GO criterion: a mechanism check** (author's ruling, 2026-10-12). It holds when all three are true:
  1. On the base and on each of the 20 development mutants, stage 1 gives llama3.1:8b, which never writes, a κ strictly below every other model's.
  2. On the same 21 specifications, every model with at least one acted behaviour has θ ≥ 0.5, and at least one model acted.
  3. At least 50% of the P1 base findings become Unobservable.
- **Disclosure: the criterion was revised after a planning pre-check.**
  - The first draft required θ(qwen3:14b) ≥ θ(llama3.1:8b).
  - The pre-check measured θ(llama3.1:8b) 0.961 against θ(qwen3:14b) 0.743, κ ranking qwen3 > qwen2.5 > llama, and 61 of 91 P1 base findings becoming Unobservable.
  - On the P1 population only one family (qwen) writes. Proposition 2′ needs at least two acting families, so a θ ordering there carries no information about v2.
  - The author therefore moved the θ ordering to the screened population (below) and kept the mechanism check for the P1 cache.
- **θ ordering on the screened population (Task 18).** After the smoke run, the population model with the highest κ must have a θ strictly above the lowest θ of the other models. If it does not, the gate is not launched, and the result goes to the author.
- **Reported in addition:** the number of P1 base findings that become Unobservable, Oₛ per item, and the S2 p-values.
- **NO-GO** stops P1b before any infrastructure work, and the result goes to the author.

## 2. Interpreter population

| Rule | Value |
|---|---|
| Harness nudges per step | 2 (only for a step whose token drafts tables and that made no successful write call) |
| Screen runs per candidate | 9: base specification, scenario `happy`, Tell variants 0–2 × seeds 1–3, through the harness |
| Admission | act rate ≥ 0.6 and valid-call rate ≥ 0.8 |
| Ranking | act rate, then valid-call rate, then model name |
| Population | ≤ 2 per family, ≤ 4 in all, ≥ 2 families |
| Stop rule | fewer than 2 families admitted: P1b stops; thresholds are never lowered |

- **Act rate:** the fraction of (run, interpreter step) pairs with aᵦₛ = 1.
- **Valid-call rate:** the fraction of tool calls (native, or parsed from text) whose name is one of the step's tools and whose arguments validate against its schema.
- **Text calls:** a JSON object anywhere in the reply, fenced or in `<tool_call>` tags, naming the tool with `name` or `function` and giving the arguments with `arguments` or `parameters`.

## 3. Inference

| Parameter | Value |
|---|---|
| Act indicator aᵦₛ | the step completed (`done` or `needs_gate`) and, when its actor drafts tables (`de_scope.draft_tables`), its slice ends with a row the starting state did not hold |
| Stage 1 (Rasch) | P(a = 1) = σ(κₘ − δₛ), L2 penalty λ = 1.0 on κ and δ, mean δ fixed at 0, Newton to a change below 1e-6, at most 100 iterations |
| Observable item | Oₛ ≥ ω = 0.3 and at least m_min = 3 acted behaviours |
| Classes | the ≡_S projection of the step's slice (locked values and blank presence), without the step status |
| Stage 2 data | acted behaviours only |
| θ prior | Beta(7, 3); M-step θₘ = (Sₘ + 6) / (nₘ + 8); a model with no acted behaviour keeps 0.75 |
| Error distribution ε | re-estimated in each EM iteration from the outputs not explained as faithful (add-one smoothing) |
| K_max, P(\|A\| = 1) | 6, 0.9 |
| V(b) | violated clauses relevant to an item where b acted |
| Typing | only candidates whose every clause is read by an observable item; others are Unobservable, never scored |
| Hidden | refuted or unattempted singleton with Hidden(c) ≥ τ_H = 0.5 |
| Weak | v1 rule on an observable item; the reference is the most probable class of the (clean) MAP set |
| S1 | P(\|Aₛ\| ≥ 2), reported at ≥ 0.5 |
| S2 | competent models θₘ ≥ 0.5; plug-in I(K; V \| M); 2000 permutations of V within each model, seeded; p = (1 + #{I_perm ≥ I_obs}) / (R + 1); tested with ≥ 6 behaviours, ≥ 2 classes, ≥ 2 variants; Benjamini–Hochberg at q = 0.1 per specification |
| Ambiguity score | max(S1, 1 − pₛ) |
| ddmin oracle | one re-run per (competent model, Tell variant) at seed 1; passes while two acted re-runs differ on the ambiguity's fields |
| T-boundary scenarios | number within 40 characters of a comparison keyword in the same sentence; % or phần trăm → discount_pct; near số lượng, đơn vị, units or quantity → received; v − 1, v, v + 1 on the anchor; at most 12 per specification; queued after the canon scenarios |
| Budgets | population 200, probe 100, localise 80 per specification |
| Sampling | temperature 0.7, Tell variants 0–2, seeds 1–3 |

## 4. Evaluation

- **Mutants:** the same 20 development mutants as P1 (`dev_per_type` 5, `rng_seed` 20261006). The test split is not run.
- **Truth targets (`observed_at`):**

  | Operator | Targets |
  |---|---|
  | C1 | the row the conflicting invariants select |
  | C2 | the golden clause's row |
  | H1 | the golden row |
  | H2 | any event with the demanded event's reference (no admissible run writes the demanded event itself) |
  | A1, A2, A3 | the steps the edited rule governs: the discount rules → draft_quote and P2; the GRN-exception rule → record_goods_receipt, reconcile and draft_payment; the gate count → draft_quote, P2, draft_purchase_order, P6 and draft_payment; the volume discount → draft_quote |
  | W1 | the golden case rows of the blanked field's table |
  | W2 | the event kind and reference |

- **Scorable mutant:** at least k = 3 base behaviours touch a target. A behaviour touches a row or event when it acted at an item whose slice covers it and its end state holds it, and a step when it acted at that step. Computed once from the base runs, before any mutant runs, and shared by SpecDx, B2 and B3.
- **Tables:** the primary table scores the scorable mutants and decides the verdict. The secondary table scores all 20 and reports the number excluded per type. The literal typed-against-typed table is kept.
- **Rule:** contradiction and hidden win when SpecDx's typed F1 > max(B2, B3) untyped F1; ambiguity and weak win when SpecDx's typed F1 ≥ 0.4; F1 in exact arithmetic. A type with fewer than 3 scorable mutants is a loss. The gate passes on at least 3 wins of 4.
- **Outcome:** PASSED, NOT PASSED or INCOMPLETE. After a second NOT PASSED, P1b stops; a third iteration needs the author's decision.
- **GO** for the gate is given only by the replay of §1 and by the screen's population of §2.
````

### Task 18: Run the capability screen and gate v2 on real models

This task runs real models. There is no new code. Each step records its output in `results/` or `work/` (the worktree's own `work/`, git-ignored, distinct from the P1 cache).
- **Preconditions.** Task 7 said GO. Tasks 8–17 are complete and reviewed: the gate must run on reviewed code. `.venv/bin/python -m pytest` passes (412).
- **Do not run the test suite while the screen, the smoke run or the gate is in progress:** they hold the database locks the tests need.
- **The run cache.** Every command that runs interpreters is prefixed with `SPECDX_WORK=/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work`. New runs are appended to the main run cache; nothing there is rewritten or deleted (Global Constraints).

**Files:**
- Create: `results/p1b-screen.md`, `results/p1b-screen.json`, `results/p1b-smoke-base.json`, `results/p1b-gate.json`, `results/p1b-gate.md`, `results/p1b-gate-mutants.jsonl`
- Modify: `experiments/p1b-gate.json` (the population, frozen from the screen)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `specdx doctor --profile`, `specdx screen --profile --out --workers`;
  - `specdx run --variant base --config --out --workers`;
  - `specdx gate --config --out --workers`;
  - `experiments/profiles/gpu16.json`, `experiments/p1b-gate.json`, `docs/RUNNING.md`.
- Produces: the P1b verdict.

- [ ] **Step 1: Pull the gpu16 candidates and confirm the GPU serves them**

```bash
mkdir -p work
df -h /home   # about 60 GB must be free for the models not yet present
for m in $(jq -r '.candidates[][]' experiments/profiles/gpu16.json); do ollama pull "$m" || break; done
ollama list | grep -E "qwen3:14b|qwen2.5:14b|qwen3:30b-a3b|gpt-oss:20b|mistral-small3.2:24b|granite3.3:8b|llama3.1:8b"
nvidia-smi --query-gpu=name,memory.total --format=csv,noheader
curl -s localhost:11434/api/generate -d '{"model":"qwen3:14b","prompt":"ok","stream":false,"think":false,"options":{"num_predict":1}}' > /dev/null
ollama ps
```
Expected:
- each pull ends with `success`, and `ollama list` shows all seven models;
- `nvidia-smi` prints one line naming the GPU and its memory (about 16 GB);
- `ollama ps` shows `qwen3:14b` with `PROCESSOR` = `100% GPU`.

Stop and report if `nvidia-smi` prints `Failed to initialize NVML: Driver/library version mismatch` (as it did on 2026-10-11, with kernel module 595.84 and user library 595.99), or if `ollama ps` shows the model on the CPU. The NVIDIA driver was updated without a reboot, and the runs would take many times longer on the CPU. Rebooting is the human's decision, not this task's.

Also record the Ollama service's parallelism:
```bash
systemctl show ollama --property=Environment 2>/dev/null
```
With `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL` below 2, the second worker's requests queue: the run is slower, but the results are the same. Note the value for the report, and do not change system services.

- [ ] **Step 2: Check the machine**

```bash
.venv/bin/specdx doctor --profile gpu16; echo "exit $?"
```
Expected:
- exit 0, with no `fail` line;
- every gpu16 model present and its digest `ok`;
- the `vram` line `ok`.

A `tools … warn` line marks a model without native tool calling. The screen will not admit it; that is not a reason to stop.

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the capability screen**

```bash
SPECDX_WORK=/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work .venv/bin/specdx screen --profile gpu16 \
  --out results/p1b-screen --workers 2 2> work/p1b-screen.log; echo "exit $?"
cat results/p1b-screen.md
```
Expected:
- 63 runs (7 models × 9), one log line each in `work/p1b-screen.log`; roughly an hour;
- `results/p1b-screen.md` shows each model's act rate, valid-call rate and admission;
- exit 0, with the "Population" section listing at least 2 families.

**Stop rule:** exit status 3 (STOP: fewer than 2 families admitted) ends P1b. Commit the screen results with `git add results/p1b-screen.md results/p1b-screen.json && git commit -m "results: P1b capability screen — STOP"`. Report the table, and never lower the thresholds.

If the command raises `InfrastructureError` (Ollama outage) or a lock error, fix the cause and run the same command again: finished runs are cached.

- [ ] **Step 4: Freeze the population into the gate configuration**

```bash
jq --slurpfile s results/p1b-screen.json '.models_by_family = $s[0].population' \
  experiments/p1b-gate.json > work/p1b-gate.json && mv work/p1b-gate.json experiments/p1b-gate.json
.venv/bin/python -c "from pathlib import Path; from specdx.eval.pilot import GateConfig; \
c = GateConfig.load(Path('experiments/p1b-gate.json')); c.check_population(); print(c.models_by_family)"
.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_preregistration.py
git add results/p1b-screen.md results/p1b-screen.json experiments/p1b-gate.json
git commit -m "results: P1b capability screen; population frozen into the gate configuration"
```
Expected:
- the printed population equals the screen's;
- `23 passed`;
- one commit.

The population is never edited by hand.

- [ ] **Step 5: Smoke-run the base specification (budget 60, two workers)**

```bash
jq '.budget = 60' experiments/p1b-gate.json > work/p1b-smoke.json
SPECDX_WORK=/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work .venv/bin/specdx run --variant base \
  --config work/p1b-smoke.json --out results/p1b-smoke-base.json --workers 2 2> work/p1b-smoke.log
jq '{runs, probe_runs, ddmin_runs, executed, theta, kappa,
     observable: ([.items[] | select(.observable)] | length),
     unobservable: (.unobservable | length), defects: (.defects | length)}' results/p1b-smoke-base.json
grep -c " ok " work/p1b-smoke.log; grep -c " error " work/p1b-smoke.log
```
Expected:
- `runs` ≤ 60; `theta` and `kappa` list every population model;
- the log shows one line per executed run, mostly `ok`;
- `observable` is above 0.

These runs are cached and reused by the gate's base run.
- If every run is `error`, read the first `error` in the log and fix the cause (usually Ollama) before going on.
- If `observable` is 0, the population acts nowhere and no mutant can be scorable. Report this to the controller before launching the gate.

**θ ordering on the screened population** (pre-registered, author's ruling 2026-10-12):
```bash
jq -r '.kappa as $k | .theta as $t
  | ($k | to_entries | max_by(.value) | .key) as $top
  | ([$t | to_entries[] | select(.key != $top) | .value] | min) as $low
  | "highest κ: \($top), θ \($t[$top]); lowest other θ \($low); ordering "
    + (if $t[$top] > $low then "OK" else "FAILS" end)' results/p1b-smoke-base.json
```
Expected: `ordering OK`. On `ordering FAILS`, do not launch the gate. Report the smoke report's κ, θ and act rates to the controller.

- [ ] **Step 6: Launch gate v2, detached and resumable**

```bash
SPECDX_WORK=/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work setsid nohup .venv/bin/specdx gate \
  --config experiments/p1b-gate.json --out results/p1b-gate.json --workers 2 \
  > work/p1b-gate.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &
pgrep -f "specdx gate --config experiments/p1b-gate.json" > work/p1b-gate.pid
cat work/p1b-gate.pid
```
Expected: one PID. A minute later, `tail -5 work/p1b-gate.log` shows `run base …` lines and `ps -p $(cat work/p1b-gate.pid)` shows the process.
- **What it does.** The gate runs the base specification, computes scorability from the base runs, then runs the 20 development mutants. Each costs at most 380 runs. With two workers on one 16 GB GPU, expect one to three days.
- **Pause and resume:** `kill -STOP $(cat work/p1b-gate.pid)` and `kill -CONT $(cat work/p1b-gate.pid)`.
- **After a crash, a reboot or an Ollama outage:** run the same command again; cached runs are reused. Never start a second gate while one is running.

- [ ] **Step 7: Record the verdict**

When `ps -p $(cat work/p1b-gate.pid)` shows that the process has ended:
```bash
tail -20 work/p1b-gate.log
cat results/p1b-gate.md
git add results/p1b-gate.json results/p1b-gate.md results/p1b-gate-mutants.jsonl \
  results/p1b-smoke-base.json
git commit -m "results: P1b gate v2 — $(head -1 results/p1b-gate.md | sed 's/^# P1b gate v2: //')"
```
- If the log ends in a traceback (an infrastructure error), fix the cause and relaunch with Step 6's command; it resumes.
- **PASSED:** P1b is complete. Report the primary and secondary tables, and stop.
- **NOT PASSED:**
  - Do not tune on the test split, and do not change any pre-registered value.
  - Report to your human partner the primary and secondary tables, the scorable mutants per type, the excluded counts, the Unobservable lists, and the δ shifts from `results/p1b-gate-mutants.jsonl`.
  - This is the second NOT PASSED (P1's gate was the first). Under spec v2 §4.2, P1b stops and reports, and a third iteration on the development split needs the author's decision.
- **INCOMPLETE:** a mutant could not run. Report its failure line from `results/p1b-gate-mutants.jsonl`. Do not rerun selected mutants by hand.
